Method: 提出非对称信任协议：自动收紧（向低特权种姓）自动放行，有界放松（向高特权种姓）需要操作员对每个轴预算进行副签名。每个转换携带签名原因链，提交到哈希链式Merkle审计日志，离线审计员仅凭操作员签名的身份清单即可验证。

Result: 在100个机器人的集群上使用真实Ed25519签名：自动收紧在个位数到低两位数毫秒内完成；协议构造性地拒绝了四种攻击（种姓洗钱、重复放松升级、操作员冒充、原因链伪造）；部分治理基线隔离了哪个门阻止哪种攻击；随机模糊攻击未发现任何漏洞。分布式审计层在仿真和真实多进程部署中验证了拜占庭容错和分叉排除。

Conclusion: 该构造将单智能体角色突变治理门泛化到了集群级别的种姓治理，为受监管的异构机器人集群提供了一种可审计、可外部授权的权限变更安全框架。

Abstract: In heterogeneous robot swarms, caste reassignment (rebinding a robot to a new capability-bound role) is a high-frequency runtime event driven by battery, payload, and priority changes. Existing approaches treat it as an internal allocation algorithm and do not expose the reassignment to external authority. We argue that for regulated embodied deployments a caste change that elevates a robot's privilege envelope is a governance event that must be auditable and externally authorised. We propose an asymmetric-trust protocol: auto-tightening reassignments (to safer, lower-privilege castes) are admitted automatically, while bounded relaxation (to higher-privilege castes) requires an operator countersignature against a per-axis budget. Each transition carries a signed cause-chain, committed to a hash-chained Merkle audit log that an offline auditor verifies from an operator-signed identity manifest alone. We evaluate a reference implementation with real Ed25519 signatures over fleets up to 100 robots: auto-tightening completes in single-digit to low-double-digit milliseconds, and the governed protocol refuses four explicit attacks (caste laundering, repeated-relaxation escalation, operator impersonation, cause-chain forgery) by construction, with a partially-governed baseline isolating which gate stops which attack and a randomized fuzz adversary finding no admission. A distributed audit layer replicates the log across N per-member replicas with quorum-committed total order and cryptographic fork exclusion; we prove agreement and fork exclusion and validate them both in simulation and as a real multi-process deployment over TCP sockets (up to 100 real processes) with a Byzantine equivocator, on which every honest replica agrees, detects the equivocation, and commits no fork. The construction generalises a single-agent persona-mutation governance gate to swarm-level caste governance.

</details>


### [72] [KAM-WM: Kinematic Affordance Maps from Latent World Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04652)
*Xinyu Shao,Keru Zhou,Guowei Huang,Yajun Gao,Tongtong Cao,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KAM-WM 从冻结的潜视频世界模型中提取运动学可供性图（KAM），为机器人操作提供交互区域和粗略运动结构先验，无需 rollout 或微调。该方法在 LIBERO 和 RoboTwin2.0 上取得显著提升，验证了方向性信息优于纯空间定位先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本操作学习需要同时捕获交互位置和交互方式（如何开始）。静态先验（如分割掩码）仅编码前者，缺乏方向性运动信息。本文旨在从冻结的视频世界模型中提取粗粒度的方向性交互线索，以弥补这一缺失。

Method: (1) 使用 Flow Matching 图像到视频骨干网络对单个查询帧进行单步推理，获取潜空间速度场作为 KAM；(2) KAM 提供任务条件化的交互区域和粗略运动结构；(3) 轻量级 Perceiver 将 KAM 压缩为 token，与 RGB 观测和本体感知一起条件化扩散策略。

Result: LIBERO 平均成功率 90.6%；RoboTwin2.0 Easy 设置 65.7%，Hard 设置 22.4%。消融实验表明方向性信息带来的增益显著高于纯空间定位先验（零阶掩码）。

Conclusion: 冻结的视频模型可以为控制提供有用的一阶视觉先验（方向信息），而无需测试时的 rollout 计算成本，在评估设置中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Learning manipulation from few demonstrations requires visual priors that capture not only where to interact, but also how the interaction should begin; static priors such as segmentation masks encode only the former. We present KAM-WM, a framework that extracts a coarse directional interaction cue from a frozen latent video world model without rollout or world-model fine-tuning. KAM-WM queries a Flow Matching image-to-video backbone once and interprets its single-step latent velocity as a Kinematic Affordance Map (KAM), which provides task-conditioned interaction regions and coarse motion structure. A lightweight Perceiver compresses KAM into tokens that condition a diffusion policy together with RGB observations and proprioception. Across LIBERO and RoboTwin2.0, KAM-WM reaches 90.6% average success on LIBERO and achieves 65.7% and 22.4% success rates in the Easy and Hard settings on RoboTwin2.0, respectively. Controlled comparisons against a zero-order mask prior suggest that part of the gains comes from directional information beyond spatial localization alone. These results indicate that, in the evaluated settings, a frozen video model can provide a useful first-order visual prior for control without the test-time cost of future rollout.

</details>


### [73] [Do Vision-Language-Action Models Mean What They Say? On the Role of Faithfulness in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04681)
*Matthew Foutter,Matteo Cercola,Lena Wild,Yunshan Wang,Michelle Li,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Pinocchio方法，通过强化学习后训练提升具身链式思维推理的忠实性（faithfulness），在自动驾驶任务中将忠实性提升4%-18%，并在分布外场景下将策略响应能力提升1.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身链式思维推理虽然提升了VLA模型的可解释性和决策能力，但其是否真实反映策略内部决策过程尚不清楚。作者区分了功能性推理（提升任务性能）与忠实性推理（反映真实决策过程），指出现有对齐策略仅提供必要但不充分的忠实性，可能导致推理步骤在中间环节掩盖因果联系，从而限制策略泛化。

Method: 通过人类评估揭示SoTA推理模型在自动驾驶中推理质量与轨迹改进之间的不一致耦合；设计学习型评论家Pinocchio作为行为代理，从观测基础（observation grounding）和步骤一致性（stepwise coherence）两个维度对忠实性进行评分；将Pinocchio的评分作为密集奖励信号，在强化学习后训练中对具身策略进行微调。

Result: 在保留的驾驶基准上，后训练规划器相比SoTA对齐基线和轨迹误差后训练基线，忠实性分别提升4%和18%，同时保持竞争性的下游任务性能。在合成分布外测试集上，忠实性后训练将策略对罕见反事实场景的响应能力提升至SoTA策略的1.6倍。

Conclusion: 忠实性推理轨迹有助于构建更鲁棒、更泛化、更可解释的具身智能体。

Abstract: Embodied Chain-of-Thought has emerged as a promising mechanism to enhance robot decision-making and interpretability in black-box Vision-Language Action (VLA) models. However, whether this verbalized Chain-of-Thought truthfully reflects the policy's underlying decision process remains poorly understood. We distinguish between functional reasoning, in which reasoning improves task performance, and faithful reasoning, in which reasoning truly reflects the policy's internal decision process. We argue that SoTA alignment strategies offer a necessary but insufficient notion of faithfulness, admitting reasoning whose intermediate steps can mask the causal links in action prediction through confounding factors (e.g., reasoning that is ungrounded in the environment and internally disconnected or inconsistent), restricting policy generalization. We study this gap through a human evaluation of a SoTA reasoning model for autonomous driving, revealing an inconsistent coupling between reasoning quality and downstream trajectory improvement. We then operationalize a behavioral surrogate for embodied faithfulness through a learned critic, Pinocchio, scoring observation grounding and stepwise coherence, and use this critic as a dense reward signal in post-training an embodied policy with reinforcement learning. Across withheld driving benchmarks, our post-trained planner improves faithfulness by 4% and 18% over SoTA alignment and trajectory error post-training baselines, respectively, while maintaining competitive downstream task performance. Finally, on a synthetic out-of-distribution test set, post-training for faithfulness improves policy responsiveness to rare counterfactual scenarios by 1.6x that of a SoTA policy, suggesting that faithful reasoning traces contribute to more robust, generalizable, and interpretable embodied intelligence. Project page: https://mjf-su.github.io/pinocchio/

</details>


### [74] [A Reliable Context-Aware and Temporal Planning Framework for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04689)
*Argho Dey,Yunfei Yin,Swachha Ray,Md Minhazul Islam,Zheng Yuan,Sijing Xiong,Hongyu Liu,Zhiqiu Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RCT-AD框架，通过显式建模特征质量和时间一致性，在BEV空间中实现更安全、更稳健的自动驾驶规划，在nuScenes基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV方法在融合时序信息时未评估底层观测的可靠性，导致在摄像头观测受遮挡、运动模糊、光照变化等退化条件下，轨迹规划变得不稳定，碰撞风险上升。

Method: RCT-AD包含三个核心组件：(1) 可靠上下文感知模块，通过质量门控FILO记忆机制评估每帧可靠性并选择性保留可信特征，从可靠历史上下文中重建退化观测；(2) 时序轨迹规划器，捕捉长期依赖和多智能体交互以生成平滑安全的轨迹；(3) 联合检测-分割头，将语义和运动线索注入共享BEV空间以增强场景理解。

Result: 在nuScenes基准上，RCT-AD取得61.5 NDS、52.9 mAP和52.3 mIoU，在感知精度、运动预测和规划鲁棒性上均优于近期端到端基线，且保持适用于实时部署的计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式建模特征质量与时间一致性能够有效提升自动驾驶在感知退化条件下的规划鲁棒性和安全性。

Abstract: Safe operation of autonomous vehicles in dense urban traffic depends on perception and planning that remain reliable when onboard sensing is degraded. In real driving conditions, camera observations are frequently corrupted by occlusion, motion blur, illumination change, and sensor noise, and when such degraded observations are aggregated indiscriminately over time, trajectory planning becomes unstable and collision risk rises for both the ego vehicle and surrounding road users. Recent Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) approaches unify perception and planning through a shared spatial representation, but most fuse temporal information across frames without assessing the reliability of the underlying observations. We present a Reliable Context-Aware and Temporal Planning framework for Autonomous Driving (RCT-AD) that explicitly models feature quality and temporal consistency to support safer, more consistent planning. A Reliable Context Awareness module scores per-frame reliability and selectively retains trustworthy features through a quality-gated First-In-Last-Out (FILO) memory mechanism, reconstructing degraded observations from reliable historical context so that corrupted inputs do not destabilize the scene representation. A Temporal Trajectory Planner captures long-term dependencies and multi-agent interactions to produce smoother, safety-aware trajectories, while a joint detection-and-segmentation head injects semantic and motion cues into the shared BEV space to strengthen scene understanding. Experiments on the nuScenes autonomous driving benchmark show that RCT-AD improves perception accuracy, motion prediction, and planning robustness over recent end-to-end baselines, achieving 61.5 nuScenes Detection Score, 52.9 mean Average Precision, and 52.3 mean Intersection over Union, while maintaining competitive computational efficiency suitable for real-time deployment.

</details>


### [75] [Geometry-Aware Motion Latents for Learning Robust Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04714)
*Yunchao Zhang,Yijia Weng,Ruizhe Liu,Ming Hu,Leonidas Guibas,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoMoLa通过预测点云在操作过程中的三维几何演变来学习离散运动潜码，而非重建视觉外观，利用单视图RGB-D输入实现了最先进的操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多依赖从视觉序列重建外观模式来提取运动潜码，但有效的动作抽象需要理解三维几何变换。作者希望从几何演化而非像素模式中学习运动表征，从而提升机器人操作的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出GeoMoLa框架，学习离散运动潜码，目标函数是预测点云在操作过程中的空间几何变化（4D目标：空间几何随时间演变），而非重建视觉观测。仅使用单视图RGB-D输入。

Result: 在多样化的操作基准测试中达到最先进性能，优于需要多视图重建的现有方法。消融实验验证了几何预测是性能提升的关键。学到的运动编码在新场景中产生物理一致的变换，且在杂乱环境中仅需少量演示即可实现鲁棒操作。

Conclusion: 有效的机器人运动潜码应从理解运动的三维几何效应中涌现，而非从像素级模式中学习；几何推理是操作任务的核心能力。

Abstract: Learning motion latents for robotic manipulation heavily relies on extracting motion patterns from visual sequences, yet effective action abstractions require understanding three-dimensional geometric transformations. Here, we introduce GeoMoLa (Geometry-Aware Motion Latents), which learns discrete motion latent codes by predicting how point clouds evolve during manipulation rather than reconstructing visual observations. This four-dimensional objective -- spatial geometry changing through time -- forces latent representations to encode actual physical motion rather than appearance patterns. GeoMoLa achieves state-of-the-art performance using only single-view RGB-D input, while existing methods require multi-view reconstruction, succeeding across diverse manipulation benchmarks. Our ablations reveal that geometric prediction is the key to driving performance, quantitatively validating that manipulation depends on spatial understanding. Furthermore, the learned codes exhibit effective motion abstraction: applying them to novel scenes produces physically consistent transformations regardless of visual context. Our real-world experiments also confirm this robustness capability, achieving robust manipulation with minimal demonstrations in cluttered environments where geometric reasoning determines success. Thus, we demonstrate that effective motion latents for robot control can better emerge from understanding motion through its three-dimensional effects rather than pixel-level patterns.

</details>


### [76] [Spatial Attention: Adapting Execution Horizons for Diffusion Policies via Observation Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04739)
*Che-Sang Park,Junsu Ha,Jianlong Fu,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应调整动作块执行时域的方法，通过空间注意力（Spatial Attention）指标动态分配执行时域，在保持平均执行时域的同时显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于生成模型采样动作块的机器人学习方法采用固定执行时域，难以平衡响应速度与计算成本，需要自适应调整机制。

Method: 定义空间注意力（Spatial Attention）为动作对数似然关于观测的梯度期望平方范数，表征策略对观测变化的敏感度。在固定采样预算下，推导使累积似然下降最小的执行时域与空间注意力成反比。通过联合预测未来空间注意力与动作块，动态分配执行时域：高空间注意力阶段用短时域，低空间注意力阶段用长时域。

Result: 在标准与扰动任务（仿真与真实机器人）上，该方法相比固定时域基线显著提升成功率，同时保持平均执行时域不变。

Conclusion: 自适应执行时域策略有效平衡了机器人学习的响应性与计算效率，空间注意力是指导时域动态调整的有效指标。

Abstract: Sampling action chunks via generative models has become a widely adopted methodology for robotic learning from demonstration. However, existing methods often struggle to balance responsiveness and computational cost because they execute each action chunk for a fixed execution horizon. In this paper, we adaptively adjust the execution horizon of sampled action chunks, balancing responsiveness and computational efficiency. We introduce Spatial Attention -- defined as the expected squared norm of the gradient of the action log-likelihood with respect to the observation -- which indicates the sensitivity of the policy's action distribution to variations in the observation. We show that, under a fixed budget of chunk samplings, the execution horizon that minimizes the cumulative likelihood drop induced by disturbances decreases as Spatial Attention increases. By forecasting future Spatial Attention values alongside the action chunk, our framework dynamically assigns shorter execution horizons to phases with high Spatial Attention, and longer horizons to phases with low Spatial Attention. Experiments on standard and perturbed tasks, in both simulation and on a real robot, show that our method significantly improves success rates over fixed-horizon baselines while maintaining the average execution horizon.

</details>


### [77] [Trajectory-Anchor Optimization for Overconfident Thermal Visual Place Recognition: Zero-Leakage OOD Auditing and Kidnapped-Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04745)
*Zhiyuan Lu,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出轨迹锚点优化(TAO)方法，通过将多视图时间验证压缩为批量SE(2) Procrustes对齐问题，以O(KN)的严格有界复杂度替代传统多假设跟踪的指数级开销，在宏观尺度(>10m)上利用多视图几何一致性可靠隔离灾难性拓扑断裂并抑制关键误接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础模型的热视觉地点识别前端在闭环检索中表现出色，但在遇到分布外或未建图条件时会产生过度自信的强制匹配失败模式，生成高置信度的假回环候选。传统多假设跟踪后端虽能缓解歧义，但其指数级计算开销违反实时机器人约束。

Method: 将多视图时间验证压缩为批量SE(2) Procrustes对齐问题，利用张量级向量化和单次批量SVD，绕过动态树扩展，保证每帧执行循环严格有界为O(KN)。

Result: 在5m半径内，幻觉具有局部一致几何性可欺骗刚性对齐；超过此阈值后，K=100个不同假设在全局地图上空间分散，破坏滑动窗口内的刚性时间共视约束，联合优化残差急剧上升，在10m处建立独特的宏观收敛盆地，可靠隔离拓扑断裂并抑制误接受。

Conclusion: TAO在严格零泄漏评估协议下证明，虽然被动几何后端无法在微观尺度(<5m)上分离度量定位误差与连贯幻觉，但TAO可在宏观尺度上作为高效故障安全滤波器运行。

Abstract: Modern thermal visual place recognition (TIR-VPR) frontends based on foundation models achieve remarkable closed-set retrieval but suffer from an overconfident forced-matching failure mode. Under out-of-distribution (OOD) or unmapped conditions, they generate highly plausible yet false loop candidates without a drop in similarity scores. While classical multi-hypothesis tracking (MHT) backends can mitigate these ambiguities by maintaining divergent trajectory beliefs, their exponential computational overhead violates real-time robotic constraints. To bridge this gap, we present Trajectory-Anchor Optimization (TAO). To counter the combinatorial challenge of evaluating parallel hypotheses (e.g., K=100), TAO compresses multi-view temporal verification into a batched SE(2) Procrustes alignment problem. By leveraging tensor-level vectorization and single-invocation batched SVD, this formulation bypasses the dynamic tree expansion of MHT, guaranteeing a strictly bounded per-frame execution loop of O(KN). Under a strict zero-leakage evaluation protocol, we show that while a passive geometric backend cannot mathematically separate metric localization errors from coherent hallucinations at a micro-scale (<5m) due to local visual ambiguities, TAO serves as an efficient fail-safe filter at a macro-scale. Within a 5m radius, hallucinations often possess a locally consistent geometry that deceives rigid alignment. However, beyond this threshold, the K=100 disparate hypotheses disperse spatially across the global map. This dispersion breaks the rigid temporal co-visibility constraint within the sliding window (N=20), causing the joint optimization residual to escalate sharply. Consequently, TAO establishes a distinct macroscopic convergence basin (10m) where multi-view geometric consistency reliably isolates catastrophic topological breaks and suppresses critical false acceptances.

</details>


### [78] [SLAM: Structured and Localized Analytic Manifold Adaptation for Lifelong VPR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04764)
*Kenta Tsukahara,Kanji Tanaka,Rai Hisada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLAM框架，通过无迹变换、高斯混合模型和H∞鲁棒边界优化的统一闭环解析递归，在视觉位置识别中实现终身部署的连续适应，无需架构分割即可通过单一正则化参数调节标称精度与最坏情况鲁棒性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉位置识别在终身部署中需要持续适应新环境，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出了SLAM（结构化局部解析流形适应）框架，统一了基于无迹变换的不确定性感知平滑、高斯混合模型的拓扑空间划分以及H∞鲁棒边界优化，形成统一的闭环解析递归。

Result: 不确定性平滑与局部映射的协同配置（U+G）在标称精度上达到27.5%的最优结果；完整H∞边界部署无需架构分割，通过单一正则化参数实现标称精度与最坏情况干扰抑制之间的内在权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过数学上保证的极小极大鲁棒边界，实现了系统在标称放置精度与最坏情况干扰衰减之间的无缝调节。

Abstract: Visual Place Recognition (VPR) in lifelong deployment requires continuous adaptation to new environments without catastrophic forgetting. In this paper, we propose SLAM, a Structured and Localized Analytic Manifold adaptation framework. Our framework elegantly unifies uncertainty-aware smoothing via Unscented transformation, topological space partitioning through a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and $H_\infty$ robust bound optimization into a singular, unified closed-form analytical recursion. Exhaustive ablation studies demonstrate that while the synergistic combination of uncertainty smoothing and localized mapping (U+G configuration) achieves the state-of-the-art nominal accuracy of 27.5%, the full deployment of the $H_\infty$ bound does not require an architectural split; rather, it introduces a mathematically guaranteed minimax robust bound. This formulation enables the system to seamlessly modulate the intrinsic trade-off between nominal placement precision and worst-case disturbance attenuation through a single regularization parameter.

</details>


### [79] [CAC-VLA: Context-Gated Action Conditioning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04816)
*Yifu Xiong,Wenhao Yu,Jiaxuan Lin,Bojun Zou,Jiahao Li,Lu Zhang,Yanyong Zhang,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CAC-VLA框架，通过轻量级潜在动作接口实现VLM原生的动作条件化，利用上下文门控机制自适应调节潜在动作引导对专家动作生成的影响，在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus基准上分别达到98.3%和89.5%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型中视觉-语言表示并未针对动作条件化进行显式优化，导致动作专家需要弥合多模态理解与精细运动控制之间的差距；近期动作推理方法虽引入额外模块生成显式动作规划或推理信号，但往往需要独立的动作生成框架，缺乏VLM原生的动作条件化能力。

Method: CAC-VLA框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 在VLM内部学习轻量级潜在动作接口，从未来动作片段中编码粗到细的结构化潜在动作表示；(2) 预测这些粗到细的潜在动作，而非生成可执行的轨迹；(3) 通过上下文门控机制自适应地调控潜在动作引导对动作专家生成的影响，实现VLM原生的动作条件化。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上达到98.3%的平均成功率，在LIBERO-Plus基准上达到89.5%的平均成功率。

Conclusion: 上下文门控的潜在动作条件化是连续专家控制的有效接口，证明了VLM原生动作条件化框架的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a promising paradigm for generalist robot manipulation, where visual-language representations are used to condition continuous action generation. However, these representations are not explicitly optimized for action conditioning, leaving the action expert to bridge the gap between multimodal understanding and precise motor control. Recent action-reasoning methods introduce additional modules to generate explicit action plans or action-space reasoning signals, demonstrating the benefit of action-level guidance but often requiring separate action-generation frameworks. We propose CAC-VLA, a Context-Gated Action Conditioning framework that learns a lightweight latent-action interface directly within the VLM. Instead of generating executable trajectories, CAC-VLA trains the VLM to predict coarse-to-fine latent actions, which are structured representations encoded from future action segments, and adaptively leverages them to condition the action expert via a context gate. This enables VLM-native action conditioning while calibrating the influence of latent-action guidance on expert action generation. Experiments on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus demonstrate the effectiveness of CAC-VLA, achieving 98.3% average success rate on LIBERO and 89.5% LIBERO-Plus, suggesting that context-gated latent-action conditioning is an effective interface for continuous expert control.

</details>


### [80] [Athena-WBC: Capability-Aligned Policy Experts for Long-Tail Humanoid Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04837)
*Yuan Jiang,Ningyuan Zhang,Xicun Yang,Shidi Li,Yuzhi Jiang,Zhiyi Rong,Shuaikang Ma,Chuanzheng Li,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Athena-WBC，一种紧凑的教师-学生流水线，通过能力对齐的策略专家解决人形机器人全身控制中长尾动作（高动态过渡和平衡关键动作）难以学习的问题。动态专家采用追踪导向、约束感知的目标函数，平衡专家使用重力课程提升早期训练存活率，最终通过DAgger蒸馏和RL微调压缩为单一控制器，在全身人形机器人上优于SONIC基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过重分配训练努力（如难动作过采样、子集隔离或专家分配）来改进人形机器人运动追踪控制器，但即使在针对性训练下，仍有一组可行的训练片段无法被解决，尤其是高动态过渡和平衡关键动作。这些失败不仅源于训练不充分，更源于运动需求与默认训练配方诱导的有效能力之间的不匹配。

Method: 提出Athena-WBC流水线：1) 动态专家使用追踪导向、约束感知的目标函数，去除保守努力和时间控制惩罚，保留物理可行性约束；2) 平衡专家使用重力课程提升早期训练存活率；3) 特权教师根据动作路由进行DAgger蒸馏；4) 压缩为单一控制器，使用可部署观测并经过RL微调。

Result: 在全尺寸人形机器人上，该方法相比SONIC基线，改善了训练集中长尾动作的恢复率和未见过动作的追踪效果，且仅使用少量专家。

Conclusion: 人形机器人运动追踪中长尾动作失败的根本原因是运动需求与诱导能力之间的不匹配，而非训练不充分；通过能力对齐的专家设计可以有效解决这一问题。

Abstract: Large-scale humanoid motion-tracking controllers are commonly improved by reallocating training effort: difficult motions are sampled more often, isolated into smaller subsets, or assigned to specialized experts. We show that this view is incomplete. In strong whole-body-control baselines, a residual set of feasible training clips remains unsolved even under targeted training, especially for high-dynamic transitions and balance-critical motions. These failures arise not only from insufficient exposure, but from a mismatch between the motion demands and the effective capability induced by the default training recipe. We propose Athena-WBC, a compact teacher-student pipeline with capability-aligned policy experts for long-tail humanoid whole-body control. Dynamic experts use a tracking-focused, constraint-aware objective that removes conservative effort and temporal-control penalties while preserving physical feasibility constraints; balance experts use a gravity curriculum to improve early-training survivability. The resulting privileged teachers are motion-routed for DAgger distillation and then compressed into a single controller with deployable observations followed by RL fine-tuning. Experiments on a full-size humanoid show improved recovery of training-set long-tail motions and better held-out tracking than a strong SONIC-recipe baseline, using only a small number of experts.

</details>


### [81] [WinTA-GIL: Windowed Trajectory Alignment for GNSS-IMU-LiDAR Heading Refinement in Intermittent Signal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04879)
*Kaixin Feng,Zhichao Wen,Zhaohong Liao,Xin Xia,You Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WinTA-GIL框架，通过时间窗口优化策略融合GNSS、IMU和LiDAR信息，将航向估计转化为可重复的轨迹一致性优化问题，并引入基于状态判别的自适应重估计机制，显著提升了多源融合定位系统在复杂环境下的航向估计精度和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有融合定位系统在复杂环境中因缺乏重力约束和航向弱可观性问题，航向估计精度和可靠性不足；多数方法仅适用于启动阶段的初始对准，无法在导航过程中动态修正航向，导致长时间导航或GNSS信号中断后累积漂移和观测噪声影响严重。

Method: WinTA-GIL框架通过时间窗口优化策略融合GNSS、IMU和LiDAR信息；利用LIO生成的高精度局部轨迹与滤波后的GNSS观测进行配准，将航向估计转化为可重复的轨迹一致性优化问题；并引入基于状态判别的自适应重估计机制，在必要时触发航向修正以抑制惯性漂移。

Result: 在开源和自采集数据集上的大量实验表明，WinTA-GIL在估计精度和系统鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: WinTA-GIL通过将航向估计从单次初始对准扩展为可重复的动态优化过程，有效解决了多源融合定位系统中航向漂移的关键挑战，为复杂环境下的高精度定位提供了新范式。

Abstract: Although multi-source fusion positioning systems have achieved significant progress, accurate and reliable heading estimation remains a critical challenge due to the lack of gravitational constraints and the inherent weak observability of heading in complex environments. Most existing methodologies are specifically tailored for the startup phase, relying on a singular initial alignment to establish the heading reference. Consequently, these approaches lack the adaptability required to refine heading estimates dynamically, which renders the system highly vulnerable to accumulated drift and observation noise during prolonged navigation or immediately following GNSS signal outages. To address these limitations, this paper proposes WinTA-GIL, a novel heading refinement framework that integrates information from Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS), Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU), and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) through a temporal window-based optimization strategy. Unlike conventional alignment methods restricted to the startup phase, WinTA-GIL leverages high-precision local trajectories from LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (LIO) to register against filtered GNSS observations. This approach transforms heading estimation into a repeatable, trajectory-based consistency optimization problem. In particular, an adaptive re-estimation mechanism based on state discrimination is incorporated to trigger heading corrections whenever necessary, thereby effectively suppressing the inertial drift accumulated during challenging conditions. Extensive experiments on both open-source and self-collected datasets demonstrate that WinTA-GIL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art approaches in both estimation accuracy and system robustness.

</details>


### [82] [PRISM: Personalized Robotic Dataset Generation via Image-based Scene and Motion Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04880)
*Dogyu Ko,Haneul Kim,Chanyoung Yeo,Dowoon Lee,Taeho Park,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一种从单张图像和自然语言指令生成个性化机器人数据集的端到端流水线，通过构建语义和几何对齐但实例多样化的数字孪生场景，无需遥操作即可合成可执行的示范轨迹，在LIBERO和真实世界操作任务中取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练视觉-语言-动作模型在特定用户环境中直接部署时泛化能力有限，需要收集目标环境定制数据集；遥操作数据质量高但成本大，仿真数据可扩展但与目标环境差异大，难以同时满足数据质量与规模需求。

Method: 提出PRISM端到端流水线：(1) 从单张图像和自然语言指令出发，构建与用户环境语义和几何对齐但在实例层面多样化的"数字孪生场景"；(2) 在此场景中无需人工遥操作即可合成可执行的机器人示范轨迹。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上，PRISM生成数据训练的策略优于基线方法；在三个真实世界操作任务中达到100%成功率；在训练时未见过的环境中仍保持更强的性能表现。

Conclusion: PRISM有效弥合了仿真数据与真实用户环境之间的差距，通过数字孪生场景实现了高质量、可扩展的个性化数据集生成，为机器人策略的部署提供了一种高效途径。

Abstract: Recent advances in large-scale pretrained vision-language-action models have improved robot policy learning, but directly deploying such policies in user-specific environments remains challenging due to limited generalization, which inevitably requires collecting a dataset tailored to the target environment. Teleoperation yields well-aligned data but is costly and difficult to scale, whereas simulation scales easily but struggles to resemble the target environment and generate task-specific trajectories. To meet both simultaneously, we propose PRISM, an end-to-end pipeline that generates personalized robotic datasets from a single image and a natural-language instruction. PRISM constructs digital cousin scenes that are semantically and geometrically aligned with the user environment yet diverse at the instance level, and synthesizes executable demonstrations without human teleoperation. Extensive experiments show that policies trained on PRISM-generated datasets outperform those trained on baseline-generated datasets on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, achieve up to 100\% success rate on three real-world manipulation tasks, and maintain stronger performance when evaluated in environments that differ from those seen during training.

</details>


### [83] [DSWAM: A Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for Fine-Grained Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04927)
*Jian Zhu,Jianjun Zhang,Taiyi Su,Tianbin Liu,Zhangyuan Wang,Kai Xie,Zitai Huang,Chong Ma,Youzhang He,Tianjian Wang,Hanyang Wang,Weihao Ding,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DSWAM是一个双系统世界动作基础模型，结合System 1的WAM执行器（默认控制路径）和System 2的VLM子任务规划器（仅在任务分解有益时激活），通过TensorRT加速、异步执行和实时分块技术实现实际机器人操作，并在DeMaVLA变形操作设置下与VLA策略进行了公平对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM模型擅长物理执行但缺乏语言级规划能力（VLM-based VLA具有），而复杂多步家庭任务需要将粗粒度指令分解为细粒度子任务序列；同时领域内缺乏WAM与VLA在真实机器人上的公平对比（数据、平台、协议不统一）。

Method: 提出DSWAM双系统架构：System 2（VLM子任务规划器）仅在需要时激活，从短期视觉历史和全局任务提示预测可执行子任务；System 1（WAM执行器）作为默认控制路径，采用动作预测与视频联合训练，推理时直接预测动作块（无需显式生成未来视频），并集成TensorRT加速、异步执行和实时分块（RTC）以避免策略查询阻塞控制。

Result: 在DeMaVLA变形操作设置下，使用匹配的机器人平台、预训练数据、后训练数据和评估标准，进行了VLA与WAM执行能力的公平对比。

Conclusion: DSWAM通过双系统设计弥补了WAM在语言级规划上的不足，并通过工程优化实现了实际机器人上的实用部署，同时为VLA与WAM的公平比较提供了实验框架。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) provide a promising alternative to Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies by using video-based world modeling as dense supervision for robot action learning. Existing WAMs excel at physically grounded execution, but typically lack the explicit language-level planning interface in VLM-based VLAs for decomposing coarse instructions. Such decomposition becomes important when household tasks involve complex multi-step goals, where coarse user commands need to be converted into sequences of fine-grained executable subtasks. Meanwhile, the field still lacks a fair real-robot comparison between VLA and WAM execution capabilities, since existing systems often differ in data, robot embodiments, and task protocols. To address both the decomposition gap and the need for a controlled WAM-VLA comparison, we introduce DSWAM, a Dual-System World Action Foundation Model for fine-grained robot manipulation. DSWAM keeps a System 1 WAM executor as the default control path and optionally activates a System 2 vision-language subtask planner only when task decomposition is useful. The planner predicts executable subtasks from short-term visual history and a global task prompt, while the WAM executor performs world-aware action generation for each instruction or subtask. The executor is trained with action prediction and video co-training, but inference directly predicts action chunks without explicit future video generation. To make this execution path practical on real robots, we further integrate TensorRT acceleration, asynchronous execution, and real-time chunking (RTC) so that policy queries do not block robot control. To provide a fair real-robot comparison with VLA policies, we build and evaluate DSWAM under the DeMaVLA real-world deformable manipulation setting with matched robot platform, pretraining data, post-training data, and evaluation criteria.

</details>


### [84] [Closing the Reality Gap: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Deployment for Dexterous Force-Based Grasping and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04940)
*Zhe Zhao,Zhibin Li,Yilin Ou,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于sim-to-real的强化学习方法，通过结合密集触觉反馈和关节扭矩传感，并引入快速触觉模拟、电流-扭矩标定以及执行器动力学建模与随机化，在五指灵巧手上实现了零样本迁移的可控抓取力跟踪和物体重新定向操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指灵巧手具备类人操作能力，但由于接触丰富的物理特性和非理想执行器驱动，在真实硬件上训练控制策略非常困难。

Method: (i) 基于并行正向运动学的快速触觉模拟，提供高分辨率触觉信号；(ii) 电流-扭矩标定，无需扭矩传感器；(iii) 执行器动力学建模与随机化，弥合驱动差距；采用非对称演员-评论家PPO在仿真中训练策略并零样本部署到五指手上。

Result: 训练的策略在真实硬件上实现了可控抓取力跟踪和物体重新定向，无需微调即可鲁棒执行。

Conclusion: 通过在观测空间中结合触觉和扭矩，并配合可扩展的传感与驱动建模，为可靠灵巧操作提供了实用解决方案，首次实现了多指灵巧手上零样本迁移的可控抓取。

Abstract: Human-like dexterous hands with multiple fingers offer human-level manipulation capabilities but remain difficult to train the control policies that can deploy on real hardware due to contact-rich physics and imperfect actuation. We present a sim-to-real reinforcement learning method that leverages dense tactile feedback combined with joint torque sensing to explicitly regulate physical interactions. To enable effective sim-to-real transfer, we introduce (i) a computationally fast tactile simulation that computes distances between dense virtual tactile units and the object via parallel forward kinematics, providing high-rate, high-resolution touch signals needed by RL; (ii) a current-to-torque calibration that eliminates the need for torque sensors on dexterous hands by mapping motor current to joint torque; and (iii) actuator dynamics modeling with randomization to account for non-ideal torque-speed effects and bridge the actuation gaps. Using an asymmetric actor-critic PPO pipeline, we train policies entirely in simulation and deploy them directly to a five-finger hand. The resulting policies demonstrate two essential human-hand skills: (1) command-based controllable grasp force tracking and (2) reorientation of objects in the hand, both of which are robustly executed without fine-tuning on the robot. By combining tactile and torque in the observation space with scalable sensing and actuation modeling, our system provides a practical solution to achieve reliable dexterous manipulation. To our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of controllable grasping on a multi-finger dexterous hand trained entirely in simulation and transferred zero-shot on real hardware.

</details>


### [85] [Multi-Robot Open Adaptive Teaming Across Unseen Environments, Partners, and Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04972)
*Yang Li,Feng Xue,Fan Mo,Yunhao Liu,Jianhong Wang,Ying Wen,Qingrui Zhang,Shaoshuai Mou,Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出超图形式博弈框架HOLA，解决机器人团队在动态变化环境、未知队友和变规模组队下的开放自适应多机器人协作问题，在无人机和四足机器人平台上验证了超越所有基线的适应性，并直接迁移到真实硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在封闭世界假设下孤立地应对环境变化、未知队友和团队规模变化，缺乏能同时处理这三种动态适应性的统一框架。

Method: 提出超图形式博弈（hypergraphic-form game）建模团队级协作关系，超越成对交互；基于此设计HOLA算法，通过渐进式扩展训练中的队友和环境多样性实现开放学习。

Result: 在多无人机和多四足机器人协同追捕任务中，HOLA在所有三个适应性维度上均优于所有基线方法，且学到的策略无需微调即可直接迁移到Crazyflie和Zsibot L1实物平台。

Conclusion: 超图形式博弈为动态团队协作提供了理论基础，HOLA通过开放学习范式有效解决了现实世界中多机器人团队面临的联合适应挑战。

Abstract: Deploying robot teams in the real world requires simultaneous adaptation to unseen environments, unknown partners, and varying team sizes, yet existing approaches often address these challenges in isolation under the closed-world assumption of fixed teammates. We formalize this as open adaptive multi-robot teaming and propose a hypergraphic-form game formulation that captures team-level cooperative relationships beyond pairwise interactions, providing a principled foundation for coordination structure inference when team composition changes dynamically within episodes. Unlike graph neural network architectures, this is a game-theoretic construct for modeling strategic interactions and payoff structures among agents. Building on this formulation, we develop the Hypergraphic Open-ended Learning Algorithm (HOLA), which progressively expands partner and environment diversity during training rather than optimizing for fixed configurations. Evaluated on cooperative pursuit with multi-drone and multi-quadruped platforms, HOLA outperforms all baselines across all three adaptability dimensions. Learned policies transfer directly to physical hardware without fine-tuning, with successful deployments on Crazyflie and Zsibot L1 platforms confirming robust real-world coordination in novel environments with unseen teammates.

</details>


### [86] [Designing Touch for Trauma-Informed Social Robots: A Design Space for Direct and Indirect Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04981)
*Madeleine Rischer,Benedikt Bußmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何在创伤知情护理原则指导下，为PTSD患者设计社交机器人的触觉交互，提出了包含直接触觉与间接触觉的区分，以及由"驱动模态、驱动目标、预期效果"三个维度构成的设计空间，并推导出创伤敏感性设计考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉是人机交互中基本且重要的沟通方式，在治疗中可支持情绪调节和压力缓解。然而PTSD患者的触觉交互设计必须遵循创伤知情护理原则，目前缺乏系统的设计框架来指导此类机器人触觉交互的设计。

Method: 首先区分直接触觉（用户与机器人物理接触）和间接触觉（通过可穿戴设备等人工制品介导的触觉）。在此基础上提出包含三个维度的设计空间：驱动模态、驱动目标、预期效果。通过创伤知情护理视角分析这些维度，推导出关键设计考量。

Result: 提出了一个三维设计空间用于指导创伤敏感的社交机器人触觉交互设计，并得出了基于创伤知情护理原则的设计考量。

Conclusion: 该设计框架为开发支持PTSD患者的创伤敏感性社交机器人提供了概念基础，使触觉交互能够兼顾安全、透明、自主和触发回避等创伤知情护理核心原则。

Abstract: Touch is a fundamental communication modality in human-robot interaction and may support grounding, emotional regulation, and stress reduction in therapeutic contexts. However, designing touch-based interactions for individuals with post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) requires careful consideration of trauma-informed care (TIC) principles, including safety, transparency, autonomy, and trigger avoidance. This paper investigates how touch actuation in social robots can be designed to align with trauma-informed care. We distinguish between direct touch, involving physical contact between a user and a robot, and indirect touch, mediated through artifacts such as wearables or smart textiles. Building on this distinction, we propose a design space comprising three dimensions: Actuation Modality, Objective of the Actuation, and Intended Effect of the Actuation. We analyze these dimensions through the lens of trauma-informed care and derive key design considerations for touch-based interventions. The resulting approach provides a conceptual foundation for developing trauma-sensitive social robots that support individuals with PTSD through touch-based interactions.

</details>


### [87] [InternVLA-A1.5: Unifying Understanding, Latent Foresight, and Action for Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04988)
*Haoxiang Ma,Junhao Cai,Xiaoxu Xu,Hao Li,Yuyin Yang,Yang Tian,Jiafei Cao,Hongrui Zhu,Zherui Qiu,Zhaxizhuoma,Yuqiang Yang,Jiaqi Peng,Xueyuan Wei,Yangkun Zhu,Jiahao Jiang,Xing Gao,Hanqing Wang,Feng Yuan,Kailin Li,Xueyue Zhu,Tai Wang,Yan Ding,Jiangmiao Pang,Jia Zeng,Jingjing Zhang,Bowen Zhou,Yao Mu,Chunhua Shen,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InternVLA-A1.5提出了一种基于原生VLM骨干的机器人操作统一模型，通过轻量级专家模块进行连续动作生成，并将未来预测重构为潜在查询问题，利用冻结的预训练视频生成模型提供动力学先验，在1.2M机器人数据和3M多模态数据上预训练后，在六个模拟基准上取得最佳结果，并在真实世界中展现出最强的组合泛化和长程任务执行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一机器人操作模型存在三个问题：(1) 预训练VLM骨干的语义被侵蚀；(2) 异构目标间的相互干扰；(3) 从零开始在像素空间学习未来预测，未能利用预训练视频生成模型的动力学先验。

Method: (1) 在原生VLM骨干上持续训练VQA和子任务预测，保持语义能力；(2) 附加轻量级统一专家模块用于连续动作生成；(3) 将未来预测重构为潜在查询问题，使用少量可学习的foresight tokens在冻结的预训练视频生成模型监督下压缩任务相关未来信息为紧凑潜在编码，避免像素级生成学习。

Result: 在1.2M机器人数据和3M多模态样本上预训练，所有六个模拟基准上取得最佳综合结果；真实世界中，保留的语义能力带来最强的组合泛化（处理未见过的指令绑定），两个设计共同支持长程任务执行。

Conclusion: 通过保留预训练VLM的语义先验并利用预训练视频生成模型的动力学先验，InternVLA-A1.5有效解决了现有方法在语义侵蚀、目标干扰和未来预测方面的问题，实现了模拟和真实场景下的最优性能。

Abstract: Unified models for robot manipulation aim to equip one policy with both the semantic priors of pretrained VLMs and the physical dynamics learned through future prediction. In practice, existing designs tend to erode the semantics of the pretrained backbone, suffer interference among heterogeneous objectives, and learn future prediction from scratch in pixel space, leaving the dynamics priors of pretrained video generators unexploited. We present InternVLA-A1.5, which builds the policy on a native VLM backbone that keeps training on VQA and subtask prediction, and attaches a lightweight unified expert for continuous action generation. Future prediction is recast as a latent-querying problem, where a small set of learnable foresight tokens condenses the task-relevant future into a compact latent code under the supervision of a frozen pretrained video generation model, so the policy inherits world-model dynamics priors without ever learning pixel-level generation. The video branch is discarded at inference, keeping real-time control. Pretrained on 1.2M robot episodes and 3M multimodal samples, InternVLA-A1.5 achieves the best overall results on all six simulation benchmarks. In the real world, the preserved semantics deliver the strongest compositional generalization on held-out instruction bindings, and the two designs together sustain long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [88] [ECO: Incremental Ego-Centric Octree Update for Point Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05092)
*Jaemin Yu,Seongyoon Jeong,Kang-Wook Chon,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向移动机器人的Ego-Centric Octree（ECO）空间数据结构，通过3D滑动窗口机制将建图空间动态约束在机器人周围，并采用高效的增量更新算法，显著降低了实时点云流处理中的计算和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全局八叉树结构在移动机器人实时处理连续点云流时存在高延迟和树生长不平衡的问题，亟需一种高效、平衡且适合实时感知的空间数据结构。

Method: ECO设计为3D滑动窗口，动态将建图空间限定在机器人当前周围区域；提出增量更新算法，将环境划分为移出、移入和重叠三个区域，消除冗余的全局坐标变换。

Result: 在KITTI基准上，ECO更新速度相比全静态重建平均提升24.87%，相比有界增量基线平均提升54.60%；下游任务中体素地图生成速度提升最高34.17%。

Conclusion: ECO在保持树结构平衡和更新成本可控的同时，自然保留了动态场景中移动物体的短期时间记忆，为实时空间感知提供了有效的时空上下文。

Abstract: Constructing octrees for mobile robots that process continuous point streams in real time poses significant computational and memory challenges. Standard global structures often suffer from high latency and unbalanced tree growth. We introduce the Ego-Centric Octree (ECO), a spatial data structure that acts as a 3D sliding window, dynamically bounding the mapping space to the robot's immediate surroundings. ECO uses an efficient incremental update algorithm that categorizes the environment into shift-out, shift-in, and overlap regions, eliminating redundant global coordinate transformations. Evaluations on the KITTI benchmark demonstrate that ECO reduces update times by up to 25.60% (24.87% on average) compared to full static reconstruction and by up to 67.52% (54.60% on average) compared to a bounded incremental baseline. Furthermore, ECO substantially lowers the total system latency of downstream tasks, running up to 34.17% faster than full reconstruction in voxel-map generation. In dynamic scenes, ECO naturally retains a short-term temporal memory of moving objects, providing useful temporal context while keeping update cost bounded and the tree balanced for real-time spatial perception.

</details>


### [89] [Geometry-Aware Visual Odometry for Bronchoscopic Navigation via High-Gain Observer Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05162)
*Mohammadreza Kasaei,Francis Xiatian Zhang,Feng Li,Farshid Alambeigi,Kevin Dhaliwal,Mohsen Khadem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的视觉里程计框架，通过利用气道腔体的消失点线索，结合高增益观测器，在纹理贫乏的支气管镜图像中实现鲁棒的导航，相比现有方法将绝对轨迹误差降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航支气管镜平台严重依赖术前CT或外部传感器，限制了其在重症监护和资源受限场景中的应用；而纯视觉导航方法在纹理贫乏、高光干扰及管状结构消失点奇异性等问题下频繁跟踪失败和漂移。

Method: 1) 从气道腔体中检测消失点并反投影至3D射线，加权融合得到稳定前向航向；2) 利用扩张（looming）估计速度；3) 通过定制的高增益观测器将航向和速度估计与噪声VO输出融合，强制执行气道跟随先验并抑制漂移。

Result: 在离体机械通气人肺数据集上，以电磁跟踪为真值，相比ORB-SLAM2、LoFTR-VO、DPVO等方法，绝对轨迹误差降低50%以上，在所有测试序列中相对位姿误差最低。

Conclusion: 几何感知的消失点线索能够有效克服支气管镜图像中纹理匮乏和结构奇异性的挑战，为重症监护和资源受限环境下的支气管镜导航提供了可行的纯视觉方案。

Abstract: Navigational bronchoscopy is critical for pulmonary interventions, yet current platforms depend heavily on pre-operative CT or external sensors, limiting their use in critical care and resource-constrained settings. Vision-only navigation offers a scalable alternative, but conventional visual odometry (VO) struggles with texture-poor airway images, specularities, and the vanishing-point singularities of tubular anatomy, leading to frequent tracking failures and drift. We present a geometry-aware VO framework that explicitly leverages vanishing-point cues from airway lumens. Detected lumens are back-projected to 3D rays, whose weighted fusion yields a stable forward heading even when parallax cues are absent. This heading, together with looming-based velocity estimates, is fused with noisy VO outputs using a bespoke high-gain observer that enforces airway-following priors and rejects drift. We validate the method on ex-vivo mechanically ventilated human lungs with electromagnetic tracking ground truth. Compared to state-of-the-art pipelines (ORB-SLAM2, LoFTR-VO, DPVO), our approach reduces absolute trajectory error by more than 50% and achieves the lowest relative pose error across all test sequences.

</details>


### [90] [GelNeuro: A Sensing-Computing Integrated Neuromorphic Tactile System for Texture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05241)
*Luoyang Bian,Xinpan Meng,Zhenghua Ma,Houcheng Li,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GelNeuro，一个将GelSight Mini触觉前端与Speck2f神经形态SoC直接集成的感算一体系统。触觉信号通过动态视觉传感器事件经片上网络路由至脉冲卷积神经网络分类器，采用硬件感知的权重裁剪策略缓解精度损失。在15类自然纹理识别任务中，80ms推理窗口内达到96.3%准确率，板级功耗仅19.6mW，比传统CPU/GPU低三个数量级，且对未接触深度具有良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经形态视触觉系统仍需依赖主机进行事件读出、预处理或中继转发，无法实现真正的传感器直接到芯片推理，限制了低延迟和低功耗优势的发挥。

Method: 将GelSight Mini触觉前端与Speck2f神经形态SoC直接集成；触觉触发的标记运动作为DVS事件捕获，经片上网络路由至SCNN分类器；提出硬件感知的权重裁剪策略应对8-bit部署精度损失。

Result: 15类自然纹理识别任务中，硬件在环测试达96.3%准确率（80ms推理窗口）；板级功耗19.6mW，比CPU/GPU基线低三个数量级；对未见接触深度具备鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 直接传感器到芯片的触觉识别在边缘神经形态硬件上是可行的，实现了超低功耗、低延迟的触觉感知，为边缘神经形态硬件上的直接触觉识别提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Neuromorphic visuo-tactile sensing offers a promising paradigm for low-latency and low-power robotic perception. However, existing systems still rely heavily on a host computer for event readout, preprocessing, or relaying prior to chip inference. This paper presents GelNeuro, a fully integrated sensing-computing visuo-tactile system that directly pairs a GelSight Mini-based optical tactile front end with the Speck2f neuromorphic system-on-chip (SoC). Contact-induced marker motions are captured as dynamic vision sensor (DVS) events and routed through the on-chip network to a spiking convolutional neural network (SCNN) classifier. To mitigate accuracy degradation during 8-bit deployment, a hardware-aware weight clamping strategy is introduced. Evaluated on a 15-class natural texture recognition task, hardware-in-the-loop testing on the physical chip achieves a 96.3% accuracy within an 80 ms inference window. Notably, the system consumes only 19.6 mW of board-level active power-over three orders of magnitude lower than conventional CPU/GPU baselines on the same benchmark. GelNeuro also exhibits robust generalization across unseen contact depths, demonstrating the viability of direct sensor-to-chip tactile recognition on edge neuromorphic hardware.

</details>


### [91] [Socially-Aware Autonomous Doorway Traversal and Payload Delivery for Emergency Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05315)
*Andrew Snowdy,Ananya Trivedi,Sarvesh Prajapati,Lorena Maria Genua,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于行为树的机器人辅助紧急疏散框架，使机器人能够同时执行开门、运送救援设备、感知人群并调整行为等任务。在丰田HSR机器人上进行了105次试验，成功完成97次，证明了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助紧急疏散场景中，机器人需要同时具备多种能力（开门、递送救援设备、感知人群并调整行为），现有方法缺乏能够统一协调这些任务的框架。

Method: 以行为树（Behavior Tree）作为核心框架，通过环境触发选择高层任务，具备可扩展性。系统部署在丰田Human Support Robot上，涵盖5种硬件场景和3种仿真场景，共105次试验。

Result: 在105次试验中，系统成功完成97次，成功率达92.4%。系统能够正确决策是否按压门按钮、避让行人、通过他人打开的门、或先取救援设备再通过门等。

Conclusion: 该框架为机器人辅助紧急响应任务提供了实用的基础，具备良好的可扩展性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: In this work, we focus on the scenario of a robot-assisted emergency evacuation. We consider two capabilities relevant to such a setting. The first is opening doors ahead of the people being evacuated, so that their path toward an exit stays clear. The second is retrieving rescue equipment and delivering it to the emergency responders carrying out the evacuation. From a systems perspective, this involves several tasks at once. The robot must locate ADA-compliant door buttons and the rescue equipment it needs to retrieve. Additionally, it must remain aware of the people around it and adapt its behavior to them, so that it supports the evacuation rather than getting in the way. We address these demands with a behavior tree at the core of our framework. This structure is chosen for its ability to select high-level tasks based on environmental triggers, and to extend to new situations as they arise. We evaluate the system in 105 trials on the Toyota Human Support Robot, across five hardware and three simulation scenarios. These trials capture the decisions the robot must make in this setting: whether to press a door button, yield to a nearby person, walk through a door someone else is holding, or first retrieve rescue equipment before traversing the door. Overall, the system completes 97 of the 105 trials successfully. These results suggest our framework provides a practical basis for robotic assistance in broader emergency response tasks. Code and video demonstrations are available at https://github.com/AndrewSnowdy/hsr_mm_control.

</details>


### [92] [GaP: A Graph-as-Policy Multi-Agent Self-Learning Harness For Variational Automation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05369)
*Kaiyuan Chen,Shuangyu Xie,Letian Fu,Justin Yu,William Pacini,Sandeep Bajamahal,Hudson Kim,Jaimyn Drake,Daehwa Kim,Haoru Xue,Jonathan Francis,Christian Juette,Peter Schaldenbrand,Muhammet Yunus Seker,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Uksang Yoo,Guanzhi Wang,Adithyavairavan Murali,Balakumar Sundaralingam,S. Shankar Sastry,Spencer Huang,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-as-Policy (GaP)，一种多智能体编码框架，通过生成有向计算图结合感知、规划与控制节点，并利用内部仿真并行优化图结构与参数，在变分自动化任务中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业和工业应用中的机器人需要兼具可解释编程与开放世界适应性，但现有无模型策略在变分自动化任务（物体几何和姿态存在较大变化）中难以弥合可靠性差距。

Method: 提出GaP多智能体编码框架，基于模块化开放机器人技能库(MORSL)生成包含感知、规划与控制节点的有向计算图，并通过内部仿真环境并行排练不同图结构的任务实例，迭代优化图结构与参数以提升成功率和吞吐量。

Result: 在8个新的开放VA任务基准（4个仿真+4个真实世界）上，GaP的成功率显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: GaP结合了可解释的机器人编程与模型无关策略的开放世界适应性，为变分自动化任务提供了可靠且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: For robots to work reliably in commercial and industrial applications, can recent advances in agentic coding systems combine interpretable robot programming with the open-world adaptability of model-free policies? We focus on "Variational Automation" (VA), a class of tasks that have larger variations in object geometry and pose than fixed automation. Model-free policies often struggle to close the reliability gap for VA tasks, which must be executed persistently and reliably in commercial and industrial applications. Motivated by prior work on Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) and the Robot Operating System (ROS), we introduce Graph-as-Policy (GaP), a multi-agent coding harness that generates directed computation graphs with perception, planning, and control nodes from a Modular Open Robot Skill Library (MORSL). GaP then generates an internal simulation environment to rehearse task instances with different graphs in parallel to iteratively refine the graph structure and parameters to improve success rates and throughput. Evaluation with 8 new open VA task benchmarks, 4 in-simulation and 4 in real-world, suggests that GaP can achieve success rates that significantly outperform baselines. Details, code, and data can be found online: https://graph-robots.github.io/gap

</details>


### [93] [Cortex: A Bidirectionally Aligned Embodied Agent Framework for Long-horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05377)
*Jiaqi Peng,Xiqian Yu,Delin Feng,Yuqiang Yang,Wenzhe Cai,Jing Xiong,Ganlin Yang,Jinliang Zheng,Jiafei Cao,Xueyuan Wei,Jiangmiao Pang,Yuan Shen,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Cortex框架，通过将高层VLM的规划语义与低层VLA的执行运动学双向对齐，并标准化为32个规范技能原语，解决了长时程操作任务中分层双系统方法的高层规划与低层执行之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型因马尔可夫性质难以处理长时程任务；分层双系统方法存在高层规划语义与低层执行运动学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 将操作子任务标准化为32个规范技能原语；2) 注入可执行性原则（代表性对象属性、改进轨迹可达性）到数据生成管线；3) 自动标注4000+小时开源视频数据并生成30小时仿真数据；4) 设计事件平衡采样策略构建训练数据；5) 推理时通过任务上下文到技能约束的精巧工程处理规划歧义。

Result: 在Libero-long上超越单块基线3.1%，在RoboTwin上超越4.1%；零样本完成未见过的真实世界长时程任务（如多阶段化学实验）。

Conclusion: Cortex通过双向对齐的高层VLM与低层VLA框架，有效弥合了规划语义与执行运动学之间的鸿沟，实现了长时程操作任务的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: While recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show promise toward generalist manipulation policies, they struggle with long-horizon tasks due to their Markovian nature-relying solely on current observations. Hierarchical dual-system methods address this but suffer from a gap between high-level planning semantics and low-level execution kinematics. We introduce Cortex, a bidirectionally aligned embodied agent framework with a customized planning interface that conveys executable and tractable subtask plans from high-level VLM to low-level VLA. Specifically, we standardize manipulation subtasks into 32 canonical skill primitives and inject tractability principles, such as representative object attributes and improved trajectory reachability, into the data generation pipeline. This enables automatic annotation of over 4k hours of open-source video data and generation of 30 hours of simulation data. We further devise an event-balanced sampling strategy to construct training data for fine-tuning the framework to better handle planning ambiguity during subtask transitions, enhanced by carefully designed harness engineering from task contexts to skill constraints during inference. Both open-loop VLM and closed-loop system evaluations demonstrate Cortex's efficacy, e.g., it outperforms monolithic baselines by 3.1% on Libero-long and 4.1% on RoboTwin. Notably, Cortex's generalist VLM enables zero-shot completion of unseen real-world long-horizon tasks, such as multi-stage chemistry experiments, by simply combining with a fine-tuned VLA-a capability infeasible through VLA fine-tuning alone.

</details>


### [94] [Deform360: A Massive Multi-view Visuotactile Dataset for Deformable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05390)
*Hongyu Li,Wanjia Fu,Xiaoyan Cong,Zekun Li,Binghao Huang,Hanxiao Jiang,Xintong He,Yiqing Liang,Rao Fu,Tao Lu,Srinath Sridhar,Kevin A. Smith,George Konidaris,Yunzhu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Deform360是一个大规模视觉-触觉数据集，包含198个日常物品、1,980个交互序列和超过215小时的观测数据，用于系统评估2D视频模型与3D粒子模型在可变形物体动态预测中的表现，揭示了结构先验与可扩展性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体的动态预测（世界建模）是机器人操作中的基础性挑战，由于高维状态空间和复杂材料特性，这一问题尤为困难。现有世界模型分为2D像素空间和3D几何空间两种范式，但由于缺乏多样化的大规模真实数据，它们的相对优势和局限性尚未明确。

Method: 构建Deform360大规模视觉-触觉数据集，包含198个日常物品、1,980个交互序列、超过215小时观测数据，采用41个环绕摄像头和双手触觉抓取器捕捉全局运动和局部接触变形。提出无标记视觉-触觉3D追踪流水线提取密集几何与运动，并系统评估当前最先进的2D视频模型与3D粒子模型。

Result: 揭示了2D视频模型与3D粒子模型在可变形物体动态预测中的权衡关系，即结构先验与可扩展性之间的优劣。初步展示了数据集在真实机器人规划任务上的适用性。

Conclusion: 为可变形物体中心的世界建模研究提供了坚实的基准，揭示了不同范式的关键权衡，为未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Predicting object dynamics (i.e., world modeling) is a fundamental challenge for robotic manipulation, and modeling deformable objects presents a particularly difficult case due to their high-dimensional state spaces and complex material properties. While current world models approach this through two distinct paradigms: learning the dynamics over the 2D pixel space or more explicit 3D geometric space. A systematic understanding of their relative strengths and limitations remains elusive due to the lack of diverse, large-scale real-world data. To address this, we present Deform360, a large-scale visuotactile dataset featuring 198 daily-life objects, 1,980 interaction sequences, and over 215 hours of observations from 41 surround-view cameras and bimanual tactile grippers to capture both global motion and contact-induced local deformations. Leveraging a novel markerless visuotactile 3D tracking pipeline to extract dense geometry and motion, we systematically evaluate current state-of-the-art world models, comparing 2D video models against 3D particle models. Finally, we provide a preliminary demonstration indicating the real-world applicability of our dataset by performing robot planning tasks on deformable objects. Our analysis reveals key insights into the trade-offs between structural priors and scalability, providing a solid benchmark for future research in generalizable deformable object-centric world modeling. Project website: https://deform360.lhy.xyz

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [Physics-Informed Dynamic State Estimation for Current Transformers Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02679)
*Michael A. Boateng,Gabriel Gauderman,Nathalie Uwamahoro*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理信息增强的电流互感器动态状态估计方法，利用COMTRADE测量数据和图神经网络从Jacobian稀疏模式中生成物理信息初始状态估计，实现冷启动到热启动的改进，在初始化距离和加权目标值上分别获得平均25%和38%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态状态估计方法依赖数值离散化和迭代求解器，其初始化未利用估计问题的物理依赖结构，导致在噪声条件下鲁棒性不足。

Method: 基于WinIGS-T生成的COMTRADE测量数据，对四种离散化方案和三种迭代求解器进行结构化基准测试，确定Gauss-Newton结合Quadratic离散化为最优基线；利用Jacobian稀疏模式构建图神经网络，生成物理信息初始状态估计，实现热启动策略。

Result: 热启动策略改善了估计器条件数，在所有测试信噪比水平下，初始化距离平均提升25%，初始加权目标值平均提升38%。

Conclusion: 将物理结构嵌入初始化阶段可改善CT饱和校正的鲁棒性，支持现代电力系统更可靠的测量和保护性能。

Abstract: Current transformers are fundamental to power system protection and measurement, yet transient core saturation can severely distort the secondary current and degrade measurement accuracy. Existing dynamic state estimation methods rely mainly on numerical discretisation and iterative solvers, but their initialisation is not informed by the physical dependency structure of the estimation problem, which limits robustness under noisy conditions. This paper presents a physics-informed enhancement for current transformer dynamic state estimation using COMTRADE measurements generated in WinIGS-T. A structured benchmark of four discretisation schemes and three iterative solvers identifies Gauss-Newton with Quadratic discretisation as the strongest baseline. To address the limitation of conventional cold-start initialisation, a graph neural network is constructed from the Jacobian sparsity pattern to generate physics-informed initial state estimates. The proposed warm-start strategy improves estimator conditioning and achieves average gains of 25% in initialisation distance and 38% in initial weighted objective value across all tested SNR levels. The results demonstrate that embedding physical structure into the initialisation stage improves the robustness of CT saturation correction and supports more reliable measurement and protection performance in modern power grids.

</details>


### [96] [RCOA Extension and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02797)
*Ricardo Tapia,Iman Soltani*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文扩展了松弛凸障碍物规避（RCOA）方法至三维环境，并将其应用于无人机导航。RCOA通过凸优化实现障碍物规避，即使在预测时域之外也能保持避障有效性。该方法还从质点模型扩展至考虑飞行器几何形状的三维物体碰撞规避。仿真结果表明RCOA计算性能达到或超越现有方法，且能实现非线性模型预测控制器在超过30Hz频率下的实时可行激进机动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有障碍物规避方法通常需要非凸优化或较长的预测时域，计算负担大，难以满足实时性要求。RCOA作为首个实现完全凸优化的避障方法，具有独特的分析框架，但其原工作限于二维且采用质点假设。本文旨在将RCOA扩展至三维环境并考虑实际飞行器几何形状，以提升无人机导航的实时可行性与避障能力。

Method: 将RCOA公式扩展至三维环境；引入飞行器几何形状模型（超越质点假设），实现三维物体间的碰撞规避；采用非线性模型预测控制器（NMPC）框架；通过数值仿真与现有方法进行对比验证。

Result: RCOA在三维环境下的计算性能达到或超越现有方法；RCOA使NMPC能够以超过30Hz的频率执行激进机动（如穿越狭窄通道），且预测时域可以缩短。

Conclusion: RCOA成功扩展至三维并支持实际飞行器几何形状，在无人机导航中兼顾了计算效率与避障有效性，尤其适用于需要高频实时控制与激进机动的场景，具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: The Relaxed Convex Obstacle Avoidance (RCOA) formulation is the first approach to enable a fully convex optimal control problem (OCP) for obstacle avoidance. Convergence analysis of RCOA yields an analytical framework that defines a unique characteristic: the ability to maintain obstacle avoidance (OA) efficacy even when obstacles reside beyond the controller's prediction horizon. In this paper, RCOA is extended to three-dimensional environments and apply it to Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) navigation. Furthermore, the formulation is enhanced to incorporate vehicle geometries, moving beyond point-mass representations to enable collision avoidance between 3D objects. Numerical simulations demonstrate that RCOA provides computational performance on par or exceeding state-of-the-art methods. Notably, RCOA is demonstrated to enable a Nonlinear Model Predictive Controller (NMPC) to execute aggressive maneuvers through narrow passages with reduced prediction horizons, ensuring real-time feasibility at frequencies exceeding 30~Hz.

</details>


### [97] [Overload-Based Cascades in Multiplex Flow Networks with Partial Functionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02844)
*Orkun İrsoy,Osman Yağan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究部分功能性下的多路复用流网络级联故障，提出跨层影响因子的模型，通过平均场分析推导递归方程，发现非单调鲁棒性曲线和单层存活相，并提出一种结合跨层影响与局部邻域信息的容量分配策略以最大化鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流网络模型假设节点要么完全工作要么完全失效，但许多真实系统中节点支持多种不同流，一种功能的失效并不必然导致其他功能失效，因此需要研究部分功能性下的级联故障建模。

Method: 采用多路复用流网络与部分功能性建模，引入跨层影响因子量化层间资源竞争；利用平均场分析推导级联停止后各层存活节点比例的递归方程；通过模拟验证多种负载-容量分布；比较多种容量分配策略以最大化鲁棒性。

Result: 验证了平均场分析的准确性；发现了非单调鲁棒性曲线和不同的级联结果相（包括联合功能性模型中不存在的单层存活相）；部分功能性相比联合功能性可提高鲁棒性；提出的结合跨层影响与局部邻域信息的容量分配策略在所有配置中表现最佳。

Conclusion: 部分功能性的多路复用流网络模型能更真实地反映实际系统的级联故障行为，跨层影响因子的调控对系统鲁棒性有显著影响，且合理的容量分配策略可有效提升系统生存能力。

Abstract: Cascading failures driven by load or flow redistribution arise in networked systems such as power grids, supply chains, and cloud computing centers. Most flow-network models assume that a node either functions or fails as a whole. In many real systems, however, a node supports several distinct flows that share node-level resources, and failure in one of them does not necessarily imply failure in the others. We study this setting through multiplex flow networks with partial functionality, where a node can remain operational in some functionalities while failing in others. A heavy load on one functionality reduces the capacity available to the others, as quantified by cross-layer influence factors. When a node fails in one layer, its load is redistributed among surviving nodes in that layer, while the node may continue to operate in the others. Using mean-field analysis, we derive recursive equations for the final system sizes, namely the fraction of surviving nodes in each layer after the cascade stops. We validate the analysis through simulations for several load-capacity distributions. We then examine key features of the cascade dynamics, including non-monotone robustness curves, different cascade-outcome regimes, and their relation with cross-layer influence. We map the outcomes to distinct steady-state regimes, including single-layer survival phases absent in joint-functionality models, and show that partial functionality can increase robustness relative to the joint-functionality case. Finally, we study robustness maximization under a fixed total capacity budget by comparing several capacity allocation strategies. We propose a strategy that combines cross-layer influence with local neighborhood information on load and degree, and show that it gives the strongest robustness performance across the configurations considered.

</details>


### [98] [Data-driven Kernel-based Predictive Control with Stability and Robustness Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02851)
*Wenjie Liu,Yifei Li,Gang Wang,Lihua Xie*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对基于数据驱动核方法的预测控制（DDKPC）方案进行了闭环性质的理论分析，证明了无噪声情况下递归可行性和闭环稳定性，并通过松弛优化和ALD在线字典更新将其扩展至噪声环境及慢时变非线性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动预测控制方法多聚焦于线性系统，针对非线性系统的理论保证（如递归可行性、闭环稳定性）尚不充分。本文旨在为基于核方法的非线性数据驱动预测控制提供严格的理论分析框架。

Method: 1) 利用表示定理构建核方法多步预测器，隐式捕捉系统非线性行为；2) 在无噪声情形下，通过足够长的预测 horizon 和足够小的核表示误差保证递归可行性与稳定性；3) 引入惩罚松弛处理非凸隐式约束以降低计算负担；4) 将表示误差与有界噪声聚合为统一不确定性界实现鲁棒化；5) 针对慢时变非线性系统，采用近似线性依赖（ALD）准则管理固定预算在线字典，周期性重构核预测器。

Result: 理论证明了无噪声情形下DDKPC方案的递归可行性和闭环稳定性；针对噪声和时变系统，给出了保持递归可行性和实际闭环稳定性的充分条件；数值算例验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: DDKPC框架在合适的预测horizon和表示误差条件下具备严格的理论保障，惩罚松弛使其更适于实时实现，且能推广至噪声环境和慢时变非线性系统，拓展了数据驱动预测控制的理论边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a theoretical analysis of the closed-loop properties of a data-driven kernel-based predictive control (DDKPC) scheme developed solely from input-output data. The proposed formulation integrates a robust data-driven predictive control framework with a multi-step predictor for nonlinear systems constructed via kernel-based methods. This predictor implicitly captures the system's nonlinear behavior using the representer theorem. For the nominal case with noise-free data, we prove that the DDKPC scheme guarantees recursive feasibility and closed-loop stability, provided that the prediction horizon is sufficiently long and the kernel representation error is sufficiently small. To facilitate real-time implementation, we introduce a penalty relaxation formulation to alleviate the computational burden inherently caused by nonconvex implicit constraints. Furthermore, the framework is robustified against measurement noise by aggregating the representation mismatch and the bounded noise into a unified uncertainty bound. Finally, we extend the DDKPC framework to slowly time-varying nonlinear systems by periodically reconstructing the kernel predictor from a fixed-budget online dictionary managed by the approximate linear dependency (ALD) criterion. Under suitable conditions on the rate of variation of the input-output evolution and the online prediction error, recursive feasibility and practical closed-loop stability are preserved. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is illustrated through numerical examples.

</details>


### [99] [Exact Closed-Form Feedforward Inversion for Dual-Bridge Series Resonant DC/DC Converter via State-Plane Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02948)
*Alex Borisevich*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对双桥串联谐振变换器（DB SRC），利用状态平面轨迹分析推导了精确的闭式前馈逆映射模型。该模型在谐振频率处与一阶谐波近似（FHA）代数等价，但在超谐振运行时消除了FHA前馈中5-72%的换向角误差，且所有结果以适合实时实现的闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHA前馈模型在超谐振运行时存在显著换向角误差（5-72%），需要推导更精确的频率相关的逆映射模型以提升控制精度。

Method: 采用状态平面轨迹分析，推导DB SRC在四个调制变量（占空比d、副边短路时间s、相移β、开关频率ω）下的精确闭式前馈逆映射，并与FHA模型进行代数对比验证。

Result: 在谐振频率处，状态平面模型与FHA代数等价；在超谐振运行时，消除了FHA前馈中5-72%的换向角误差。控制器架构保持与FHA设计类似的并联非线性补偿结构，但前馈映射基于谐振时间量，自然耦合了换向与频率控制。

Conclusion: 所提精确闭式前馈逆映射模型在保持与FHA兼容架构的同时，显著提升了超谐振工况下的前馈精度，且适用于实时实现。

Abstract: This paper derives exact closed-form feedforward inversion maps for the dual-bridge series resonant converter (DB SRC) using state-plane trajectory analysis. The converter employs four modulation variables: primary duty cycle $d$, secondary shorting time $s$, phase shift $β$, and switching frequency $ω$. While the established first harmonic approximation (FHA) provides frequency-independent inversion, the exact state-plane approach yields frequency-dependent inversion model that is proven algebraically identical to FHA at resonance frequency. For practical above-resonance operation, the exact inversions eliminate the 5--72\% commutation angle errors inherent in the FHA-based feedforward. The resulting controller architecture mirrors the parallel nonlinear compensation structure of the FHA-based design, with feedforward maps now operating on resonant-time quantities that naturally couple commutation and frequency control. All results are expressed in closed form suitable for real-time implementation.

</details>


### [100] [Physics-Informed Neural State-Space Modeling of Battery-Electric Vehicle Dynamics for Closed-Loop Automated Parking Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03000)
*Sirong Pan,Guannan Tian,Pan Song*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向量产电动车低速泊车工况的物理信息神经状态空间模型，仅用16次场地测试数据即可高精度复现包含执行器延迟、蠕行、制动保持等复杂动态行为，并成功嵌入交互式模拟器用于自动驾驶泊车栈的虚拟预校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动学模型在低速泊车时忽略了执行器延迟、传动系统蠕行、制动保持穿越零速以及频繁换向等关键动态特性，而现有数据驱动方法又依赖庞大的训练数据且缺乏物理可解释性；同时，信号层面的执行器建模 fidelity 并不保证在闭环系统中的有用性。

Method: 采用物理信息神经状态空间模型：1) 引入齿轮条件速度约束；2) 将横摆角速度建模为运动学自行车模型先验上的学习残差；3) 对驱动、制动和转向执行器分别建立专用子模型，且在制动建模中采用基于速度后果而非信号本身的调优策略。

Result: 模型在完全开环仿真中泛化到未见过的机动工况；基于仅16次场地测试数据，在ISO/TS 18571客观评级指标下获得"良好"等级；作为交互式模拟器的实时被控对象，能支持完整的规划堆栈通过学到的动力学完成泊车操作。

Conclusion: 该模型在无需制造商专有底盘和执行器参数的情况下，适用于自动驾驶泊车规划与控制栈的虚拟开发阶段预校准，且证明了基于速度后果而非信号 fidelity 的制动调优策略能逆转信号层面的错误判断。

Abstract: This paper contributes to vehicle dynamics modeling by introducing a physics-informed neural state-space model tailored for the parking regime of a production battery-electric sedan, identified entirely from field-test maneuvers. At parking speeds the model captures what the kinematic idealization omits, including actuator lag, drivetrain creep, brake-hold transitions through standstill, and frequent reversals of the motion direction. A gear-conditioned velocity constraint is imposed during training, and the yaw rate is read out as a learned residual on a kinematic-bicycle prior, so that the network devotes its capacity to the deviation from physics rather than to its reproduction. These training-time physics make the customary inference-time state limiter redundant. The commanded-to-actual behavior of the drive, brake, and steering actuators is reproduced by dedicated submodels, for which signal fidelity proves an unreliable proxy for closed-loop value; tuning the brake on its velocity consequence rather than on its own signal reverses the verdict reached at the signal level. The model generalizes to held-out maneuvers in fully open-loop simulation, and, despite being identified from only 16 field tests, the assembled command-to-vehicle chain earns Good ratings on the vehicle states under the ISO/TS 18571 objective rating metric. Embedded as the real-time plant of an interactive simulator, it enables a production-representative planning stack to park the vehicle through the learned dynamics. This makes the model suitable for pre-calibrating an automated-parking planning and control stack in the virtual development phase without the manufacturer's proprietary chassis and actuator parameters.

</details>


### [101] [Integrating Power Electronics-based Energy Storages to Power Systems: A Review on Dynamic Modeling, Analysis, and Future Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03098)
*Qiang Fu,Changlong Dai,Siqi Bu,C. Y. Chung*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于电力电子的储能系统（PEESs）接入交直流电力系统的动态分析，指出简化模型在大规模PEESs中可能导致“误差聚合”，传统稳定性分析方法对分布式PEESs失效并提出了“动态重构”分析思路，同时强调PEESs带来的失稳风险与可再生能源并网不同，需要更多关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PEESs接入电力系统可能引发不稳定问题，但现有研究在动态建模、分析方法和失稳风险方面存在不足，亟需系统综述以明确研究现状与未来方向。

Method: 对交直流电力系统中PEESs的动态建模、分析方法和潜在失稳风险进行综述性研究，基于已有文献归纳三大结论并指出挑战。

Result: 1) 简化模型在大规模PEESs中会因误差聚合导致结果错误；2) 传统稳定性方法适用于单机及串并联大规模PEESs，但对分布式连接不足，提出“动态重构”分析思路；3) PEESs的失稳风险因功能控制和双向功率流而与可再生能源并网不同，但缺乏全面研究。

Conclusion: 总结PEESs稳定运行面临的重大挑战，为未来研究提供启示。

Abstract: The integration of power electronics-based energy storage systems (PEESs) into power systems introduces potential instabilities. This study reviews efforts in dynamic analysis of both AC and DC power systems integrated with PEESs, covering dynamic modeling, analysis methods, and potential instability risks. Major conclusions are drawn as: 1) Simplified models of PEESs have been widely used for dynamic analysis of power systems. However, it may cause "error aggregation" as the scale of PEESs increases, leading to mistakes in results, which induces significant concerns. 2) Traditional stability mechanism analysis methods remain effective for single grid-connected PEES and large-scale PEESs with parallel and series connections. However, they are inadequate for PEESs with distributed connections. To fill in this gap, an idea of mechanism analysis based on "dynamic reconstruction" is proposed. 3) Potential instability risks caused by PEESs integration may differ from those caused by renewable energy integration due to differences in functional controls and bidirectional power flow. However, comprehensive investigations in this regard are lacking and require significant attention. To ensure the stable operation of power systems with increasing integration of PEESs, significant challenges are summarized in the end, providing inspirations for future studies.

</details>


### [102] [Damping Analysis of Subsynchronous Oscillations at the Sending-End of Practical Grid-Forming MTDC Power System for Isolated Renewable Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03104)
*Qiang Fu,Siqi Bu,Xiao Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对张北构网型多端直流输电系统送端出现的次同步振荡问题，提出了一种自-耦合阻尼分析方法，量化了不同构网控制环路的固有动态及外部动态耦合对SSO的影响，并基于阻尼灵敏度分析识别关键影响因素，最后提出了抑制策略并通过电磁暂态仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张北构网型多端直流输电系统送端出现了次同步振荡，对此进行详细报告并为GFM控制的广泛应用提供警示。

Method: 提出自-耦合阻尼方法，综合考虑功率同步控制、d/q轴交流电压控制和内环电流控制环路，量化固有动态与外部动态耦合对SSO的影响，并通过阻尼灵敏度分析识别关键影响因素。

Result: q轴AVC参数比d轴AVC参数更能改善自阻尼，PSC参数可降低负耦合阻尼；基于此提出了SSO抑制策略，并在Simulink电磁暂态平台上建立了镜像张北工程模型验证了结论的准确性。

Conclusion: SSO的主要成因可归因于GFM固有动态或外部动态耦合，通过调节特定控制参数可实现有效抑制。

Abstract: Subsynchronous oscillations (SSOs) have occurred at the sending-end of Zhangbei grid-forming MTDC power system (SE-GFPS). This paper gives an alert for the wide application of grid-forming (GFM) control by presenting a detailed report on such SSOs and the practical GFM configuration. Different from traditional studies that consider only parts of GFM control loops for simplification, power synchronous control (PSC), d- and q-axis AC voltage control (AVC), and inner current control (ICC) loops are comprehensively involved in this study. A self- and coupling-damping method is proposed to quantify the impact of both the inherent dynamics of different GFM control loops and the external dynamic coupling between the GFM control and the remaining SE-GFPS on the SSOs. It determines whether the major causes of SSOs are attributed to inherent GFM dynamics or external dynamic couplings. Based on the damping sensitivity analysis, the major impact factors of the SSOs are identified. Self-damping can be improved more by q-axis than by d-axis AVC parameters, and negative coupling damping can be reduced by PSC parameters. Finally, SSO mitigation strategies are proposed, and an SE-GFPS mirroring the real-world Zhangbei project is established on the electromagnetic transient (EMT) platform in Simulink, validating the accuracy of our conclusions.

</details>


### [103] [Sparse State Feedback Control for Industrial Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03159)
*Alba Gurpegui Ramón,Frida Norlund,Kristian Soltesz,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于优化的稀疏状态反馈控制器设计方法，并将其应用于Aitik矿的工业粗选浮选槽液位控制。与现有稠密LQ控制器相比，该方法强制施加与浮选槽交互结构一致的稀疏模式，在调优时考虑最坏情况入流扰动，并通过IAE指标优化性能。非零增益通过坐标搜索算法优化，保持闭环稳定性。稀疏控制器在扰动抑制方面优于LQ控制器，且增益矩阵更易调整和解释，适合工业部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业浮选槽使用的稠密LQ控制器增益不易调整和解释，且工业应用需要更结构化的控制器设计方法。本文旨在设计一种与浮选槽交互结构一致的稀疏控制器，在保持性能的同时提高可解释性和可调整性。

Method: 采用优化方法设计稀疏状态反馈控制器：强制增益矩阵的稀疏模式与浮选槽交互结构一致；考虑最坏情况入流扰动进行调优；以IAE指标优化性能；使用带边界约束的坐标搜索算法优化非零增益，并保持闭环稳定性。

Result: 稀疏控制器在浮选槽的负载扰动抑制方面优于现有LQ控制器；在线性和非线性仿真中均一致观察到性能改进；稀疏增益矩阵更易调整和解释；生成的控制器适合直接工业部署。

Conclusion: 所提出的稀疏控制框架为工业应用提供了稠密LQ设计的有效替代方案，在保持甚至提升控制性能的同时，增强了控制器的可解释性和可调整性，具有直接的工业实用性。

Abstract: We present an optimization-based methodology for designing sparse state-feedback controllers for industrial applications that are suited for linear control, and demonstrate the framework by designing a level controller for an industrial rougher flotation bank at the Aitik mine. In contrast to the dense linear-quadratic (LQ) controller gains currently operating at the concentrator, our approach enforces a sparsity pattern that is consistent with the interaction structure of the flotation bank and accounts for the worst-case expected inflow disturbances during tuning, while optimizing controller performance through the Integral Absolute Error (IAE) index. The non-zero elements of the sparse gain matrices are optimized using a coordinate search algorithm that handles bound constraints and preserves closed-loop stability. The resulting sparse controller achieves improved load disturbance rejection in the flotation cells compared to the LQ controller. These improvements are consistently observed in both linear and nonlinear simulations. In addition, the imposed structure, results in gain matrices that are easier to adjust and interpret. Importantly, the sparse controllers generated for the Aitik mine are directly suitable for industrial deployment and offer an effective alternative to the existing dense LQ design.

</details>


### [104] [Multimodal Nonblocking Supervisory Control Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03263)
*Marijn Minkenberg,Michel Reniers,Martijn Goorden,Asia van de Mortel-Fronczak,Wan Fokkink*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对包含故障行为、重构或多控制目标的系统模型，提出模态非阻塞和多模态非阻塞两种新的非阻塞变体，通过限制通往标记状态路径上的状态访问，提供了更合适的非阻塞性质，并给出了相应的综合算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非阻塞性质在系统模型包含故障行为、重构或多个控制目标时表现较弱，无法充分捕捉实际系统所需的活性性质，因此需要定义更合适的非阻塞变体。

Method: 定义了模态非阻塞和多模态非阻塞两种变体，通过限制通向标记状态路径上的状态访问来加强非阻塞性质；提出了相应的综合算法来构建模态和多模态非阻塞监督控制器；通过三个直观示例进行说明，并与已有非阻塞变体进行对比分析。

Result: 通过示例验证了新的非阻塞变体与已有变体是不同的；在可能的情况下，给出了一个变体蕴含另一个变体的条件。

Conclusion: 模态和多模态非阻塞为含故障、重构或多目标的系统提供了更合适的非阻塞性质，拓展了监督控制综合的应用范围。

Abstract: Supervisory control synthesis leverages the nonblocking property to show liveness of the supervised system. This property is particularly weak when system models include fault behavior, reconfiguration, or multiple control goals. To capture a more suitable nonblocking property for such system models, this paper introduces modal and multimodal nonblocking. These novel nonblocking variants impose a restriction on the states visited on the path towards a marked state. Synthesis algorithms are presented to construct modal and multimodal nonblocking supervisors. The novel nonblocking variants are illustrated with three intuitive examples, inspired by real synthesis problems encountered while applying supervisory control synthesis to safety-critical water infrastructures. A comparison is made between the novel nonblocking variants and established nonblocking variants to show that they are distinct. Additionally, where possible, conditions are formulated under which one variant implies the other.

</details>


### [105] [Hierarchical Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Carbon-Aware AI Data Centers in Power Distribution Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03324)
*Hyunsoo Lee,Panggah Prabawa,Dae-Hyun Choi,Joongheon Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层碳感知多智能体强化学习（CA-MARL）框架，通过全局工作负载管理器与本地AIDC智能体协同，实现AI数据中心在不确定性下的低碳高效运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI应用快速扩张导致AIDC能耗激增，碳排放显著上升，亟需兼顾经济性与低碳性的鲁棒运行框架。

Method: 采用分层CA-MARL框架：全局WM智能体基于NCI信息进行空间作业分配；本地AIDC智能体执行训练任务时间迁移、GPU空间分配及冷却系统送风温度控制，智能体均使用多智能体Transformer方法训练。

Result: 在IEEE 33节点配电系统上验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提分层CA-MARL框架能够实现AIDC在不确定性下的鲁棒低碳运行，兼顾经济性与环保性。

Abstract: Eco-friendly energy management for artificial intelligence data centers (AIDCs) is crucial because of the significant increase in energy consumption-induced carbon emissions from AIDCs resulting from the rapid expansion of AI applications. This paper proposes a hierarchical carbon-aware multi-agent reinforcement learning (CA-MARL) framework for robust and efficient operations of AIDCs under uncertainties while ensuring low-carbon operation of power distribution systems. The framework comprises a workload manager (WM) agent and multiple local AIDC agents trained using a multi-agent transformer method, corresponding to a global AIDC aggregator and a local AIDC operator, respectively. Leveraging AIDC operation data along with nodal carbon intensity (NCI) calculated from the carbon emission flow-integrated distribution system operator problem, the WM agent spatially allocates AI training and inference jobs among all AIDCs. Based on the jobs allocated from the WM agent and NCI information, each AIDC agent schedules economical and eco-friendly operations of the AIDC by performing the following tasks: i) temporal shifting of training jobs, ii) spatial allocation of training graphics processing unit (GPU) blocks and inference GPUs within the AIDC, and iii) control of the supply air temperature of the cooling system. The effectiveness of the proposed framework was assessed using an IEEE 33-node power distribution system.

</details>


### [106] [Direct Data Driven Natural Gradient Descent for Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03393)
*Ramin Esmzad,Farnaz Adib Yaghmaie,Bahare Kiumarsi,Hamidreza Modares*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自然梯度下降（NGD）的数据驱动控制框架，无需显式模型辨识即可设计可解释且鲁棒的闭环策略。通过Fisher信息矩阵引入闭环协方差矩阵进行梯度预条件处理，并利用两种数据参数化方法实现稳定性保证的策略综合。在仿真和ROSbot XL硬件平台上验证了该方法在收敛速度、鲁棒性和控制可解释性方面优于LQR等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制方法通常需要显式模型辨识或缺乏对系统内在不确定性的有效利用，导致控制策略的可解释性和鲁棒性不足。本文旨在弥合轨迹导向的自然梯度方法与实际数据驱动控制设计之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于自然梯度下降（NGD）的闭环策略设计；通过Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）引入闭环协方差矩阵进行梯度预条件；利用两种数据参数化方法表示闭环系统；使用半定规划（SDP）提供收缩和收敛的理论保证。

Result: 在仿真和ROSbot XL硬件平台上验证了该方法；与LQR和标准数据驱动基线相比，在收敛速度、鲁棒性和控制可解释性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将自然梯度方法应用于数据驱动控制设计，实现了无需模型辨识的可解释、鲁棒的闭环策略综合，具有理论保证和实际硬件验证。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel direct data-driven control framework based on Natural Gradient Descent (NGD) to design interpretable and robust closed-loop policies without requiring explicit model identification.
