We propose two data-driven NGD formulations that incorporate the closed-loop covariance matrix through the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM), allowing gradient updates to be preconditioned according to the system's intrinsic uncertainty. Leveraging two distinct data-based parameterizations of the closed-loop system, our method enables stability-guaranteed policy synthesis directly from data. We provide theoretical guarantees for contraction and convergence using semidefinite programs (SDPs) and validate our framework in both simulations and on hardware on a ROSbot XL platform. The results demonstrate intuitive features compared to linear-quadratic regulator (LQR) and standard data-driven baselines, particularly in terms of convergence speed, robustness, and control interpretability. This work bridges the gap between trajectory-oriented natural gradient methods and practical data-driven control design.

</details>


### [107] [An Efficient Non-Gaussian Chance Constraint Method for Stochastic Nonlinear Problems in Spaceflight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03424)
*Ethan R. Burnett,Spencer Boone,Niccolo Michelotti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非高斯分布的非线性机会约束航天器制导方法，通过将真实置信边界视为协方差预测边界的扰动，并利用高阶统计矩（偏度和峰度张量）对“香蕉形分布”进行参数化，最终应用于考虑非凸约束的随机脉冲机动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机会约束制导假设状态不确定服从高斯分布，但在地月空间或深空飞越等强非线性动力学场景中，测量间隔长、非线性强，导致不确定性传播后呈非高斯分布，现有高斯方法失效。

Method: 将真实置信等值线视为协方差预测边界的扰动，从高阶统计矩（偏度和峰度张量）推导扰动边界几何，从而对轨道力学问题中常见的“香蕉形分布”的置信边界进行参数化。

Result: 该参数化方法成功应用于随机非线性脉冲航天器机动目标问题，并特殊处理了相关的非凸约束。

Conclusion: 所提出的非高斯置信边界技术为强非线性动力学场景下的随机制导提供了可行方案，能够处理非高斯分布下的机会约束问题。

Abstract: Standard chance-constrained spacecraft guidance typically relies on the assumption that uncertainties in vehicle states obey Gaussian statistics. In frontier applications such as the cislunar environment or deep space flybys, the dynamics can be particularly nonlinear, and time between measurements can be long, leading to the need to make decisions whose outcomes produce non-Gaussian distributions. This paper demonstrates a non-Gaussian confidence boundary technique for stochastic guidance in such applications. Our approach is to consider the true confidence contour as a perturbation of the one predicted from covariance, then to derive perturbed boundary geometry from computed higher-order statistical moments. Applying this technique to so-called "banana-shaped distributions", found in orbital mechanics problems, enables a simple parameterization of the confidence contour using the skew and kurtosis tensors. This parameterization is then applied to a stochastic and nonlinear impulsive spacecraft maneuver targeting problem, with special treatment of a relevant non-convex constraint.

</details>


### [108] [Data-Driven Discovery of Multiscale Power System Oscillation Governing Equations Using SINDy-SENDAI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03485)
*Andrea Pomarico,Yuxuan Bao,Liyao Mars Gao,Salvatore Tessitore,Giorgio Maria Giannuzzi,Alberto Berizzi,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SINDy-SENDAI算法的层级多尺度框架，用于从广域测量数据中表征电力系统机电振荡的暂态动力学。该框架通过深度学习分离高低频分量，并在潜在空间利用SINDy模块辨识稀疏控制方程，在Kundur两区系统、2016伊比利亚振荡事件及2021意大利南部电网实测数据上验证，优于Hankel-DMD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统中逆变器资源（IBRs）渗透率增加引入了新的动态行为，传统方法难以有效监测和解释机电振荡，亟需一种兼具鲁棒性、可解释性和稳定性的实用化分析框架。

Method: 提出层级多尺度深度学习架构，利用SINDy-SENDAI算法分离传感器数据中的低频和高频分量，在潜在空间中嵌入稀疏非线性动力系统辨识（SINDy）模块，学习显式的动力学方程。该方法避免了传统黑箱模型，支持物理解释、稳定性评估和振荡预测。

Result: 在Kundur两区系统上通过模态分析验证了框架有效性，并在2016伊比利亚振荡事件和2021意大利南部电网实测数据上持续优于Hankel-DMD方法，潜在动力学足以准确重构和预测全系统行为。

Conclusion: SINDy-SENDAI框架满足实际部署所需的鲁棒性、可解释性和稳定性能，为高IBR渗透率电力系统机电振荡监测提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Monitoring electromechanical oscillations is crucial for maintaining the stability of modern power systems, particularly in the presence of increasing penetrations of inverter-based resources (IBRs), which introduce new dynamic behaviors. In this work, we propose a hierarchical multiscale framework based on the SINDy-SENDAI algorithm to characterize the transient dynamics captured by wide-area measurements. The proposed deep learning architecture robustly separates low- and high-frequency components embedded in sensor data and incorporates a Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamical Systems (SINDy) module in the latent space to identify parsimonious governing equations. In contrast to conventional deep learning approaches that often produce black-box models with limited interpretability, the proposed framework learns an explicit dynamical representation, enabling physical interpretation, stability assessment, and forecasting of electromechanical oscillations. Given the societal importance of modern power systems, the proposed approach is specifically designed to satisfy key requirements for practical deployment, namely robustness, interpretability, and stable performance under diverse operating conditions. The framework is first validated on the two-area Kundur test system using conventional modal analysis as ground truth and subsequently demonstrated on two real-world datasets: the 2016 Iberian oscillatory event and the 2021 ambient measurements from the southern Italian power grid. The results show that SINDy-SENDAI consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art Hankel-DMD method and that the learned latent dynamics are sufficiently informative to accurately reconstruct and predict the behavior of the full system in the original state space.

</details>


### [109] [Interception-Driven Inverse Reachability for Engagement Zone Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03554)
*Grant Stagg,Cameron K. Peterson,Alexander Von Moll,Isaac Weintraub*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于拦截驱动的逆可达性框架，通过观测到的拦截事件推断对抗性追击者的可行发射区域，并分别从确定性和概率性角度进行安全分析与风险规划，同时引入信息驱动的牺牲Agent轨迹规划器以加速定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗环境中，自主车辆可能面临发射位置未知的追击者，传统方法难以快速推断追击者来源，从而限制了安全路径规划能力。

Method: 利用拦截事件对未知发射位置施加几何约束，求交得到有界可行区域；映射至追击者可达域得到确定性安全区；引入概率扩展建模发射位置不确定性，生成分级风险场；设计信息驱动的牺牲Agent轨迹规划器最大化可行区域期望收缩。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明该框架能快速降低发射位置不确定性，少量牺牲部署即可大幅缩短安全轨迹长度。

Conclusion: 该框架通过逆可达性推理结合信息驱动规划，有效解决了对抗环境下未知追击者来源的推断与安全规划问题。

Abstract: In contested environments, autonomous vehicles may need to plan around adversarial pursuers whose launch locations are unknown. This paper presents an interception-driven inverse-reachability framework for inferring a feasible pursuer launch region directly from observed interception events for a single pursuer. Each interception induces a geometric constraint on the unknown launch location, and intersecting these constraints yields a bounded set guaranteed to contain the true origin under maximum-capability assumptions. Mapping this inferred set through the pursuer reachable region produces deterministic engagement zones with an explicit worst-case safety interpretation. A probabilistic extension models uncertainty in the pursuer launch location and yields graded engagement-risk fields for risk-aware planning. To accelerate localization, we introduce an information-driven planner for sacrificial agents that selects trajectories to maximize expected contraction of the feasible launch region. Monte Carlo simulations show that the proposed framework rapidly reduces launch-location uncertainty and enables substantially shorter safe trajectories after only a small number of sacrificial deployments.

</details>


### [110] [On Determining the Convergence Rate of an Infinite Product of Stochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03623)
*Ron Ofir,A. Stephen Morse*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究收敛集（矩阵集合）中无限乘积收敛速率的界定问题。收敛集是指每个紧子集中的所有无限矩阵乘积收敛到秩一矩阵的随机矩阵集合。作者利用次乘性半范数来界定收敛速率，证明了仅在某些收敛集中所有矩阵在同一半范数下是压缩映射，特别指出该方法不适用于正对角元且具有根图的矩阵类。此外，证明了对于每个紧收敛集和每个次乘性半范数，存在有限数k使得集合中任意k个矩阵的乘积在半范数下都是压缩映射。最后提出了若干开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知收敛集中紧子集的无限乘积以指数速率收敛，但除所有矩阵均为scrambling矩阵的情形外，对收敛速率的定量估计知之甚少。本文旨在利用次乘性半范数来界定一般收敛集（非所有矩阵均为scrambling矩阵）的收敛速率。

Method: 采用次乘性半范数作为工具来界定收敛集的收敛速率。通过分析不同收敛集（scrambling矩阵集、正对角元+根图矩阵集、Sarymsakov矩阵集、双随机矩阵集等）在半范数下的压缩性质，证明存在性和有限乘积压缩性。

Result: ①仅在某些收敛集中所有矩阵在同一半范数下为压缩映射；②次乘性半范数方法不能用于确定正对角元且具有根图的矩阵类的收敛速率；③对于每个紧收敛集和每个次乘性半范数，都存在有限数k使得k个矩阵的乘积均为半范数下的压缩映射。

Conclusion: 次乘性半范数方法对界定收敛集收敛速率有一定有效性但存在局限，并非适用于所有类型的收敛集。作者最后提出了若干未来研究方向。

Abstract: By a convergent set is meant a set of stochastic matrices where every infinite product of matrices from every compact subset converges to a rank one matrix. Well-known examples include the set of all scrambling matrices, the set of all stochastic matrices with all diagonal entries positive and a rooted graph, the set of all Sarymsakov matrices, and the set of doubly stochastic matrices with positive diagonal entries and a weakly connected graph. It is known that every infinite product from each compact set of every convergent set converges to its limit exponentially fast, but not much is known about the rate of convergence when not all matrices involved are scrambling matrices. This paper deals with bounding the rate of convergence in convergent sets using submultiplicative seminorms. It is shown that only in some convergent sets all matrices are contractions in the same seminorm, and in particular that this method cannot be used to determine the convergence rate for the class of matrices with positive diagonal entries and a rooted graph. As a second contribution, it is shown that for every compact convergent set and every submultiplicative seminorm, there is a finite number $k$ such that all products of $k$ matrices from the set are contractions in the seminorm. Finally, several open questions are posed for future research.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Robust Control Lyapunov Functions through Lipschitz Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03713)
*Shiqing Wei,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Lipschitz神经网络的鲁棒控制Lyapunov函数与镇定控制器联合学习框架，适用于受状态依赖有界加性扰动影响的非线性动力系统，并通过GPU友好的分支定界算法加速Lyapunov条件的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对存在状态依赖有界加性扰动的非线性动力系统，现有方法在联合学习鲁棒控制Lyapunov函数与镇定控制器方面存在不足，且Lyapunov条件的验证效率较低。

Method: 利用Lipschitz神经网络联合学习Lyapunov函数与状态反馈控制器；建立神经网络Hessian矩阵和三阶导数在谱范数下的显式界；提出基于高阶界的GPU友好分支定界算法加速Lyapunov条件验证。

Result: 在六个不同动力系统上的大量仿真实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够有效学习鲁棒控制Lyapunov函数与镇定控制器，且GPU友好的分支定界算法显著提升了Lyapunov条件的验证效率。

Abstract: This work presents a novel framework for learning robust control Lyapunov functions and stabilizing controllers for nonlinear dynamical systems subject to additive disturbances upper bounded by a state-dependent function. We leverage recent advances in Lipschitz neural networks to jointly learn both the Lyapunov functions and state-feedback controllers. We establish explicit bounds on the Hessian and third-order derivatives of these neural networks in the spectral norm, and introduce a GPU-friendly branch-and-bound algorithm that utilizes higher-order bounds to significantly accelerate the verification of the Lyapunov conditions. Finally, we validate the proposed approach through extensive simulations on six different dynamical systems.

</details>


### [112] [Handover-Aware Trajectory Planning for Cellular-Connected UAVs under STL Specifications and URLLC Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03781)
*Yuqi Ping,Tianhao Liang,Bofeng Zheng,Bingyan Xie,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了蜂窝连接无人机在执行任务时，在超可靠低延迟通信约束下的切换感知轨迹规划问题。通过信号时序逻辑将任务语义转化为时域轨迹需求，结合有限块长URLLC可行性保障控制链路可靠性，构建了联合优化无人机轨迹、任务满意度、基站关联和切换行为的混合整数二次约束规划模型，并采用逻辑网络流、贝塞尔参数化等方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蜂窝连接无人机在任务执行中需要同时保证通信可靠性和任务完成度，现有工作未能有效联合优化轨迹规划、基站切换和URLLC约束，导致通信中断或任务失败。

Method: 1) 采用信号时序逻辑将任务语义形式化为时域轨迹约束；2) 利用有限块长URLLC可行性建模C2链路可靠性；3) 结合逻辑网络流、贝塞尔参数化轨迹和二进制关联变量，将问题转化为混合整数二次约束规划；4) 使用标准分支定界求解器求解。

Result: 所提规划器能在相同蜂窝地图下执行不同STL任务规范并保持URLLC可行性；揭示了任务时序、切换感知关联和有限块长严格度对轨迹形状、服务余量和计算复杂度的联合影响。

Conclusion: 本文为蜂窝连接无人机提供了兼顾URLLC通信可靠性与任务时序需求的联合轨迹规划框架，有效处理了混合离散-连续优化问题，为未来无人机通信与控制一体化设计提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper investigates handover-aware trajectory planning for cellular-connected UAVs executing mission-centric tasks under ultra-reliable low-latency communication (URLLC) constraints. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal specification layer for translating mission semantics into time-bounded trajectory requirements, while finite-blocklength URLLC feasibility characterizes reliable command-and-control (C2) links with serving base stations (BSs). We formulate a joint planning problem that optimizes the UAV trajectory, STL mission satisfaction, serving-BS association, and handover behavior. To solve this mixed discrete-continuous problem, we adopt and integrate a Logic Network Flow (LNF) based STL reformulation with Bézier-parameterized motion, disk-shaped URLLC service regions, and binary association variables, so that the resulting mixed-integer quadratically constrained formulation can be solved by standard branch-and-bound solvers. Numerical simulations over a library of STL missions show that the proposed planner can execute different mission specifications under the same cellular map while maintaining URLLC feasibility. The results further reveal how mission timing, handover-aware association, and finite-blocklength stringency jointly affect trajectory shape, serving margin, and computational complexity.

</details>


### [113] [Approximate Dynamic Optimization via Deep Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03861)
*Amin Nassaji,Ilias Mitrai,Prodromos Daoutidis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出使用深度算子网络（DeepONets）和傅里叶增强型DeepONets作为替代模型，求解非线性动态优化问题，以计算最优操作输入剖面。在间歇聚合反应器案例中，傅里叶增强型DeepONet在预测误差上优于标准DeepONet和前馈神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学过程中存在大量需要求解非线性动态优化问题以计算最优操作输入剖面的场景（如间歇过程的最优温度或进料剖面），传统数值方法计算成本高，亟需高效的替代模型。

Method: 采用深度算子网络（DeepONets）和傅里叶增强型DeepONets，将函数到函数的映射学习作为替代模型。在间歇聚合反应器案例中，通过最优温度程序实现对数均和重均分子量剖面及最终转化率目标的控制。

Result: 傅里叶增强型DeepONet架构在近似动态优化问题解方面表现优异，针对不同实例的预测误差均低于标准DeepONet和前馈神经网络。

Conclusion: 傅里叶增强型DeepONet能有效作为动态优化问题的替代求解器，为化工过程中的轨迹优化提供高效、准确的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the solution of nonlinear dynamic optimization problems that compute optimal manipulated input profiles to enforce desired output profiles. Such trajectory optimization problems commonly arise in chemical process applications, for example, batch processes where optimal temperature or feeding profiles (in case of fed-batch processes) are calculated to enforce time-varying product quality profiles, tightly controlling the reaction rate or rate of heat generation. We propose deep neural operators that approximate function to function mappings as surrogates for the solution of such dynamic optimization problems. We specifically employ deep operator networks (DeepONets) and Fourier-enhanced DeepONets in a batch polymerization reactor case study for which number-average and weight-average molecular weight profiles, together with a final conversion target, are enforced through an optimal temperature program. Our results show that the Fourier-enhanced DeepONet architecture performs very well in approximating the solution of the dynamic optimization problem for different instances, achieving a lower prediction error compared to the standard DeepONet architecture and standard feedforward neural networks.

</details>


### [114] [ThermoForce: A Physics-Structured Interventional World Model for Building HVAC Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03942)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出ThermoForce，一种适用于建筑暖通空调系统模型预测控制的干预感知热世界模型。它将冻结的时序基础模型作为被动自由响应先验，并学习一个紧凑的、物理结构化的强迫响应算子来建模控制动作的因果效应。该算子仅需1-3天的控制激励数据进行辨识，能正确预测干预效果（包括符号），在BOPTEST基准测试中相比原生控制器减少33-84%的热不舒适度并同时降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序基础模型虽然能高精度预测建筑被动热轨迹（无控制干预时），但高事实精度并不保证对控制干预的正确响应。研究发现：具有最佳被动精度的观测灰箱模型会预测出错误的冷却效果符号（方向性错误），而向TSFM添加控制与天气协变量也无法修正干预响应。因此需要一种能正确建模控制干预因果效应的模型。

Method: ThermoForce将冻结的TSFM作为被动自由响应先验，学习一个紧凑的、物理结构化的强迫响应算子来建模暖通空调驱动的因果效应。该算子通过构造保证对控制输入的单调性，仅需1-3天控制激励数据即可辨识，并与自由响应组合成具有反事实推理能力的完整世界模型。

Result: 在配对EnergyPlus供暖与制冷干预实验中，ThermoForce实现了最低的干预效应误差和正确的效应符号，而协变量TSFM、观测灰箱模型和蒸馏基线均失败。嵌入BOPTEST基准的MPC中，在三个两周期窗口内相比原生控制器减少33-84%的热不舒适度，同时降低能耗，仅使用195个可训练参数、冻结的骨干网络和纯CPU计算。

Conclusion: 被动预测与强迫干预响应必须在结构上分离，模型才能具备控制就绪（control-ready）能力。ThermoForce为建筑控制领域的基础模型应用提供了新的范式框架。

Abstract: Model predictive control (MPC) of building HVAC systems needs thermal models that answer a causal question: what indoor temperature, energy use, and comfort will result if a control action is applied? Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) can forecast passive building trajectories with strong zero-shot skill, but high factual accuracy does not imply valid response to control interventions. We show that an observational grey-box model with the best passive accuracy predicts cooling effects with the wrong sign, and that adding control and weather covariates to a TSFM does not fix intervention response. We introduce ThermoForce, a control-ready interventional thermal world model that keeps a TSFM frozen as a passive free-response prior and learns a compact, physics-structured forced-response operator for the causal effect of HVAC actuation. The operator is monotone in the control input by construction, is identified from one to three days of control excitation, and composes with the free response into a counterfactual-capable world model. Across paired EnergyPlus heating and cooling interventions, ThermoForce attains the lowest intervention-effect error and correct effect sign where covariate-TSFM, observational grey-box, and distillation baselines fail. Embedded in MPC on the BOPTEST benchmark, it reduces thermal discomfort by 33--84\% relative to the native controller across three two-week windows while simultaneously reducing energy, using a frozen backbone, 195 trainable parameters, and CPU-only computation. ThermoForce reframes foundation models for building control: passive prediction and forced intervention response must be structurally separated for a model to be control-ready.

</details>


### [115] [Finite Reliability Representations: Noise-Calibrated Belief-Space Covers for Reliable Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04019)
*Hyung-Jin Yoon,Hunmin Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出有限可靠性表示（FRR）框架，通过可靠性单元覆盖信念空间，其中最优动作价值函数在容差ε内变化。该框架区分了信念表示充分性与感知、过程和执行噪声所施加的底层性能下限，并适用于有限状态POMDP、线性高斯滤波器及粒子滤波器等多种系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理感知和执行噪声基底应决定决策系统可可靠使用的信念分辨率上限，现有方法未能系统地将噪声限制与信念分辨率联系起来。

Method: 提出FRR框架：用可靠性单元覆盖信念空间，单元内Q*(b,u)变化不超过ε；分离固定观测滤波映射、预测观测律和受控信念转移核三个对象；在非线性系统中通过可达集Lipschitz模量求解，在有限状态POMDP中贝叶斯修正为归一化正线性映射。

Result: 在动作价值Lipschitz条件下，FRR覆盖支持单元常数策略，其次优性有界于2ε/(1-γ)；引入可靠性熵作为认证决策相关信念复杂度的度量。

Conclusion: FRR框架有效区分了表示充分性与噪声决定的基本性能下限，适用于有限POMDP、线性高斯滤波器、局部线性化非线性滤波器及粒子滤波器实现。

Abstract: Physical sensing and actuation noise floors should inform how much belief resolution a decision-making system can reliably use. We introduce Finite Reliability Representations (FRR), a framework for covering belief spaces by reliability cells: regions within which the optimal action-value function Q*(b,u) varies by at most a tolerance epsilon, uniformly over actions. The framework is formulated on beliefs rather than states and uses a cover rather than an equivalence quotient, because approximate decision-closeness is not transitive in general. A central technical point is that noisy Bayesian updates should not be treated as globally contractive on arbitrary beliefs. We therefore separate three objects: the fixed-observation filter map, the predictive observation law, and the controlled belief-transition kernel. For nonlinear continuous-state systems, FRR is obtained under a reachable-set Lipschitz modulus for the belief-transition kernel. For finite-state POMDPs, the same construction becomes exact on the belief simplex: prediction is linear, Bayesian correction is a normalized positive linear map, sensor noise enters through observation-distribution distinguishability, and actuation uncertainty enters through an action-execution channel. Under the corresponding action-value Lipschitz condition, an FRR cover supports a cell-constant policy whose suboptimality is bounded by 2 epsilon/(1 - gamma). We also introduce reliability entropy, the logarithm of the minimal number of reliability cells, as a measure of certified decision-relevant belief complexity. The framework distinguishes representation sufficiency from fundamental performance floors imposed by sensing, process, and actuation noise. It applies to finite POMDPs, linear-Gaussian filters, locally linearized nonlinear filters, and particle-filter implementations through analytic or empirical certification of reliability cells.

</details>


### [116] [Scenario-based Data-Enabled Predictive Control: Robustification via the Scenario Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04165)
*Sebastian Zieglmeier,Nikolas Recke,Mathias Hudoba de Badyn*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出Scenario-DeePC方法，将场景优化框架集成到数据驱动预测控制中，通过观测预测误差构建经验扰动场景，为不确定性下的约束满足提供概率保证，并在线自适应扩展适应变化的环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeePC方法在处理不确定性时缺乏严格的约束满足保证，且通常需要分布假设，违背了DeePC完全数据驱动的理念。

Method: 将场景优化框架融入DeePC，利用历史预测误差构造经验扰动场景来刻画不确定性，无需分布假设；提出自适应扩展以在线收集场景，适应时变噪声特性和模型失配。

Result: 在线性波音747模型和非线性双水箱系统上，相比标准DeePC显著减少约束违反，在标称条件下保持相当的跟踪性能，在非线性设置中提高了跟踪精度。

Conclusion: Scenario-DeePC在保持数据驱动纯粹性的同时，为不确定性下的约束满足提供了分布无关的概率保证，并具备递归可行性与自适应能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes Scenario-Based Data-Enabled Predictive Control (Scenario-DeePC), which integrates the scenario optimization framework into Data-enabled Predictive Control (DeePC) to provide probabilistic guarantees on constraint satisfaction under uncertainty. In contrast to existing methods, the uncertainty is characterized directly from data by constructing empirical disturbance scenarios from observed prediction errors, keeping the method fully consistent with the data-driven philosophy of DeePC and free of distributional assumptions. We establish the supporting theory, including a distribution-free probabilistic guarantee on constraint satisfaction and recursive feasibility of the receding-horizon scheme. An adaptive extension collects scenarios online, enabling the controller to adjust to changing noise characteristics, disturbances, and operating-point-dependent model mismatches. The approach is demonstrated on a linear Boeing 747 model and a nonlinear two-tank system, showing a significant reduction in constraint violations compared to standard DeePC, while maintaining comparable tracking performance in nominal conditions and improving tracking accuracy in the nonlinear setting.

</details>


### [117] [Towards Effcient Low Altitude Sensing: A Dual Heterogeneous Graph Learning Method for UAV Task Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04255)
*Guangyu Lei,Tianhao Liang,Bingyan Xie,Tingting Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双异构图学习的无人机任务分配方法，将有向任务图和无向无人机通信图进行结构匹配，结合图注意力网络和交叉注意力机制与PPO优化，实现更高的任务完成率和更短完成时间，并支持自然语言驱动的任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统任务分配方法将任务和无人机视为孤立实体，难以捕捉任务之间的依赖关系以及无人机之间的通信关系，限制了多无人机协同执行复杂任务的能力。

Method: 构建有向任务图（编码任务依赖与资源需求）和无向无人机通信图（编码通信关系与资源状态），将任务分配建模为图结构匹配问题；采用图注意力网络进行特征提取，结合交叉注意力机制与近端策略优化（PPO）优化任务节点与无人机节点的匹配。

Result: 在多种评估设置下，相比基准方法实现了更高的任务完成率和更短的任务完成时间；在AirSim平台上开发了无人机感知与计算应用，利用大语言模型将自然语言任务需求转化为结构化任务图，实现自主任务执行。

Conclusion: 所提出的双异构图学习方法有效解决了任务依赖和通信关系建模问题，提升了多无人机协同任务分配性能，并展示了自然语言驱动无人机任务规划的潜力。

Abstract: With the development of low altitude intelligent systems, multiple unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can collaboratively execute more complex tasks. Conventional task allocation methods usually regard tasks and UAVs as isolated entities, making it difficult to capture task dependencies and UAV communication relationships. To address this issue, this paper proposes a dual heterogeneous graph learning based UAV task allocation method. A directed task graph is constructed to represent task dependencies and encode task resource requirements, while an undirected UAV communication graph is built to model communication relationships and encode UAV resource states. The task allocation problem is formulated as a structural matching problem between the task graph and the UAV communication graph. A graph attention network based feature extraction method is introduced to learn structural representations from both graphs through message passing. A cross attention mechanism is further integrated with proximal policy optimization to optimize the matching between task nodes and UAV nodes for task allocation. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method achieves a higher task completion rate and shorter task completion time than benchmark methods under different evaluation settings. Furthermore, a UAV sensing and computing application is developed on the AirSim simulation platform. A large language model is employed to convert natural language task requirements into a structured task graph for autonomous UAV task execution, demonstrating the potential of the proposed framework for natural language driven UAV mission planning and execution.

</details>


### [118] [Certified Breathing Stability Regions in Nonlinear Dynamical Systems: Composite Lyapunov Certificates, M-Matrix Conditions, and a Resilience-Fragility Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04305)
*Marian Mester*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对慢参数漂移下互联耗散系统的暂态稳定性，建立了时间-边界裕度M的统一认证下界。通过构造复合（混合区域）李雅普诺夫函数，结合群内耦合吸收与跨群耦合比较原理，在测试矩阵为非奇异M-矩阵时得到正不变吸引域内估计。认证区域随漂移"呼吸"，其大小由临界同步刚度k_c(λ)决定。在WSCC九节点系统和惯性Kuramoto网络上验证了方法的同一性，归一化呼吸曲线可塌缩至单一主曲线，提示普适形式的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作在单机无穷大母线系统上精确建立了裕度M=CCT，但多机系统的认证下界问题尚未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，为互联耗散系统在慢参数漂移下的暂态稳定性提供统一的、有理论保证的下界估计。

Method: 采用复合（混合区域）李雅普诺夫函数：将恢复性群内耦合吸收进群能量函数，仅对残余的跨群耦合通过比较原理处理。当关联测试矩阵为非奇异M-矩阵时，得到正不变吸引域内估计。引入临界同步刚度k_c(λ)刻画认证区域的"呼吸"行为。给出非线性扇区形式构造、弹性-脆性解读以及控制推论（阻尼注入与结构作用之间的尖锐二分法）。

Result: 在WSCC九节点电力系统和惯性Kuramoto网络上进行了相同机制的验证，归一化呼吸曲线在主导阶上塌缩至单一轮廓。该数值结果被呈现为所推测普适形式成立的证据。

Conclusion: 提出了多机系统暂态稳定裕度的认证下界方法，通过复合李雅普诺夫函数和M-矩阵条件保证了吸引域的正不变性。数值实验提示可能存在一种普适的呼吸曲线形式，其严格证明（正规形证明）被明确为下一步待解决的关键问题。

Abstract: We develop a unified, certified lower bound on the time-to-boundary margin M for transient stability of interconnected dissipative systems under slow parameter drift. The companion work establishes M as the first-passage time of the joint state-parameter motion to the synchronism boundary and proves M = CCT exactly on the one-machine-infinite-bus reduction, while leaving the multimachine certified margin open. Here a composite (mixed-region) Lyapunov function, formed by absorbing the restoring intra-group coupling into group energy functions and treating only the residual cross-cut coupling through the comparison principle, yields a positively invariant inner estimate of the region of attraction whenever an associated test matrix is a nonsingular M-matrix. The certified region breathes with the drift: its size is governed by a single critical synchronising stiffness k_c (lambda), and as k_c -> 0 at the boundary the region breathes shut and the certified margin M_low <= M_true vanishes. We give a nonlinear sector form of the construction, a domain-neutral resilience-fragility reading in which the coupling that certifies order is the one whose growth certifies collapse, and a constructive control corollary establishing a sharp dichotomy between damping injection and structural action. The mechanism is demonstrated identically on the WSCC nine-bus power system and on an inertial Kuramoto network, whose normalised breathing curves collapse, to leading order, onto a single profile. We present this collapse as numerical evidence for a conjectured universal form; a normal-form proof is identified as the precise open step.

</details>


### [119] [Approximate Feedback Linearization for a Nonlinear Hyperbolic PDE Class -- Part I: Volterra Truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04361)
*Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 非线性PDE反步法生成的精确反馈线性化律（无限Volterra级数）在低阶截断后虽不再精确线性化，但仍能保持镇定能力，其关键在于高阶项在原点附近可忽略，通过限制初始条件尺寸可恢复稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性PDE的反步法反馈线性化在理论上优雅，但无限Volterra级数形式在实际实现中面临挑战，需要探索低阶截断近似是否仍能有效镇定系统。

Method: 通过分析低阶截断控制器（不再精确线性化）的稳定性，利用高阶项在原点附近可忽略的特性，建立空间上确界范数下的有限时间实际稳定性和渐近稳定性（class-$\mathcal{KL}$估计），并克服点态核界缺失和非线性闭环适定性问题。

Result: 低阶截断控制器仍具镇定能力，吸引域估计随截断阶数增大而增大，随非线性增长率增大而缩小；简单的近似已能捕捉非线性PDE反馈线性化的本质。

Conclusion: 低阶截断的非线性PDE反步法控制器在实践中有效，为简化实现提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Backstepping for nonlinear PDEs yields exact feedback linearizing laws in the form of infinite Volterra series -- elegant in theory, but with challenges for implementation. This paper shows that even very low-order truncations of such controllers, no longer exactly linearizing, retain the stabilizing power. The key insight is that higher-order terms become negligible near the origin, so stability is recovered for any fixed truncation order by restricting the initial condition size. We establish spatial sup-norm results: finite-time practical stability and asymptotic stability characterized by a class-$\mathcal{KL}$ estimate. The region-of-attraction estimate grows with the truncation order and shrinks with the growth rate of the nonlinearity. The analysis overcomes the lack of pointwise kernel bounds and resolves well-posedness of the nonlinear closed loop, showing that surprisingly simple approximations already capture the essence of nonlinear PDE feedback linearization.

</details>


### [120] [Approximate Feedback Linearization for a Nonlinear Hyperbolic PDE Class -- Part II: Neural Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04362)
*Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出用神经算子学习Volterra级数反馈线性化控制器，无需针对每个新被控对象重新求解核函数PDE，并证明了闭环系统的实际稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Volterra级数反馈线性化能线性化一类非线性双曲型PDE，但即使经过截断，控制器仍需要求解复杂的核函数PDE并计算嵌套积分，计算负担大，难以实际应用。

Method: 证明截断后的控制器对被控对象和状态联合Lipschitz连续，进而将其作为单个神经算子，以被控对象非线性和状态为输入、边界控制为输出进行学习。

Result: 训练完成后，对于训练类别内的任何被控对象，无需重新求解核函数。闭环系统具有$\mathcal{KL}$形式的实际稳定性，剩余残差球大小与训练精度成线性比例。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效消除了传统方法中针对每个新被控对象重新求解核函数的需求，同时保证了闭环稳定性，为非线性PDE控制提供了一种高效且可泛化的学习框架。

Abstract: Volterra series feedback linearizes a class of nonlinear hyperbolic PDEs but produces a controller that, even after truncation, demands solving a tower of plant-specific kernel PDEs and evaluating nested integrals. We prove the truncated controller is jointly Lipschitz in plant and state, and learn it as a single neural operator from plant nonlinearity and state to boundary control. Once trained, no kernel is ever solved again, for any plant in the trained class. The closed loop is practically stable in class-$\mathcal{KL}$ form, with a residual ball scaling linearly with training accuracy.

</details>


### [121] [An End-to-End Explainable AI Framework with Automated LLM-Based Natural Language Explanation Generation for Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04374)
*Venkata Sesha Sai Raj Nanduri,Akthar Hussain,Van-Hai Bui*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复用的端到端可解释AI框架，结合深度神经网络预测、LIME/SHAP解释生成、 fidelity/stability评估以及LLM自然语言转换，在电力系统故障检测（99%准确率，ROC-AUC=1.00）和建筑能耗预测（R²=0.67）任务上验证了其稳定性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高风险领域（如电力系统、建筑能耗）对机器学习系统的透明度、信任度和问责性要求极高，但深度神经网络等黑箱模型决策难以解释，现有XAI方法生成的技术性解释对非技术利益相关者不友好，需要一种能生成自然语言解释的端到端框架。

Method: 1）训练深度神经网络执行分类/回归任务；2）使用LIME生成局部解释、SHAP生成全局解释；3）通过fidelity（保真度）和stability（稳定性）指标评估解释质量；4）将特征重要性、预测属性等结构化信息输入LLM，生成自然语言解释文本。

Result: 电力系统故障检测：神经网络模型准确率99%，ROC-AUC 1.00；建筑能耗预测：R²分数0.67。生成的解释在保真度和稳定性上表现良好，LLM转化的自然语言解释提高了非技术人员对黑箱模型决策的理解。

Conclusion: 提出的端到端XAI框架能够生成稳定且保真的解释，并通过LLM将技术性解释转化为可理解的自然语言，有效提升了黑箱模型在高风险领域的可解释性和可访问性。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) is important for deploying machine learning systems in domains where stakes are very high and where transparency, trust and accountability are critical. Although black box models like deep neural networks often perform with high efficiency, interpreting their decisions remains as a difficult task. This paper proposes a reusable end-to-end XAI framework that is the combination of prediction, explanation generation, evaluation and converting these explanations into natural language text of explanation which can be easily understood by the non-technical stakeholders as well. This framework initially trains deep neural network for both classification and regression tasks. Local and global explanations are generated using XAI algorithms, including Local Interpretable Model-agnostic Explanation (LIME) and SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) respectively. To evaluate these explanations, we use fidelity and stability metrics to know how accurately and consistently explanations reflect the model behavior. The generated explanation includes feature importance scores, prediction specific attributes, and then transformed into a structured input to the Large Language Model (LLM), which generates a natural language explanation through which everyone can understand the explanations generated by XAI algorithms. This framework is tested on power system fault dataset detection dataset and building energy labels dataset. For fault detection, the neural network model achieved 99% accuracy with ROC-AUC score of 1.00. For building energy prediction, model achieves R2 score of 0.67. These findings say that the proposed approach produces a stable and faithful explanations while improving the interpretability of black box model to everyone with the help of LLMs.

</details>


### [122] [Real-Time Location-Aware Demand-Shaping for Power-Constrained AC Railway Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04406)
*Marton Laszlo Ambrus,Xiao Liu,Stuart Hillmansen,Zhongbei Tian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时位置感知控制器，通过选择性削减仅对约束条件起作用的列车，以最小代价恢复25kV交流铁路供电区段的电气可行性，相比传统静态限流方案将旅程时间损失降低至三十分之一，且匹配离线遗传算法最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的25kV交流铁路供电区段在降级供电模式下采用粗放的全区段功率限制，对所有列车无差别惩罚，即使有些列车并不导致约束条件越限，造成不必要的运行效率损失。

Method: 提出实时位置感知控制器：采用免求解器在线估计方法替代全功率流计算，快速评估区段可行性和每列车可用功率；基于估计值略偏乐观的特性，引入小电压裕度作为安全筛选；通过云-边缘连接的驾驶员咨询系统执行选择性削减策略，并由全多导体功率流求解器验证恢复后的可行性。

Result: 在代表性英国25kV供电走廊的故障馈电场景下，静态限流以较大旅程时间代价恢复可行性；位置感知控制器以仅三十分之一的旅程时间代价（仅建议削减一列车）恢复相同可行性，且约1.5秒内匹配离线遗传算法优化器耗时1分钟的最优解。聚合峰值需求未受影响，因主动约束为局部远场电压而非总需求。

Conclusion: 所提位置感知选择性削减控制器能够在降级供电工况下以最小时间损失恢复区段电气可行性，效果与离线全局优化相当但计算速度快得多。所有结论基于代表性供电走廊的基线对比，具体线路部署研究留待未来工作。

Abstract: Power-constrained 25kV AC railway sections, particularly under degraded feeding, are protected today by blunt, section-wide power limits that penalise every train irrespective of whether it contributes to the binding condition. This paper presents a real-time, location-aware controller that restores the electrical feasibility of a feeding section with minimal impact on the timetable: it curtails only the trains that bind, where and when they bind, evaluating feasibility and per-train available power online with a solver-free estimate as an in-loop surrogate for the full power flow. Because the estimate is accurate on average but slightly optimistic at the binding instants, the controller screens with a small voltage margin, and a full multi-conductor power-flow solver confirms the restored feasibility. The resulting selective-curtailment policy is delivered through a cloud-to-edge connected driver advisory system. On a representative GB 25kV corridor under outage feeding, solver-selected to be infeasible uncontrolled yet restorable, the controller is compared against the uncontrolled case, the incumbent static limit, and an offline genetic-algorithm optimum, with every feasibility figure solver-validated. The static limit restores feasibility at a large journey-time cost by throttling the whole section; the location-aware controller restores the same feasibility at one thirtieth of that cost by advising a single train, and matches the offline optimum's solution in about a second and a half against the optimiser's minute. Aggregate peak demand is unmoved, because the active constraint is local far-field voltage rather than gross demand. All claims are relative to the baselines on a representative corridor; a specific-route deployment study is future work.

</details>


### [123] [On the Modulating Function Method for Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04544)
*Davi G. Accioli,Jerome Jouffroy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文将对偶调制函数方法从传统的状态/参数估计框架拓展为LTV系统的控制器设计框架，提出了固定时间控制律，并通过数值仿真验证了其与LQR相当的抗扰性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调制函数方法此前仅用于估计与故障检测，本文旨在证明该方法也能用于控制器设计，从而统一估计与控制的理论框架。

Method: 引入对偶调制函数和对偶调制的概念，利用辅助系统与对偶性建立控制器设计框架；推导了控制律存在的充要条件；将状态反馈、输出反馈、LTI滑模控制、能达性格拉姆矩阵和状态转移矩阵作为特例纳入该框架；提出适用于LTI和LTV系统的固定时间控制律。

Result: 所提出的固定时间控制律在处理未匹配扰动时表现出与基准LQR控制器相当的性能水平。

Conclusion: 调制函数方法不仅是一个估计框架，也可以作为LTV系统的控制器设计框架，具有统一估计与控制的潜力。

Abstract: The modulating function method is an algebraic framework that, thus far, has been used for state and parameter estimation, as well as fault detection, of linear, fractional-order, distributed, and some nonlinear systems. At the core of the method lies the modulating function, which can either be selected directly or be obtained as a solution to an auxiliary system. By introducing the notion of dual modulating functions and dual modulations using auxiliary systems and duality, this paper shows that this framework is not only an estimation framework, but also a controller design framework for LTV systems. In particular, necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of the associated control laws are introduced; the well-known state feedback law is obtained as a particular case of the dual modulation approach, along with output feedback, LTI sliding mode control, the reachability gramian, and the state-transition matrix; and a new fixed-time control law is proposed for both LTI and LTV systems, including an estimate of the transient behavior. Moreover, numerical simulations of the newly proposed control law are performed, indicating similar performance levels to a benchmark LQR even when handling unmatched disturbances.

</details>


### [124] [Storage as a Transmission Asset (SATA) for Large-Load Congestion Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04545)
*Abanish Tiwari,Chandan Chaudhary,Yansong Pei,Mohammed Ben-Idris,Joydeep Mitra*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文评估了储能系统作为输电资产（SATA）在运营商调度下对受限输电走廊的可靠性价值，结果表明相比纯套利模式，运营商直接调度的SATA能更有效降低期望缺供电量、失负荷小时数和尾部风险，证明储能的运行模式是影响其输电价值的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心等集中负荷对现有输电网造成巨大压力，在输电走廊传输能力不足时需切负荷、延迟互联或进行网络加固。储能系统作为非导线替代方案可提供运营商支持的受限走廊支撑，但其运行层面的可靠性价值尚未被充分量化。

Method: 采用日前直流最优潮流模型，协同优化发电、储能调度和负荷削减，基于蒙特卡洛场景模拟需求和发电机可用性不确定性；利用阻塞电价和潮流灵敏度指标识别受限走廊和储能位置，并通过EENS、LOLH和CVaR评估运行可靠性。

Result: 运营商直接调度的SATA相比纯套利模式，能显著降低平均缺供电量、失负荷暴露和尾部风险。储能的运行设计是决定其输电价值的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: SATA为受限输电走廊提供了一种有效的非导线替代方案，储能的运行模式（运营商调度 vs. 纯套利）对其输电可靠性价值有决定性影响。

Abstract: Hyperscale data centers and other large concentrated loads can impose substantial new demand on existing transmission networks. If import corridors lack sufficient transfer capability, operators may need to curtail load, delay interconnection, or reinforce the network to maintain reliable service. An energy storage system (ESS) deployed as a storage-as-transmission asset (SATA) offers a non-wires alternative by providing operator-directed support to constrained import corridors. However, the operating-level reliability value of SATA dispatch remains insufficiently quantified. This paper evaluates operator-directed SATA using a day-ahead DC optimal power flow that co-optimizes generation, ESS dispatch, and load curtailment across Monte Carlo scenarios of demand and generator availability. Operating reliability is assessed using expected energy not served (EENS), loss-of-load hours (LOLH), and the conditional value at risk (CVaR) of daily unserved energy. Congestion-price and flow-sensitivity metrics are used to identify the limiting corridor and storage location. The interconnection is then screened to determine whether SATA is suitable, reinforcement is required, or storage would provide little transmission value. Results show that operator-directed SATA reduces average unserved energy, loss-of-load exposure, and tail risk compared with deploying the same ESS for pure arbitrage. These results demonstrate that the operating designation of storage is a primary driver of its transmission value.

</details>


### [125] [Large-Load Demand Flexibility as Virtual Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04564)
*Chandan Chaudhary,Mohammed Ben-Idris,Joydeep Mitra*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过虚拟储能（VS）等效性建立了大规模柔性负荷与电池储能系统（BESS）的统一调度框架，将负荷的功率边界和能量窗口约束转化为虚拟储能设备的充电轨迹，实现了约束数量从O(NT)到O(T)的降维，并证明了联合采购中虚拟储能贡献了主要的经济节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电解水制氢厂、超大规模数据中心和铝电解槽等吉瓦级柔性负荷与电池储能系统在调度上分别采用不同的数学形式（功率边界+能量窗口 vs. 荷电状态动态），缺乏统一的数学结构，导致两者共址时的联合采购价值无法实现。

Method: 提出虚拟储能（VS）等效性，证明任何满足功率边界和能量窗口约束的大负荷可行轨迹，等价于一个单位效率虚拟储能设备的充电轨迹；通过聚合共址的BESS和VS，将约束数量从O(NT)降至O(T)，并导出联合调度价格。

Result: 在IEEE RTS-GMLC系统上针对三类代表性负荷的验证表明：虚拟储能贡献了联合采购节省的主要部分；测试案例中节省具有加性特征，因为两种资源调度到非重叠区间；削减的影子价格跟踪的是峰值价格频段的起始点而非日峰值价格。

Conclusion: 虚拟储能等效性有效统一了大规模柔性负荷与储能的调度框架，为共址资源的联合优化提供了理论基础和高效求解方法。

Abstract: Water electrolysis plants, hyperscale data centers, and aluminum potlines represent gigawatts of demand-side flexibility for bulk power system balancing, operational planning, and procurement services. Such loads are scheduled through per-interval power bounds and horizon energy windows, whereas co-located battery energy storage systems (BESS) operate under state-of-charge dynamics. The two formulations share no common mathematical structure, and the joint procurement value of co-located loads and storage goes unrealized as a result. This paper establishes the connection between the two formulations through a virtual storage (VS) equivalence. Every feasible large-load trajectory under power-bound and energy-window constraints is a valid charge trajectory of a VS device that operates at unity accounting efficiency in the grid power balance. Production and service-level costs lie outside this abstraction and enter the dispatch through curtailment opportunity costs. For a portfolio co-located with a BESS, aggregation reduces the constraint count from O(NT) to O(T) and yields a co-dispatch price for both resources. Validation on the IEEE RTS-GMLC with three representative load classes shows that virtual storage delivers the dominant share of joint procurement savings. In the tested case, savings are additive because the two resources dispatch to non-overlapping intervals, and the curtailment shadow price tracks the peak-price band onset rather than the daily peak price.

</details>


### [126] [LLMs for Agentic Home Energy Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04569)
*Sokipriala Jonah*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM代理（GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.6）作为家用能源调度自然语言界面的可行性，使用ReAct工具调用架构与MILP真实基准对比。所有模型在原生函数调用下实现100%调度成功率和接近MILP最优性，一周模拟捕获96.7-98.0%的oracle节省，预计年省约£1,270。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭能源管理系统（HEMS）可降低电费并支持需求响应，但采用受限，因为将家庭偏好转化为技术调度约束存在困难。本文探究LLM代理能否提供实用的自然语言接口来解决这一问题。

Method: 设计了一个工具调用的ReAct代理，使用半小时制Octopus Agile电价、天气预报、光伏发电估计、家庭用电数据和检索增强知识库进行灵活负载调度，以混合整数线性规划（MILP）为真实基准。在多种场景下对GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.6三个商业模型进行基准测试。

Result: 原生函数调用下所有模型实现100%调度成功率和近MILP最优性；文本解析动作界面大幅降低可靠性。约束测试中Claude在不可行性和功率上限冲突下最强，GPT-4o-mini效率最高。一周模拟中代理捕获96.7-98.0%的oracle节省，相对非高峰定时基线预计年省约£1,270。

Conclusion: LLM代理能有效作为家用能源调度的自然语言接口，不同模型在成本最优与安全最优方面存在差异，原生函数调用是可靠性的关键因素。

Abstract: Home Energy Management Systems (HEMS) can reduce residential electricity costs and support demand response, but adoption is limited by the difficulty of translating household preferences into technical scheduling constraints. This paper evaluates whether large language model (LLM) agents can provide a practical natural-language interface for multi-appliance home energy scheduling. We present a tool-calling ReAct agent that uses live half-hourly Octopus Agile prices, weather forecasts, photovoltaic generation estimates, household usage data, and a retrieval-augmented knowledge base to schedule flexible loads against a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) ground truth. Three commercial models, GPT-4o-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Claude Sonnet 4.6, are benchmarked across tariff days, constraint-conflict scenarios, weather-aware solar co-optimization, and week-long deployment. With native function calling, all models achieve 100% scheduling success and near-MILP optimality, while text-parsed action interfaces sharply reduce reliability. Constraint testing shows that cost-optimal and safety-optimal models differ: Claude is strongest under infeasibility and power-cap conflicts, while GPT-4o-mini is most efficient. Over a simulated week, agents capture 96.7-98.0% of oracle savings, projecting approximately GBP 1,270 annual savings over an off-peak timer baseline. Code and a live demonstration are available at https://github.com/sokistar24/ecohome-energy-agent and https://www.ecohomeagent.com/.

</details>


### [127] [Short-Horizon Sparse Model Predictive Control for Precipitation Reduction Using Numerical Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04746)
*Yuta Tanikawa,Yuga Tomita,Toshiyuki Ohtsuka*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将数值天气预报（NWP）模型与模型预测控制（MPC）相结合的降水控制框架，通过构建有限差分灵敏度矩阵和稀疏凸优化求解控制输入，在暖气泡基准实验中实现了与收缩时域最优控制相当的降水减少效果，且计算时间更优。即使在开环控制方法因非线性大气演化失败的情况下，MPC仍能成功实现具有挑战性的降水减少目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降水控制方法在处理复杂天气事件时面临非线性大气演化导致的优化控制失败问题，且全时域开环控制计算成本高昂。因此，需要一种能够有效处理非线性动态、同时降低计算开销的实时降水控制框架。

Method: 将NWP模型与MPC结合，在每个控制时刻构建有限差分灵敏度矩阵作为局部线性模型，求解稀疏凸优化问题获得大气状态扰动的控制输入。为降低灵敏度分析的计算成本，将多个网格点集体视为一个块并施加统一扰动；同时设计定制化凸优化问题，用远短于事件全长的预测时域控制累积降水量，并促进空间稀疏的大气扰动。

Result: MPC实现了与收缩时域最优控制（SHOC）相当的降水减少效果，同时比全时域开环控制（FH-OL）和SHOC的总计算时间更短。在线性预测模型下，MPC成功实现了具有挑战性的降水减少水平，而开环控制方法（IO-OL和FH-OL）因非线性大气演化失败。

Conclusion: MPC是基于NWP的复杂天气事件降水减少的有前景控制框架，能够平衡控制效果与计算效率，并有效应对大气系统的非线性演化。

Abstract: This study proposes a precipitation control framework integrating a realistic Numerical Weather Prediction (NWP) model with model predictive control (MPC). At each control instant in MPC, a finite-difference sensitivity matrix is constructed from the NWP model and used as a local linear model of how perturbations to the atmospheric state affect future precipitation. A sparse convex optimization problem is then solved to compute the control input, which is implemented as a perturbation to the atmospheric state. To reduce computational cost in sensitivity analysis, multiple grid points in the NWP model are treated collectively as a single block, and a uniform perturbation is applied to all points within each block. Moreover, a tailored convex optimization problem is introduced to effectively control the accumulated precipitation at the end of a weather event, using a prediction horizon much shorter than the entire event duration while promoting spatially sparse atmospheric perturbations. To evaluate the proposed MPC method, four control methods are compared: (i) initial-only open-loop optimal control (IO-OL), (ii) full-horizon open-loop optimal control (FH-OL), (iii) shrinking-horizon optimal control (SHOC) with a fixed terminal time, and (iv) single-move MPC with a fixed prediction-horizon length. Numerical experiments on a warm bubble benchmark demonstrate that MPC achieves precipitation reduction comparable to SHOC while reducing the total computational time relative to FH-OL and SHOC. Moreover, despite using a linear prediction model, MPC successfully achieves a challenging level of precipitation reduction, even when open-loop optimal control methods, namely, IO-OL and FH-OL, fail because of nonlinear atmospheric evolution. These findings suggest that MPC is a promising control framework for NWP-based precipitation reduction in complex weather events.

</details>


### [128] [Cross-Scale Performance Analysis of Metaheuristic Algorithms for Simultaneous DG and DSTATCOM Placement in Radial Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04954)
*Md. Tanvirul Islam*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了七种元启发式算法（GWO、SCA、PSO、WOA、GA、HHO、SMA）在IEEE 33、69、136节点辐射配电网中同时优化分布式发电机和DSTATCOM选址定容问题的可扩展性。在维度d=4、8、12下，通过30次独立运行和Wilcoxon、Friedman统计检验发现：维度增长是算法行为的判别器；GA在所有配置中表现最优；SCA方差小但收敛至次优解；HHO存在灾难性不稳定性。关键发现是136节点系统在d=12时CFR仅3.3%，而33节点系统在相同维度下CFR高达73%-83%，表明网络拓扑对可靠性的影响无法被单一系统指标捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对分布式发电与DSTATCOM联合选址这一混合整数优化问题在高维决策空间下的算法可扩展性探索不足，缺乏跨尺度、多维度的系统性对比分析。

Method: 在IEEE 33、69、136节点三个辐射配电网系统上，针对GWO、SCA、PSO、WOA、GA、HHO、SMA七种元启发式算法，在维度d=4、8、12下各执行30次独立运行，采用Wilcoxon符号秩检验和Friedman秩和检验进行统计验证，并引入灾难性故障率（CFR）指标衡量算法可靠性。

Result: Friedman χ²随维度增加而增大，在136节点、d=12时达到最大值χ²=143.79，算法分化为高低性能两簇；GA在所有配置中排名最低（最优）；SCA方差低但收敛性差；HHO在所有尺度下均表现灾难性不稳定；GA和PSO在136节点d=12时CFR仅3.3%，而33节点相同维度下CFR高达73%-83%。

Conclusion: 维度扩展可作为算法行为的有效判别器；网络拓扑规模与算法可靠性之间存在非线性关系，单一系统指标无法准确预测算法性能；GA在处理高维配电网优化问题时具有最佳鲁棒性。

Abstract: The problem of simultaneous placement of distributed generators and DSTATCOMs in radial distribution networks (RDNs) is a combinatorial mixed-integer optimization problem whose scalability with growing decision dimensionality has been insufficiently explored. A cross-scale analysis of seven metaheuristic algorithms, GWO, SCA, PSO, WOA, GA, HHO and SMA, is conducted on the IEEE 33-bus, 69-bus, and 136-bus systems at three problem dimensions \( d = 4, 8, 12 \), with 30 independent runs per configuration being validated through Wilcoxon and Friedman tests. Mean-performance statistics are extended with a Catastrophic Failure Rate (CFR) metric. The main result will be that dimensional scaling serves as a behavioral discriminator. The Friedman \( χ^2 \) rises with the dimensionality, reaching its maximum value of \( χ^2 = 143.79 \) in the 136-bus at \( d = 12 \) that corresponds to the progressive phase separation of the algorithms into two clusters of high and low performance. GA is the best performer in terms of the lowest rank in all the configurations. SCA has low variance but convergence to increasingly sub-optimal solutions. HHO exhibits catastrophic instability at all scales. Perhaps most strikingly, GA and PSO obtain a \( 3.3\% \) CFR on the 136-bus at \( d = 12 \) while the 33-bus at identical dimensionality has a \( 73\%-83\% \) CFR, showing that topology influences the reliability in a manner that is not captured by single-system measures.

</details>


### [129] [Pathological Regimes of Closed-Loop Recommendation Systems over Social Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05010)
*Mariano Simone,Frasca Paolo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文从控制理论视角将推荐系统设计建模为无限时域状态反馈最优控制问题，通过引入包含对齐/参与度、极化惩罚、基线偏差、推荐失配、控制代价和邻居曝光差异的二次型性能指标，推导了保证代价函数正定性的谱条件，并证明了条件失效会导致不稳定自由模态、下确界不可达或稳态仿射综合失败等病态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对社交网络和电商平台推荐系统，传统方法缺乏对推荐长期动态影响（如极化、用户分歧）的理论保证，本文旨在从控制理论视角提供严格的分析框架。

Method: 将推荐设计建模为无限时域状态反馈最优控制问题，采用包含对齐/参与度奖励、极化惩罚、基线偏差、推荐失配、控制代价和邻居曝光差异的二次型性能指标，推导保证阶段代价严格正定的显式谱条件，并分析条件失效时的病态行为。

Result: 推导了保证二次型阶段代价严格正定的显式谱条件；证明了条件失效会导致不稳定自由模态、下确界不可达或稳态仿射综合失败等病态推荐行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱条件可有效保障推荐系统设计的理论良定性，为设计具有可控长期行为的推荐策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of designing recommendation systems for social networks and e-commerce platforms from a control-theoretic perspective. We formulate recommendation design as an infinite-horizon state-feedback optimal control problem whose performance index rewards alignment/engagement while penalizing polarization, large deviations from an uncontrolled baseline, recommendation mismatch, control effort, and exposure disagreement across neighboring users. We derive explicit spectral conditions under which the reduced quadratic stage cost is strictly positive-definite, and we show that the failure of these conditions makes the resulting recommendation design exhibit pathological behaviors, such as unstable free modes, non-attainment of the infimum, or failure of the stationary affine synthesis.

</details>


### [130] [Weighted Phase Volume Method in Stability Analysis: Integral Criteria and Ellipsoidal Reachable Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05033)
*Igor B. Furtat*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加权相体积与时间重缩放的动力学系统稳定性分析方法，通过选择加权函数和缩放因子控制相体积收缩性质，同时保持相肖像拓扑结构。推导了积分耗散条件，定义了积分稳定性、渐近稳定性和指数稳定性新概念，并利用二次加权函数构造包络椭球与内椭球以实现可达集的几何估计。建立了与经典Lyapunov稳定性的联系，数值示例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Lyapunov方法在分析复杂动力学系统稳定性时存在局限性，尤其是难以同时兼顾相体积收缩特性的灵活控制与相肖像拓扑结构的保持。作者旨在提出一种更普适的分析框架，通过加权相体积与时间重缩放技术，在保留系统拓扑特征的前提下增强稳定性分析的灵活性。

Method: 引入加权相体积概念，并通过正函数对时间进行重缩放；通过选择加权函数与缩放因子控制相体积的收缩/膨胀特性；推导积分耗散条件（integral dissipativity conditions）以定义积分稳定性、渐近稳定性和指数稳定性；针对二次加权函数，构造包络椭球（covering ellipsoids）和内椭球（inner ellipsoids）用于可达集的几何估计。

Result: 建立了加权相体积方法与经典Lyapunov稳定性理论之间的形式化联系；提出了一套基于积分耗散条件的稳定性新定义；通过椭球构造实现了可达集边界的几何量化估计；数值实验验证了该方法在典型动力学系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持系统拓扑结构的同时，通过灵活调节加权函数和缩放因子提供了更丰富的稳定性分析手段，且与经典Lyapunov理论兼容，为复杂动力学系统的稳定性研究开辟了新途径。

Abstract: A method for analysing the stability of dynamical systems is proposed, based on the introduction of a weighted phase volume and time rescaling by a positive function. The advantage of the method is the ability to set the contraction properties of the phase volume by choosing the weighting function and the scaling factor, while preserving the topology of the phase portrait. Integral dissipativity conditions are derived, leading to new definitions of integral stability, asymptotic stability, and exponential stability. For quadratic weighting functions, covering and inner ellipsoids are constructed, providing geometric estimates of reachable sets. The connection between the proposed approach and classical Lyapunov stability is established. The efficiency of the method is demonstrated through numerical examples.

</details>


### [131] [Double interior-point regularization for large-scale capacity expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05047)
*Leonard Göke,Giovanni Sansavini*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出双内点正则化（DIP-set）方法用于Benders分解，通过结合解空间内部遍历与参考解约束，解决了可再生能源系统规划中因天气依赖发电和长时间储能导致的计算规模过大的问题。在电力部门和能源系统问题上的测试表明，DIP-set在所有测试案例中均优于竞争方法，对最大问题的加速比达到30-50%，有效缓解了Benders分解接近最优解时收敛急剧下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内点求解器无法处理因天气依赖发电和长时间储能导致的大规模能源规划问题，难以在广泛天气条件下实现成本高效且可靠的规划。

Method: 提出双内点正则化（DIP-set）方法应用于Benders分解，结合遍历解空间内部与保持参考解邻近的双重优势。

Result: DIP-set在所有测试案例中均优于竞争方法，加速比随问题规模增大而提高，对最大问题达到30-50%的加速；能有效缓解Benders分解接近最优解时收敛性能急剧下降的问题。

Conclusion: DIP-set为解决大规模可再生能源系统规划问题提供了一种高效的方法，尤其适用于现有方法无法处理的关键大型问题。

Abstract: Capacity expansion is a key tool for planning future energy systems. However, weather-dependent generation and long-duration storage result in problem sizes that exceed the computational limits of conventional interior-point solvers, making it impossible to plan renewable systems that are cost-efficient and reliable across a wide range of weather conditions. To tackle such large problems, this paper introduces the double interior-point regularization (DIP-set) for Benders Decomposition, combining the advantages of traversing the interior of the solution space while remaining close to a reference solution. We benchmark the method on a power-sector problem and an energy-system problem, varying problem size and the level of foresight during operations. Results demonstrate that DIP-set outperforms competing regularizations in all test cases. The speed-up increases with size, reaching 30-50% for the largest problems, which are the most critical for planning renewable systems and are too large for state-of-the-art methods. The key benefit of DIP-set is its ability to mitigate the sharp decrease in convergence as BD approaches the optimal solution.

</details>


### [132] [Surrogate-based prioritization of sub-problems for Benders decomposition in energy planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05063)
*Wanhong Yu,Boyung Jürgens,Leonard Göke*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代理模型（surrogate）的子问题优先级排序方法，用于加速Benders分解算法。该方法通过代理模型估计子问题目标函数值及其切割平面估计误差，优先求解误差最大的子问题，并在序贯和异步Benders分解中实现。在能源规划问题的基准测试中，序贯Benders分解的加速比随场景数增加可达33%-55%，但异步并行化下的加速效果不显著（平均19%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Benders分解每轮迭代需求解所有子问题，计算成本高。虽然仅求解部分子问题仍可保证收敛，但如何选择子问题以最小化求解时间是一个关键问题。

Method: 利用代理模型（如几何插值、机器学习方法）估计子问题的目标函数值，评估当前切割平面估计器的误差，优先求解误差最大的子问题，并在序贯和异步Benders分解中集成该策略，同时利用代理触发收敛检查和正则化。

Result: 在能源规划问题中，几何插值代理模型比机器学习方法更准确；序贯Benders分解中，优先级排序持续且显著优于标准算法，加速比随场景数增加：4个场景达33%，10个场景达55%；异步并行化中平均加速比为19%，但性能影响不明确。

Conclusion: 基于代理模型的子问题优先级排序能有效加速序贯Benders分解，加速效果与代理精度正相关；但异步并行化场景下的收益有限，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Benders decomposition solves optimization problems by separating the first-stage master problem from one or more second-stage sub-problems. While the standard Benders decomposition solves all sub-problems in each iteration, solving only selected sub-problems still guarantees convergence and can reduce solution time, but raises the question of how to select.
  In this work, we introduce surrogate-based prioritization of sub-problems. The method leverages surrogates to estimate the sub-problems' objectives, assess the current error of the cutting-plane estimator, and then prioritize the sub-problem with the largest error. We implement surrogate-based prioritization within sequential and asynchronous Benders decomposition. Both these algorithms also leverage the surrogate to trigger convergence checks and implement regularization.
  Benchmarks for an energy planning problem with a few large sub-problems show that the applied prioritization strategy works. The reduction in solution time correlates with the surrogate's accuracy. In our case, geometric interpolation-based surrogates are more accurate than machine learning methods. As a result, prioritization consistently and significantly outperforms the standard algorithm in sequential Benders decomposition. The speed-up increases with the number of scenarios, reaching 33\% with four scenarios and 55% with ten scenarios. In the case of asynchronous parallelization, the impact on performance is less clear, and the average speed-up from prioritization is 19%.

</details>


### [133] [Model-Guided Local Bayesian Optimization for Tuning of Interpretable Controllers in Injection Molding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05159)
*Jens Ahlers,Robert Göllinger,Xu Chen,Heike Vallery,Sebastian Stemmler*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于物理启发式神经混合局部专家模型与残差高斯过程的方法，在注塑成型过程中自动优化可解释控制器参数，兼顾循环效率与风险意识，在20轮优化内达到或优于全局贝叶斯优化的成本表现，同时减少高成本波动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进控制方法虽能有效控制注塑成型中的型腔压力，但其控制律抽象难解，限制了工业应用；同时，模具几何形状与材料的多样性使控制器优化极具挑战。

Method: 采用物理启发式神经混合局部专家模型（Physics-Inspired Neural Mixture-of-Local-Experts）建模注塑动态，并用残差高斯过程补偿模拟闭环成本误差，进而实现局部贝叶斯优化（Local Bayesian Optimization）控制器参数。

Result: 在仿真中针对1至30个参数的三种控制器进行测试，局部方法在20轮优化迭代中取得了与全局贝叶斯优化相当或更低的成本，同时有效抑制了调优过程中的高成本波动。

Conclusion: 所提方法能在制造过程中自动、高效、安全地优化可解释控制器，兼顾成本性能与风险控制，具有较好的工业应用潜力。

Abstract: Advanced control methods have proven effective for controlling cavity pressure, a key determinant of part-quality attributes, in the plastics injection molding process. However, the abstract nature of the resulting control laws makes them difficult to interpret in a production environment, thereby limiting adoption in industrial applications. Additionally, controller optimization poses a severe challenge due to the diversity of mold geometries and materials. We propose a method to automatically optimize interpretable controllers during manufacturing while being cycle-efficient and risk-aware. The approach uses a Physics-Inspired Neural Mixture-of-Local-Experts model of the injection molding dynamics and augments its simulated closed-loop costs with a residual Gaussian Process, enabling Local Bayesian Optimization of controller parameters. We benchmark the algorithm against Vanilla Bayesian Optimization (BO) in simulation, using three controllers with parameter counts ranging from 1 to 30. Using the local method, we identify controller parameters that yield costs comparable to or lower than those of global BO over 20 optimization iterations, while mitigating high-cost excursions during tuning.

</details>


### [134] [Shadow Pricing of Static Voltage Stability Services within Unit Commitment for Inverter-Dominated Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05209)
*Peng Wang,Luis Badesa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对逆变器主导的电力系统中GFL型IBR因缺乏电压直接控制和常功率因数运行导致的静态电压失稳问题，本文在机组组合问题中引入基于二阶锥的静态电压稳定约束，并设计市场定价机制为无功电压支撑辅助服务分配影子价格，对比了可调度定价与受限定价两种方案的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统中GFL型IBR不直接控制端电压，常功率因数运行缺乏电压自适应无功支撑，加之低短路比环境加剧电压问题；现有方案（构网控制、电网规范）缺乏市场激励，尚未有理论框架建立针对电压稳定的辅助服务市场机制。

Method: 在机组组合问题中引入基于二阶锥规划的静态电压稳定约束（针对GFL-IBR节点），提出为该辅助服务分配影子价格的机制；在非凸条件下评估不同定价方案（可调度定价与受限定价）。

Result: 在改进的IEEE 30节点系统上验证：可调度定价与受限定价均可产生收益充足的辅助服务价格；可调度定价价格信号效率较低，受限定价可能需要明确的补偿支付（uplift payments）。

Conclusion: 不同定价机制下价格信号存在差异，运营商需根据实际系统条件和市场规则选择合适的定价方法。

Abstract: Modern power systems are increasingly dominated by Inverter-Based Resources (IBR), most of which work in Grid-following (GFL) mode. This implies that they do not directly control their terminal voltage, so the static voltage stability at these buses may be compromised, especially under constant-power-factor operation that lacks voltage-adaptive reactive support. In addition, weather-driven IBR are often installed in electrically remote areas with low Short-Circuit Ratio (SCR), further exacerbating voltage issues. To address this challenge, grid-forming control can be utilized to enhance low-SCR buses, while GFL-IBR could be explicitly required to provide voltage support through grid codes. As an alternative, a market mechanism could be devised that incentivizes relevant generators to proactively adjust their operating points as a service to maintain voltage stability, while the theoretical framework for such a market has not been developed. To fill this gap, this work adopts a second-order cone-based static voltage stability constraint for GFL-IBR buses within a unit commitment problem, and proposes a mechanism to assign shadow prices to this ancillary service. To determine appropriate price values under non-convex conditions, different pricing schemes are assessed. Using a modified IEEE 30-bus system, we demonstrate that both the dispatchable and restricted pricing methods can yield revenue-adequate service prices, though the former may deliver less efficient price signals and the latter may require well-defined uplift payments. This implies that, given differentiated pricing mechanisms and price signals, operators need to select a suitable pricing method in accordance with actual system conditions and market rules.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Physics-Informed Tensor Completion with Application to Distribution System State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02688)
*Alexander While,Anil Aswani,Chen Chen,William N. Caballero*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对配电网低可观测性下的状态估计问题，提出了一种基于物理信息的低秩张量补全方法，通过将电压相量、幅值和功率注入编码为低秩张量，并利用线性化潮流方程构建凸残差惩罚，结合混合条件梯度算法求解，在低可观测性场景下显著优于纯数据驱动的张量补全和加权最小二乘法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网状态估计中测量数据稀疏、含噪且异构，经典加权最小二乘法在低可观测性场景下不可靠，亟需一种能融合物理模型与低秩结构的新方法。

Method: 1) 将电压相量分量、电压幅值和功率注入时域数据编码为低秩张量；2) 通过线性化潮流方程构建凸残差惩罚项以融入物理信息；3) 将问题转化为张量规范球上的凸优化问题（等价于整数规划隐式描述的多面体）；4) 采用全局收敛的混合条件梯度法求解，形成通用算法框架；5) 推导了基于受限稳定性条件的确定性误差界。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提方法在低可观测性场景（含缺失数据）下，状态恢复效果显著优于纯数据驱动的张量补全和加权最小二乘法；推导的误差界表明估计误差受测量噪声和物理模型失配控制。

Conclusion: 该方法为配电网低可观测性状态估计提供了有效方案，且因算法框架的通用性，可推广至其他具有低秩结构和凸物理残差的应用领域。

Abstract: State estimation in power distribution networks often involves sparse, noisy, and heterogeneous measurements, making classical weighted least-squares methods unreliable in low-observability regimes. To meet this challenge we develop a physics-informed low-rank tensor completion method. The method encodes voltage phasor components, voltage magnitudes, and power injections over time in a low-rank tensor, while incorporating physics through convex residual penalties. For distribution network state estimation, these residuals are instantiated using linearized power-flow equations, yielding a convex optimization problem over a tensor gauge ball, equivalently represented as a polytope implicitly described via integer programming. We solve this problem using globally convergent blended conditional gradients, resulting in a general-purpose algorithmic framework that can be adapted across different domains and applications where low-rank structure and convex physics residuals are available. We also derive a deterministic error bound showing that, under a restricted stability condition, the estimation error is controlled by measurement noise and physics-model mismatch. Numerical experiments show that the proposed method substantially improves state recovery compared with purely data-driven tensor completion and weighted least-squares, especially in low-observability regimes with missing data.

</details>


### [136] [Elastically safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02697)
*Ernesto G. Birgin,Alberto De Marchi,Patrick Mehlitz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种弹性安全防护的增广拉格朗日方法，通过动态增长的安全防护机制替代传统固定安全防护，在保持全局收敛性的同时克服了先验选择困难和适应性不足的问题，并建立了最坏情况迭代复杂度界，数值实验验证了其优于ALGENCAN等现有求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全防护增广拉格朗日方法虽然具有更强的全局收敛保证，但其固定安全防护机制存在先验选择困难且缺乏自适应能力（如对问题缩放不敏感），限制了方法的实用性和适应性。

Method: 提出弹性安全防护机制，允许安全防护动态增长，在保留固定安全防护变体全局收敛性质的前提下克服其局限性，并给出最坏情况迭代复杂度分析。

Result: 数值实验对比了采用刚性安全防护的ALGENCAN求解器与弹性安全防护变体，结果表明弹性安全防护在实用中具有一致的性能优势。

Conclusion: 弹性安全防护机制有效解决了固定安全防护的适应性问题，同时保持了理想的全局收敛特性，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate, theoretically and numerically, a class of elastically safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods for nonlinear optimization problems with inequality and equality constraints. Safeguarded augmented Lagrangian methods are known to exhibit substantially stronger global convergence guarantees than their non-safeguarded counterparts, making them attractive in practice. A persistent limitation, however, is that existing methods rely on a fixed safeguard, whose a priori selection can be difficult and inherently limits adaptivity, e.g., with respect to problem scaling. We propose an adaptive safeguarding mechanism that allows the safeguard to grow dynamically, overcoming these drawbacks while preserving the desirable global convergence properties of variants with a fixed safeguard. We further establish complexity bounds in terms of worst-case iteration counts. Numerical experiments comparing ALGENCAN, a state-of-the-art solver with rigid safeguard, against an elastically safeguarded variant thereof confirm that elastic safeguarding yields consistent practical benefits.

</details>


### [137] [Optimality-Informed Neural Networks for Lunar Landing Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02741)
*Zhenbo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种最优性信息神经网络（OINN）方法，用于月球着陆器在自由最终时间下的能量最优动力下降。该方法将庞特里亚金极小值原理和HJB方程嵌入网络架构，硬编码所有边界和横截条件，直接代入解析最优推力律，仅通过最优性必要条件构成的物理残差损失进行训练，无需预计算最优轨迹。理论分析提供了训练平稳性保证、残差与着陆误差之间的显式界以及固定计算成本。数值仿真在6个代表性初始状态和80次蒙特卡洛测试中与间接法解高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如间接法）需要离线求解两点边值问题，计算成本高且难以实时部署；而直接使用神经网络学习最优策略通常需要大量预计算的最优轨迹作为监督数据。本文旨在开发一种无需预计算最优轨迹、可实时部署且满足最优性必要条件的神经网络方法。

Method: OINN方法：1) 将庞特里亚金极小值原理和HJB方程嵌入网络架构；2) 硬编码所有边界条件和横截条件；3) 使用闭合形式的庞特里亚金最优推力幅值和方向律（不通过学习获得）；4) 训练状态、协态和辅助值函数输出，损失函数完全由最优性必要条件的物理残差构成。同时给出了离线训练的随机优化驻点保证、训练残差与着陆误差的显式界以及固定计算成本的理论分析。

Result: 在6个代表性初始状态和80次蒙特卡洛仿真中，OINN训练的模型（无需重新训练）与独立求解的间接法两点边值解高度一致，动力学残差和横截残差在整个操作包络内持续很小。

Conclusion: OINN方法成功实现了无需预计算最优轨迹、满足最优性必要条件、且计算成本固定的月球着陆实时制导策略，理论分析和数值仿真均验证了其有效性。

Abstract: This paper develops an Optimality-Informed Neural Network (OINN) approach for the energy-optimal, free-final-time powered descent of a lunar lander from any initial position, velocity, and mass within a bounded operating envelope to a fixed landing site with zero terminal velocity. Building on a recent framework that jointly embeds Pontryagin's minimum principle and the Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation for general nonlinear optimal control, the proposed OINN approach specializes that idea to a lunar landing problem with free time of flight and fixed terminal state. Every boundary and transversality condition is hard-encoded into the network architecture by construction, the closed-form Pontryagin-optimal thrust magnitude and direction law is substituted directly rather than learned, and the remaining state, costate, and an auxiliary value-function output are trained against a physics-residual loss formed entirely from the necessary conditions of optimality, with no precomputed optimal trajectories required. A preliminary theoretical analysis is explored, establishing a stochastic-optimization stationarity guarantee for the offline training procedure, an explicit bound translating the achieved training residual into bounds on touchdown position, touchdown velocity, and flight-time error, and a fixed, input-independent onboard computational and memory cost suitable for real-time deployment. Numerical simulations evaluate the trained policy, with no retraining, against an independently solved indirect-method boundary-value problem at six representative initial states spanning the operating envelope and against eighty additional Monte Carlo simulation runs, demonstrating close agreement with the indirect-method solution and consistently small dynamics and transversality residuals throughout the envelope.

</details>


### [138] [Online Modeling and Sequential Convex Programming for Lunar Landing Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02750)
*Zhenbo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合序列凸规划（SCP）与实时在线模型辨识的月球动力下降制导框架，通过递归最小二乘滤波和指数移动平均平滑器估计未知扰动，并注入修正偏置项，实现能量最优着陆。数值仿真表明该在线算法能有效降低着陆位置和速度误差，更准确地跟踪真实推进剂消耗，同时保持凸优化的可预测收敛性和实时计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球动力下降和着陆过程中存在未知的引力扰动、推力缩放和质量测量偏差，这些不确定性会影响着陆精度和推进剂消耗。现有方法难以在实时计算的同时有效补偿这些扰动，因此需要一种结合在线模型辨识的制导框架。

Method: 将非凸能量最优着陆问题通过变量替换、逐次线性化以及推力方向约束的无损二阶锥松弛转化为一系列凸二阶锥规划（SOCP）；构建基于带指数遗忘的递归最小二乘（RLS）滤波器和指数移动平均（EMA）平滑器的在线辨识层，从含噪导航测量中估计未知扰动，并在每个制导周期将修正偏置项注入凸子问题的动力学约束中；开发了基线SCP算法和带模型辨识的滚动时域在线SCP算法。

Result: 在四种复杂度递增的扰动场景下的数值仿真表明，所提出的在线算法相比未修正的名义轨迹，能够持续降低着陆位置和速度误差，更准确地跟踪真实推进剂消耗，同时保持凸优化的可预测收敛性和实时计算性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCP与在线模型辨识相结合的制导框架能有效处理月球着陆中的未知扰动，提升着陆精度和燃料利用效率，且具备理论保障的收敛性和实时性，适用于实际月球动力下降任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance framework for lunar powered descent and landing that combines sequential convex programming (SCP) with real-time online model identification. A nonconvex energy-optimal landing problem is developed and then reformulated into a sequence of convex second-order cone programs (SOCPs) through a change of variables, successive linearization, and a lossless second-order cone relaxation of the thrust direction constraint. An online identification layer, built from a recursive least squares (RLS) filter with exponential forgetting and an exponential moving average (EMA) smoother, estimates unknown gravitational, thrust-scale, and mass-gauging perturbations from noisy navigation measurements and injects a corrected bias term into the dynamics constraint of each convex subproblem at every guidance cycle. Building on this architecture and my prior work in this area, a baseline SCP algorithm and a receding-horizon online SCP algorithm with model identification are developed. Also, I try to explore some theoretical foundations, establishing the losslessness of the convex relaxation, the mean-square stability and convergence of the identification filters, the guaranteed convergence of the SCP iteration, and explicit convergence radius and convergence rate results. Numerical simulations across four perturbation scenarios of increasing complexity are implemented in MATLAB using YALMIP and the ECOS solver. The results show that the proposed online algorithm consistently reduces landing position and velocity error and better tracks the true propellant consumption relative to an uncorrected nominal trajectory, while retaining the predictable convergence and real-time computational properties of convex optimization.

</details>


### [139] [Singularities in Multi-Objective Optimization and their Crossing during Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02803)
*Arjun Manoj,Michail E. Kavousanakis,Shanqing Liu,Ioannis G. Kevrekidis*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于奇异重参数化的延拓方法，通过伪弧长延拓和坐标射影紧化，在单次运行中系统穿越加权标量化中的奇异点，从而恢复双目标无约束优化问题中Pareto前沿的断连分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统延拓方法在求解多目标优化问题时本质上是局部的，单次运行只能追踪一个连通分支，而Pareto前沿可能存在多个断连的分量，需要多次重启才能恢复，效率低下。

Method: 将Pareto最优性重构为非线性系统，应用伪弧长延拓追踪Pareto临界集；通过奇异重参数化系统穿越权重λ发散（目标梯度共线）的奇异点；采用坐标射影紧化统一参数空间和决策空间变量。

Result: 在ZDT3基准测试及其修改版本上验证，该方法能在单次运行中成功恢复Pareto前沿的断连分支。

Conclusion: 加权标量化中Pareto前沿的断连可能是标量化参数奇异点的人为产物，所提方法通过奇异重参数化可系统性地跨越这些奇异点，实现单次运行下的完整Pareto临界集追踪。

Abstract: Continuation methods help trace Pareto sets in multi-objective optimization but are inherently local: a single run traces a single connected branch, requiring multiple restarts to recover disconnected components of Pareto fronts. We show that, for unconstrained bi-objective problems under weighted-sum scalarization, these disconnects can be artifacts of singularities in the scalarization parameter, where the weight $λ$ diverges as the objective gradients become collinear. Recasting Pareto optimality as a nonlinear system, we apply pseudo-arclength continuation to follow the Pareto-critical set, and show that suitable singular reparameterizations allow crossing these singularities in systematically, recovering disconnected branches in a single run. A coordinate-wise projective compactification further provides a unified framework for parameter and decision-space variables. We demonstrate the approach on the ZDT3 benchmark and modifications.

</details>


### [140] [A Proximal Point Semismooth Newton Algorithm for Structural Composite Cardinality Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02926)
*Penghe Zhang,Naihua Xiu,Houduo Qi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对复合基数优化问题（CCOP），通过考虑其对偶形式（包含可分离的基数项），提出了一种高效的基于邻近点算法（PPA）的求解框架。该算法利用半光滑牛顿法（SNA）求解子问题，并设计了可实现的非精确准则，在适当条件下具有全局收敛性和局部线性收敛速度。数值实验在跳稀疏信号恢复和CT图像重建任务中验证了方法的效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合基数优化问题中，基数函数与线性算子复合后破坏了可分离性，且收敛性分析常需满射性假设，带来算法挑战。本文通过转向对偶形式，获得更有利的结构（可分离基数项），从而开发高效算法。

Method: 考虑CCOP的平稳对偶形式（含两个凸项和一个可分离基数项）；设计非精确邻近点算法（PPA）求解对偶问题；子问题通过半光滑牛顿法（SNA）在低维子空间上最小化光滑凸函数；提出可实现的非精确准则用于SNA子程序的提前终止。

Result: 理论方面：算法在适当条件下具有全局收敛性和局部线性收敛速度，且给出了收敛假设自动满足的实例。实验方面：在跳稀疏信号恢复和CT图像重建任务中，所提方法在时间效率和求解精度上均表现优异。

Conclusion: 提出的非精确PPA框架有效解决了复合基数优化问题，克服了原问题中基数函数不可分离的困难，理论和实验均验证了算法的收敛性与高效性。

Abstract: Many image-processing problems can be formulated as composite cardinality optimization (CCOP), whose objective is the sum of two convex terms and a cardinality function composed with a linear operator. The composite cardinality term creates major algorithmic challenges: the separability of the cardinality function is lost and convergence analysis often requires surjectivity-type assumptions on the linear operators. To overcome these challenges, we consider the stationary dual formulation of CCOP, which has more favorable structure consisting of two convex terms and a separable cardinality function. In this paper, we develop an efficient proximal point algorithm (PPA) to solve the stationary dual problem. The efficiency of our PPA stems from two aspects. Firstly, the key step of its subproblem solver minimizes a smooth convex function over a low-dimensional subspace by the classic semismooth Newton algorithm (SNA), which has global convergence and local superlinear rate under suitable conditions. Secondly, implementable inexact criteria are proposed for early termination of the SNA subroutine. These components form the basic framework of our inexact PPA. Under suitable conditions, it enjoys global convergence and local linear convergence rate. In particular, we provide examples in which the convergence assumptions are automatically satisfied. Finally, the SNA subroutine is incorporated into our inexact PPA to solve jump-sparse signal recovery and computed tomography (CT) image restoration. Numerical results demonstrate the time efficiency and solution accuracy of our proposed method.

</details>


### [141] [Entropy Regularization Improves Policy Robustness in Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03168)
*Jialun Cao,Fernando Acero,David Šiška,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次为连续时间强化学习中的熵正则化提供了鲁棒性理论保证，证明其最大化目标相当于最坏情况鲁棒RL的下界，且鲁棒集随正则化强度单调扩张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 熵正则化在连续时间RL中广泛用于降低对环境扰动的敏感性，但其鲁棒性优势缺乏严格的理论基础。

Method: 理论分析证明熵正则化目标函数等价于一个联合奖励与转移扰动的最坏情况鲁棒RL问题的下界，并解析表征了诱导的鲁棒集及其随正则化强度的单调扩张性质。

Result: 建立了连续时间MDP中熵正则化的鲁棒性保证，消除了离散时间分析中难以处理的状态分布熵项，提供了不依赖于动作频率的不变性保证。实验验证了熵正则化策略在队列网络控制和做市任务中的鲁棒性优于贪心和ε-贪心基线。

Conclusion: 熵正则化在连续时间RL中具有理论上的鲁棒性增强作用，且正则化强度越大鲁棒性越强，为实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Entropy regularization is widely used in continuous-time reinforcement learning (RL) to reduce sensitivity to environmental perturbations, yet its robustness benefits lack a rigorous theoretical foundation. This paper establishes the first robustness guarantees for entropy-regularized continuous-time Markov decision processes. We show that maximizing an entropy-regularized objective yields a lower bound on a worst-case robust RL problem with joint reward and transition perturbations. We analytically characterize the induced robust sets and prove that they expand monotonically with the regularization strength, justifying the empirical observation that stronger entropy improves robustness. In contrast to prior discrete-time analyses, our results remove the intractable state-distribution entropy term and provide guarantees invariant to action frequency. Experiments on queueing network control and market making confirm our theory, showing that entropy-regularized policies outperform greedy and $ε$-greedy baselines under dynamics perturbations.

</details>


### [142] [Inverse Optimal Control for Linear Quadratic Problem with Poisson Jumps: Model-Free Inverse Reinforcement Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03169)
*Wen Du,Na Li,Xun Li,Zuo Quan Xu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对受布朗运动和泊松跳影响的随机线性系统，提出两种基于逆强化学习的无模型离线逆最优控制算法，通过专家演示数据逆向求解等价代价权重，无需知道系统动力学或求解Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决随机线性系统在面临布朗运动和泊松跳两种随机扰动时，如何从专家策略中逆向识别等价代价函数的问题，特别是在系统动力学未知的情况下。

Method: 提出两种无模型离策略逆强化学习算法：(1) 逆Q学习算法，从专家演示构建数据驱动方程计算Q函数矩阵，代数更新代价权重；(2) 无模型离策略逆策略迭代算法，利用初始稳定策略下收集的数据进行迭代学习。两种算法均将数据收集行为策略与迭代更新策略解耦。

Result: 两种算法无需知道系统动力学或跳跃强度，仅需充分激励的轨迹数据即可学习等价代价权重，数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的无模型离策略逆强化学习方法能够有效解决随机线性系统在未知动力学下的逆最优控制问题，为实际应用中难以获取精确模型的情况提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the inverse optimal control (IOC) problem for stochastic linear systems subject to both Brownian motion and Poisson jumps, using an inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) framework. Given a target feedback gain from an expert, the objective is to identify an equivalent cost functional-specifically, the set of all cost weights-that yields this same gain. To solve this problem when system dynamics are unknown, we propose two model-free, off-policy IRL algorithms that operate entirely from data, circumventing the need to solve the generalized algebraic Riccati equation or compute the cost weights analytically. The first is an inverse Q-learning algorithm that constructs data-driven equations from expert demonstrations to compute the Q-function matrix, with equivalent cost weights updated algebraically and without requiring additional trajectory data. The second is a model-free off-policy inverse policy iteration algorithm that leverages data collected under an initial stabilizing policy, offering a complementary approach suited to different data availability scenarios. Crucially, by decoupling the data-collection behavior policies from the policies being iteratively updated, both algorithms can learn equivalent cost weights from sufficiently excited trajectories without identifying the system dynamics or jump intensity. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [143] [The Clarke tangent and normal cones to decomposable sets in Lebesgue spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03195)
*Petar Evgeniev*

Main category: math.OC
