TL;DR: 本文研究了在完备σ-有限测度空间上，可测多值函数的Lp选择集（Sel_p(S)）的Clarke切锥是否等于逐点Clarke切锥的Lp选择集。主要结论是：当1≤p<∞且Z为自反Banach空间时，该等式成立；当p=∞时，仅得到包含关系。文章还讨论了Clarke法锥的相应结果，并应用于非光滑约束优化、Nemytskii算子和带分解约束的积分泛函极小化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究可测多值函数的Lp选择集的Clarke切锥结构，旨在建立"整体"切锥（无穷维函数空间中的切锥）与"逐点"切锥（底空间中的切锥）之间的联系，为处理具有可分解约束的无穷维优化问题提供理论基础。

Method: 利用可测多值函数理论、Clarke切锥的定义及性质、Lp空间分析技术，通过构造合适的逼近序列和可测选择，在Z自反的假设下证明等式关系；对于p=∞情形，通过反例和包含关系分析刻画差异。

Result: 对于1≤p<∞且Z自反，证明了 T_{Sel_p(S)}(x) = {v∈L^p(T,Z): v(t)∈T_{S(t)}(x(t)) a.e.}；对于p=∞，仅得包含关系T_{Sel_∞(S)}(x)⊂{v∈L^∞(T,Z): v(t)∈T_{S(t)}(x(t)) a.e.}；相应的Clarke法锥结果也成立。

Conclusion: 文章建立了可测多值函数选择集切锥的逐点刻画定理，为处理非光滑约束优化、Nemytskii算子和带分解约束的积分泛函极小化等应用问题提供了严格的数学工具。

Abstract: Let $(T,Σ,μ)$ be a complete, $σ$-finite measure space, let $Z$ be a separable Banach space, and let $S : T \rightrightarrows Z$ be a measurable multifunction with nonempty closed values. For $1 \leq p\leq \infty,$ we consider $$\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S):=\{z \in L^p(T,Z) : z(t) \in S(t) \hspace{0.1cm} \text{a.e.}\}.$$ We study whether the Clarke tangent cone to $\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S)$ is obtained by taking $L^p$-selections of the pointwise Clarke tangent cones to the values of $S;$ namely, whether $ \operatorname{\widehat{\textbf{T}}}_{\mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S)}(x)=\{v \in L^p(T,Z) : v(t) \in \operatorname{\widehat{\textbf{T}}}_{S(t)}(x(t)) \hspace{0.1cm} \text{a.e.}\} $ holds for $x \in \mathsf{Sel}_{p}(S).$ The main result gives an affirmative answer for $1 \leq p < \infty$ under the additional assumption that $Z$ is reflexive. If $p=\infty,$ we prove $\widehat{\mathbf T}_{\mathrm{Sel}_{\infty}(S)}(x)\subset \{v\in L^\infty(T,Z): v(t)\in \widehat{\mathbf T}_{S(t)}(x(t))\ \mathrm{a.e.}\}.$other possible partial results are discussed. Consequently the corresponding assertions for Clarke normal cones follow. We derive applications to nonsmooth constrained optimization problems, Nemytskii operators and minimization of integral functional with decomposable constraints.

</details>


### [144] [Adaptive Linear Quadratic Control of Unknown Linear Time-Varying Systems via Policy Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03251)
*Feiran Zhao,Florian Dörfler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略梯度自适应控制（PGAC）方法，用于未知线性时变（LTV）系统的控制。PGAC通过在每个时刻对线性二次型调节器（LQR）成本进行一步梯度下降来在线更新状态反馈策略，利用滑动窗口最小二乘法估计局部模型。针对慢时变和分段常数LTV系统，分别证明了实际指数稳定性和实际稳定性，并给出了平均冻结时间最优性差距界。数值案例验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常通过求解一次性优化问题来更新策略，计算量大且对噪声模型估计敏感。本文旨在提出一种计算轻量、对噪声鲁棒、且具有理论保证的自适应控制方法。

Method: 提出PGAC方法：在每个时刻对LQR成本进行一步策略梯度下降更新状态反馈策略；采用归一化滑动窗口最小二乘法从最近闭环轨迹中在线估计局部模型，从而显式计算策略梯度。

Result: 针对慢时变LTV系统证明了实际指数稳定性（无需驻留时间条件）；针对分段常数LTV系统通过驻留时间收缩论证建立了实际稳定性；给出了两类系统的平均冻结时间最优性差距界；在LTV和非线性系统上进行了数值验证。

Conclusion: PGAC方法在计算效率、噪声鲁棒性和理论保证方面优于传统方法，为LTV系统自适应控制提供了一种新的有效方案。

Abstract: Unknown linear time-varying (LTV) systems require the control policy to adapt from online closed-loop data as dynamics evolve. Existing methods usually update the policy by solving a one-shot optimization problem, which can be computationally demanding and sensitive to noisy model estimates. In this paper, we propose a policy gradient adaptive control (PGAC) method for LTV system control with unknown model parameters. Specifically, PGAC integrates online policy optimization into feedback by updating the state-feedback policy with one-step gradient descent of the linear quadratic regulator cost at each time instant. This incremental update is computationally light and naturally limits policy variations caused by noisy data. To explicitly compute the policy gradient online, we estimate local models from recent closed-loop trajectories using normalized sliding-window least-squares. We provide stability and convergence certificates of PGAC for two classes of LTV systems. For slowly time-varying systems, we prove that the closed-loop system achieves practical exponential stability without a dwell-time condition. For piecewise-constant LTV systems, we establish practical stability through a dwell-time contraction argument. We also provide average frozen-time optimality-gap bounds of the policy sequence for both classes. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method via numerical case studies of both LTV and nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [145] [Dynamic Congestion Pricing in Distribution Networks via a Convex-Analytic Bilevel Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03359)
*Reza Rahimi Baghbadorani,Ali Nikseresht,Jehum Cho,Yashar Ghiassi-Farrokhfal*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于凸分析框架的动态拥塞定价机制，利用Fenchel-Young等式和惩罚DC规划处理双层优化问题，避免传统KKT-大M混合整数方法的复杂性，在配电网上实现96%以上的拥塞削减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态拥塞定价是管理主动配电网拥塞和协调分布式能源的关键工具，但运营商与资源之间的双层交互结构使得可扩展且保护参与者自主性的机制在计算上具有挑战性。

Method: 提出凸分析框架：利用Fenchel-Young等式（涉及扩展跟随者目标的凸共轭）表示跟随者可行性与最优性；通过惩罚DC（difference-of-convex）重构和序列凸逼近处理剩余的双线性价格-响应项；求解连续凸子问题，避免约束互补和大M线性化。

Result: 在IEEE 13节点和34节点 feeders上，分别减少拥塞96.89%和96.45%；接近集中式全信息调度；价格-响应一致性达到数值精度；在计算预算内残差拥塞低于限时KKT方法。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了混合整数KKT重构的约束互补和分支定界扩展，主要计算瓶颈为价格-响应维度和共轭求值而非跟随者不等式的二进制编码，是一种可扩展的双层拥塞定价求解方案。

Abstract: Dynamic congestion pricing is an important tool for managing congestion and coordinating distributed energy resources in active distribution networks. However, scalable mechanisms that preserve participant autonomy remain computationally challenging because the operator-resource interaction is naturally bilevel. This paper develops a convex-analytic framework in which a distribution system operator computes dynamic congestion-price adders, while decentralized energy hubs schedule flexible demand, storage, local generation, renewable curtailment, and grid import/export. Unlike conventional single-level reformulations that replace lower-level problems by Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, complementarity constraints, and big-M linearizations, the proposed model represents follower feasibility and optimality through a Fenchel-Young equality involving the convex conjugate of an extended follower objective. The remaining bilinear price-response term is handled through a penalized difference-of-convex reformulation and sequential convex approximation. The method solves continuous convex subproblems and avoids the constraint-wise complementarity and branch-and-bound scaling of mixed-integer KKT reformulations; its main computational drivers are price-response dimension and conjugate evaluation rather than binary encodings of follower inequalities. On augmented IEEE 13- and 34-node feeders, it reduces congestion by 96.89% and 96.45%, respectively, approaches centralized full-information dispatch, certifies price-response consistency to numerical precision, and yields lower residual congestion than time-limited KKT incumbents within the computational budget.

</details>


### [146] [Optimal mean-time path planning for unmanned underwater vehicles: a Hamilton-Jacobi approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03407)
*Jonathan Valyou,Jeremy Brandman,Sanghyun Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集合海洋预报的无人水下航行器（UUV）最优平均时间路径规划方法，通过构建一组时间无关的Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程来融合预报不确定性，并扩展快速扫描法进行高效数值求解。实验表明，考虑不确定性的最优路径与基于单一确定性预报的路径存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际海洋预报存在不确定性，不同预报之间存在分歧，而传统路径规划假设使用确定的单一预报，无法处理这种不确定性对路径规划的影响。

Method: 将确定性最优路径规划扩展至集合预报场景，构建一组时间无关的Hamilton-Jacobi偏微分方程系统以融合预报不确定性，并采用扩展的快速扫描法进行高效数值求解。

Result: 验证和基准测试结果证明了方法的有效性；数值算例表明，考虑不确定性后的最优路径与基于各集合成员单独确定性预报得到的最优路径存在显著偏差。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理海洋预报不确定性，为UUV在不确定环境下的路径规划提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Unmanned underwater vehicles (UUV) integrate ocean forecasts with path planning algorithms in order to identify energy- or time-minimizing paths that enable mission completion. Typically, a well-defined deterministic ocean forecast is assumed to be available for path planning; however, in practice, different ocean forecasts can disagree. In this paper, we extend previous work on deterministic optimal path planning to identify optimal mean-time paths when presented with an ensemble of possible ocean forecasts. In particular, we formulate a system of time-independent Hamilton-Jacobi partial differential equations that incorporates forecast uncertainty and yields the optimal mean reachability travel time and the necessary controls to find the associated optimal path. An efficient numerical solution of this system of PDEs is obtained through an extension of the Fast Sweeping Method; verification and benchmarking results are provided. Additional numerical examples illustrate the impact uncertainty can have on the optimal path; in particular, these results demonstrate that the vehicle's optimal path can deviate significantly from the deterministic optimal paths associated with the individual ensemble members.

</details>


### [147] [Asynchronous Sensitivity-Based Distributed NMPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03412)
*Maximilian Pierer von Esch,Andres Völz,Knut Graichen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对非线性连续时间系统的异步协同分布式模型预测控制方案，通过固定次数的基于灵敏度的分布式编程迭代近似求解集中式最优控制问题，仅需邻接通信且无需代理间同步，在名义MPC稳定性和有界信息延迟下建立了局部指数稳定性，实验表明异步执行可减少60%以上的执行时间并保持相近的闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决分布式模型预测控制中严苛的同步要求和全局通信需求带来的计算与通信瓶颈，实现更高效、更具可扩展性的分布式控制方案。

Method: 提出基于灵敏度分析的分布式编程（SBDP）迭代方法，以固定迭代次数近似求解集中式最优控制问题；采用异步执行架构，代理间仅需邻接通信且无需同步；结合名义MPC稳定性与有界信息延迟假设进行理论分析。

Result: 通过以太网和Wi-Fi下的数值仿真与硬件在环实验验证，异步执行可减少超过60%的执行时间，同时闭环性能与同步方案相当；在足够多的局部SBDP迭代次数下建立了局部指数稳定性。

Conclusion: 异步执行机制能显著降低分布式MPC的计算与通信开销，且不牺牲稳定性与性能，为实际部署提供了理论保证和实验验证。

Abstract: This paper presents a cooperative distributed model predictive control (MPC) scheme for nonlinear continuous-time systems. The centralized optimal control problem is solved asynchronously via a fixed number of sensitivity-based distributed programming (SBDP) iterations. The proposed scheme requires only neighbor-to-neighbor communication and no synchronization between agents during optimization. Under nominal MPC stability and bounded information delay, local exponential stability is established for a sufficiently large number of per-agent SBDP iterations. Numerical and hardware-in-the-loop results on both Ethernet and Wi-Fi demonstrate the benefits of an asynchronous execution, reducing execution times by over 60% while maintaining comparable closed loop performance.

</details>


### [148] [On disjunction convex hulls for generalized cross polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03460)
*Yushan Qu,Jon Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了与 n+1 个多面体析取相关联的自然多面体 D，给出了当 n=1 且两个多面体为任意广义交叉多面体时 D 的完整描述，并刻画了何时该描述仅使用最优大 M 提升。对于 n>1，推广了面描述不等式族，并通过计算实验验证了理论结果的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继续推进对与一组多面体析取相关联的自然多面体 D 的完整刻画研究，此前已完成了 d∈{1,2} 以及超矩形推广情形的刻画，本文旨在将刻画推广到广义交叉多面体情形。

Method: 理论分析：对 n=1 且两个多面体为任意广义交叉多面体时给出 D 的完整描述，并刻画最优大 M 提升的条件；对 n>1 推广面描述不等式族；通过计算实验验证理论结果的价值。

Result: 完整刻画了 n=1 时两个任意广义交叉多面体情形下 D 的面描述；确定了何时该完整描述仅使用最优大 M 提升；对 n>1 情形推广了不等式族；计算实验证明了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究进一步扩展了对析取多面体 D 的完整刻画边界，为混合整数规划中的大 M 方法提供了更深入的理论基础，实验表明这些理论结果具有实际价值。

Abstract: We continue the study of the natural polytope $\mathcal{D}$ in $\mathbb{R}^{n+d}$ associated with the disjunction of a set of $n+1$ polytopes in $\mathbb{R}^d$, managed by $n$ binary variables. Already $\mathcal{D}$ had been characterized for arbitrary $n\geq 1$ and (i) $d\in\{1,2\}$, and (ii) for a broad generalization of hyper-rectangles. In both cases, the complete characterization employs full optimal big-M lifting. Here, we give a complete description of $\mathcal{D}$ for the case of $n=1$ and arbitrary $d$, when the (two) polytopes are arbitrary generalized cross polytopes. Furthermore, we characterize when our complete description employs only optimal big-M lifting. For $n>1$, we generalize the family of facet-describing inequalities used for $n=1$. Finally, we carry out some computational experiments demonstrating the value of our theoretical results.

</details>


### [149] [Regularized Projection Algorithms for Monotone Inverse Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03464)
*Griffin Smith,Zeinab Alizadeh,Afrooz Jalilzadeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一般单调随机逆变分不等式（SIVIs），提出了一种结合Tikhonov正则化与递增批大小的正则化投影算法，证明了迭代序列几乎必然有界且收敛到解集，并首次在一般单调性下建立了期望平方残差的非渐近收敛率O(T^{-1/2})，相应迭代复杂度为O(ε^{-2})，随机预言复杂度为O(ε^{-4-2δ})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解随机逆变分不等式的方法通常依赖于强单调性或余强制性假设，而对于更一般的单调SIVIs问题尚缺乏有效的理论分析与算法保证。

Method: 提出了一种正则化投影算法，该算法融合了Tikhonov正则化技术并采用递增的批大小策略，在单调性和Lipschitz连续性假设下进行分析。

Result: 证明了迭代序列几乎必然有界且距离SIVI解集的误差几乎必然收敛；首次在一般单调性下给出了期望平方残差的非渐近收敛率O(T^{-1/2})，得到迭代复杂度O(ε^{-2})和随机预言复杂度O(ε^{-4-2δ})（对任意δ>0）。确定性变体达到相同迭代复杂度，仅需O(ε^{-2})次精确算子求值。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效填补了单调SIVIs理论分析的空白，数值实验验证了其在单调SIVI问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic inverse variational inequalities (SIVIs) arise in applications in which equilibrium responses are observed under uncertainty, such as inverse road pricing and network equilibrium control. Existing methods typically rely on co-coercivity or strong monotonicity, while general monotone SIVIs remain less understood. We propose a regularized projection algorithm that combines Tikhonov regularization with increasing batch sizes. Under monotonicity and Lipschitz continuity, we prove almost sure boundedness of the iterates and almost sure convergence of their distance to the SIVI solution set. We further establish, to the best of our knowledge, the first explicit nonasymptotic rate of $O(T^{-1/2})$ for the expected squared residual under general monotonicity. This yields $O(ε^{-2})$ iterations and $O(ε^{-4-2δ})$ stochastic oracle calls, for any $δ>0$, to obtain an $ε$-solution in expected squared residual. A deterministic variant attains the same iteration complexity using $O(ε^{-2})$ exact operator evaluations. Numerical experiments illustrate the proposed methods on monotone SIVI problems.

</details>


### [150] [Rapid Concurrent GPU-CPU Solvers for Scalable Unit Commitment in Large Power Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03627)
*Hussein Sharadga,Yuhan Du,Javad Mohammadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GPU和CPU求解器并发执行的加速框架，通过先收敛者终止另一求解器的方式，结合激进预求解策略，将大规模电力系统机组组合问题的求解时间从42.12分钟降至5.77分钟（加速比2.14x-5.61x），在6049节点系统上验证了其可扩展性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电力系统机组组合问题计算复杂度高，传统单一求解器存在性能瓶颈，需要利用异构计算资源（GPU和CPU）的互补优势来加速求解过程。

Method: 在同一台机器上并发执行GPU和CPU优化求解器，率先收敛的求解器终止另一个以最小化总体运行时间，并结合系统性的激进预求解策略进一步加速收敛。

Result: 在6049节点系统（含数百万决策变量和约束）的45个测试案例上，最大运行时间从42.12分钟降至5.77分钟，加速比范围为2.14倍至5.61倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPU-CPU并发求解器框架能够有效结合异构计算资源的优势，显著提升大规模机组组合问题的求解速度，具有良好的可扩展性和实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents an accelerated solver for the unit commitment problem in large-scale power systems. The approach is based on the concurrent execution of GPU- and CPU-based optimization solvers on a single machine, with the solver that converges first terminating the other to minimize overall runtime. This strategy effectively harnesses the complementary strengths of different solvers. Convergence is further accelerated through a systematic and aggressive presolve approach. Numerical experiments on a 6,049-bus system with millions of decision variables and constraints demonstrate speedups ranging from 2.14x to 5.61x, reducing the maximum runtime from 42.12 minutes to 5.77 minutes across 45 test cases. These results highlight the scalability and computational efficiency of the proposed GPU-CPU concurrent solver framework.

</details>


### [151] [Dissipativity properties of a class of nonlinear time-delay systems via Bessel-Legendre inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03808)
*Ikram El Haskouki,Hannes Gernandt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用Bessel-Legendre不等式，针对一类具有多个离散时滞的非线性时滞系统，推导了时滞依赖和时滞无关的耗散性充分条件，并将线性系统的条件转化为可数值求解的线性矩阵不等式（LMI）以获取Lyapunov-Krasovskii型存储函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时滞普遍存在于物理和工程系统中，且会显著影响系统的稳定性和性能，因此有必要研究时滞系统的耗散性分析问题。

Method: 采用Bessel-Legendre不等式推导耗散性条件；对线性系统将条件转化为线性矩阵不等式（LMI）形式进行数值求解。

Result: 得到了时滞依赖和时滞无关的耗散性充分条件；线性系统的耗散性条件以LMI形式表达，可数值求解。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效分析了多离散时滞非线性系统的耗散性，且线性情形下可通过LMI求解Lyapunov-Krasovskii型存储函数。

Abstract: Time delays are inherent in many physical and engineered systems and can significantly affect their stability and performance. In this work, we investigate the dissipativity of a class of nonlinear time-delay systems with multiple discrete delays and derive sufficient conditions for both delay-dependent and delay-independent dissipativity using Bessel-Legendre inequalities. For linear systems, the resulting dissipativity conditions are expressed in terms of linear matrix inequalities (LMIs) which can be solved numerically to obtain Lyapunov-Krasovskii-type storage functions.

</details>


### [152] [Stability of input-output maps and their minimal realizations in state-linear, state-affine, LPV, and linear switched systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03849)
*Mihály Petreczky,Juan-Pablo Ortega,Florian Rossmannek,Bálint Daróczy*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了从输入-输出数据直接刻画稳定性的问题，证明了输入-输出族存在稳定有限维状态线性实现当且仅当它具有有限Hankel秩且响应随时间均匀衰减；对于状态线性可实现的映射，这种衰减必然是指数级的。并将结果扩展到状态仿射、LPV和线性切换系统，引入适当的输入遗忘概念，建立了遗忘与脉冲响应衰减（子马尔可夫参数）之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定性在学习和辨识中常被假设，但很少直接从输入-输出数据中刻画，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过Hankel秩分析、响应衰减特性、输入遗忘概念以及子马尔可夫参数衰减等方法，从理论上建立输入-输出数据与稳定性之间的直接联系。

Result: 证明了输入-输出族存在稳定有限维状态线性实现的充要条件；状态线性可实现的映射的衰减必然是指数级的；将结果扩展到了状态仿射、LPV和线性切换系统；建立了遗忘与脉冲响应衰减的关系。

Conclusion: 所有情形下，衰减/遗忘率决定了每个最小实现的衰减率，为从数据直接刻画系统稳定性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Stability is often assumed in learning and identification, yet it is rarely characterized directly from input--output data. We show that an input--output family admits a stable finite-dimensional state-linear realization iff it has finite Hankel-rank and its response decays uniformly with time; for state-linear realizable maps this decay is necessarily exponential. We extend these results to state-affine, LPV, and linear switched systems via suitable input-forgetting notions, and relate forgetting to decay of impulse responses (sub-Markov parameters). In all cases, the decay/forgetting rate determines the decay rate of every minimal realization.

</details>


### [153] [Course timetabling under conflict and lecturer preferences using bi-objective optimization model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03901)
*Murat Elhüseyni*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带有多种实际学术约束的大学课程 timetabling 问题，提出一个双目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，通过加权求和法优化课程冲突与教师日程紧凑性之间的权衡。基于真实数据集的实验表明，该模型相比实际排课方案减少了13.33%的冲突，提升了26%的教师日程紧凑度，并完全消除了理论与实践课节顺序违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的课程排课问题往往涉及多种复杂约束（如理论课先于实践课、教师休息时段、教师与助教同时分配等），传统手工排课难以同时满足这些要求并优化多项目标，亟需一种高效的数学规划方法来解决这一实际需求。

Method: 构建了一个双目标 MILP 模型，目标函数包括最小化课程冲突和最大化日程紧凑性（即减少教师的分散工作日），约束条件涵盖理论-实践课顺序、教师休息期、教师与助教联合分配以及每日工作时长限制；通过加权求和法将双目标转化为单目标求解。

Result: 使用真实大学排课数据集进行实验，结果表明：课程冲突减少13.33%，教师日程紧凑度提升26%，理论-实践课节顺序违规完全消除。

Conclusion: 所提出的 MILP 模型在处理复杂大学排课问题中表现出高效性和鲁棒性，能够显著提升排课质量，为实际应用提供了可行且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we address a real-life course timetabling problem that incorporates several practical academic requirements, including the chronological precedence of theoretical sessions over practical ones, the enforcement of rest periods for lecturers, and the simultaneous assignment of lecturers and teaching assistants. The formulation involves a trade-off between minimizing course conflicts and maximizing schedule compactness by reducing the number of dispersed working days for lecturers under strict daily working hour constraints. To handle this trade-off, we develop a bi-objective mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model solved using a weighted-sum objective approach. Computational experiments using a real-world dataset demonstrate that the proposed model reduces timetable conflicts by 13.33%, improves lecturer schedule compactness by 26%, and completely eliminates violations of theoretical-practical session precedence compared to the real-life timetable. Overall, the findings highlight the efficiency and robustness of the proposed MILP model for complex university timetabling problems.

</details>


### [154] [L1 Optimal Control of Continuous-Time Stochastic Positive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03952)
*Alba Gurpegui,Takashi Tanaka,Anders Rantzer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类具有线性非负成本的L1最优控制问题，该问题受乘性Itô扩散过程影响且带有元素级线性输入约束。建立了随机动态下正象限的前向不变性，并提出了一种符合该不变性的模拟方法。考虑了有限时域和折扣无限时域两种随机L1最优控制问题，这些问题可通过向量值常微分方程（有限时域）或代数方程（无限时域）显式求解。最优值函数和反馈策略与对应确定性问题一致，表现出对乘性随机不确定性的鲁棒性。通过投资组合示例验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有乘性随机不确定性和线性输入约束的L1最优控制问题，并探索其显式解结构及与确定性问题的关系。

Method: 利用正象限前向不变性理论处理随机动态系统，提出一致模拟方法；通过向量值ODE（有限时域）和代数方程（无限时域）推导显式解。

Result: 最优值函数和反馈策略与对应确定性问题的解一致，表明对乘性随机不确定性具有鲁棒性；投资组合示例验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提出的L1最优控制框架在乘性随机环境下仍保持与确定性情形相同的显式解结构，为正象限约束下的随机控制问题提供了高效求解途径。

Abstract: We present an L1-optimal control problem class with linear nonnegative costs subject to multiplicative Itô diffusion processes with elementwise linear input constraints. Forward invariance of the positive orthant is established for the considered stochastic dynamics, and a simulation method consistent with this invariance property is proposed. Both finite-horizon and discounted infinite-horizon stochastic L1-optimal control problems are considered. These problems admit explicit solutions characterized by a vector-valued ordinary differential equation in the finite-horizon case and by an algebraic equation in the infinite-horizon case. Notably, the optimal value function and feedback policy coincide with those of the corresponding deterministic problem, demonstrating robustness to multiplicative stochastic uncertainty. A portfolio example illustrates our results.

</details>


### [155] [A Static Mean Field Game for Optimal Renewable Energy Investment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03976)
*Deniz Sezer,Shanukie Vithana,Sara Hastings-Simon,Souren Iwazian*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了静态平均场博弈（MFG）模型，用于优化加拿大阿尔伯塔省的风能容量选址。模型基于风资源及其空间协方差构建收益函数，通过PCA与参数化残差核校准空间依赖结构，将均衡投资问题转化为二次规划求解，并分析了四种政策情景（逐步限制选址区域）对经济效率和空间分布的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化政策和基础设施约束（如景观视线和输电限制）对风能投资空间分布和经济效率的影响，揭示监管用地目标与可再生能源经济效率之间的权衡。

Method: 采用静态平均场博弈（MFG）框架，构建基于风资源期望及其空间协方差的收益模型；利用PCA结合参数化残差核校准空间风依赖协方差结构；将均衡选址问题转化为二次规划求解，并通过四种政策情景（逐步增加选址约束）进行对比分析。

Result: 不同政策情景下的MFG均衡结果显示了用地限制与经济效率之间的短期与长期权衡，定量揭示了政策设计如何影响风电投资的 spatial distribution（空间分布）和财务表现。

Conclusion: 静态MFG公式可为输电规划和可再生能源开发规划等电力系统规划提供决策支持。

Abstract: We develop a static mean field game formulation for optimal wind energy capacity siting in Alberta, Canada. The revenue model for agents is based on the expected wind resource at a location and the covariance of this wind resource with other locations. Agents choose locations to maximize long-run revenue while accounting for spatial correlations in wind resource availability. Spatial wind dependence is incorporated through an empirically calibrated covariance structure, combining a PCA-based component estimated from historical weather station data with a parametric residual kernel, to capture variability across locations. The equilibrium investment problem can be formulated as a quadratic program, which we solve for four policy scenarios that progressively restrict the feasible siting area, incorporating viewscape and transmission constraints beyond a baseline of minimal siting restrictions. These policy and infrastructure-driven land use restrictions are represented as constraints on the agents' action space, allowing us to examine their economic implications through a comparison of the MFG equilibria across policy scenarios.Our findings highlight the trade-off between regulatory land use objectives and economic efficiency in the use of renewable energy resources in the short and long term, and provide quantitative insights into how policy design shapes the spatial distribution and financial performance of wind investments. The static MFG formulation could be used to inform electricity system planning including, transmission planning and renewable energy development planning.

</details>


### [156] [Stochastic control with signatures via Riccati equations on the tensor algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03986)
*Eduardo Abi Jaber,Elie Attal,Dimitri Sotnikov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用路径签名方法，在半显式形式下求解了一类具有路径依赖奖励的非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，通过Boué-Dupuis表示将控制问题转化为签名泛函的拉普拉斯变换计算，并在扩展张量代数上建立了无限维Riccati方程组，得到了最优控制与价值过程的显式反馈表示，并提出了动态重校正算法以实现全局表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机最优控制方法（如动态规划）通常要求系统具有马尔可夫性，难以处理路径依赖奖励和非马尔可夫动力学。作者旨在突破这一局限，利用路径签名这一数学工具为更广泛的实际问题提供可计算的解决方案。

Method: 首先通过Boué-Dupuis表示将原控制问题转化为签名泛函的拉普拉斯变换计算；然后利用张量代数上的签名表示，将问题转化为扩展张量代数上的无限维Riccati方程组；最后提出动态重校正算法，通过局部展开的迭代更新实现全局表示。

Result: 得到了最优控制和价值过程的显式反馈表示，形式为受控过程时间扩展签名的无限线性组合（系数随时间变化）。在跟踪签名线性泛函和Volterra控制问题的签名提升等非马尔可夫、非线性实例上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将路径签名理论应用于非马尔可夫随机最优控制问题，拓展了超出传统线性-二次型框架的可解问题类别，为处理具有记忆效应的随机控制问题提供了新的理论框架和计算范式。

Abstract: We solve in semi-explicit form a class of non-Markovian stochastic optimal control problems with path-dependent rewards, using path signatures. We reformulate the control problem as the computation of Laplace transforms of signature functionals thanks to the Boué-Dupuis representation. Exploiting recent signature representations of such transforms on tensor algebras, we determine the value process and the optimal control through an infinite-dimensional system of Riccati equations on the extended tensor algebra. We establish an explicit feedback representation of the optimal control and the value process as an infinite linear combination of the time-extended signature of the controlled process, with time-dependent coefficients. The expansions being intrinsically local, we propose a dynamic recentering algorithm to ensure a global representation over the entire time horizon. We illustrate the approach on genuinely path-dependent, non-linear examples that go beyond the tractable linear-quadratic setting, including the tracking of linear functionals of the signature and signature lifts of Volterra control problems.

</details>


### [157] [Finite-Sample Closed-Loop Stability of Model Predictive Path Integral Control for Linear Time-Invariant Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04006)
*Hyung-Jin Yoon,Hunmin Kim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制在离散时间线性时不变（LTI）系统（含高斯过程扰动）中的有限样本闭环稳定性保证。通过揭示MPPI可视为LQR的随机扰动，推导了近似误差界（蒙特卡洛项+温度偏置项），并利用Lyapunov扰动论证证明了实际指数稳定性，给出了显式的充分样本阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPPI作为一种有效的采样型模型预测控制方法，其有限样本下的闭环稳定性理论保证尚不充分。本文旨在为MPPI控制提供严格的有限样本稳定性分析，填补理论空白。

Method: 1) 证明无约束LTI/二次型系统中，有限时域MPC与无限时域LQR的首步控制律一致；2) 将有限样本MPPI建模为LQR的随机扰动，分解近似误差为蒙特卡洛项（随样本数递减）和温度偏置项（随温度降低消失）；3) 采用Lyapunov扰动论证推导实际指数稳定性，给出显式样本阈值条件。

Result: MPPI控制律以高概率逼近LQR反馈，近似误差由样本数和温度决定。系统在实际指数稳定意义下，期望状态范数指数衰减至三个残差底限（过程噪声底限、MPPI近似底限、采样失败置信底限）。充分样本阈值由DARE解、LQR稳定裕度、采样参数、温度及规划时域显式计算。

Conclusion: 在无限样本和温度偏置消失的联合极限下，MPPI稳定性恢复为随机LQR稳定性界。该工作为MPPI提供了首个有限样本闭环稳定性保证，理论结果可参数化于规划时域选择。

Abstract: We establish finite-sample closed-loop stability guarantees for Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control applied to discrete-time Linear Time-Invariant (LTI) systems with additive Gaussian process disturbances. The key observation is that, for unconstrained LTI/quadratic systems with the DARE terminal cost, the exact finite-horizon MPC law has the same first control action as the infinite-horizon LQR law for every planning horizon. Thus, finite-sample MPPI can be analyzed as a stochastic perturbation of LQR. First, we show that the MPPI control law approximates the LQR feedback with high probability. The approximation error decomposes into a Monte Carlo term that decreases with the sample count and an infinite-sample temperature bias that persists at finite temperature but vanishes as the temperature is reduced. The resulting constants are written in terms of the horizon-dependent stacked cost matrices, making explicit that the finite-sample certificate is parametrized by the selected planning horizon. Second, we use a Lyapunov perturbation argument to prove practical exponential stability in expectation. On sample paths that remain in a compact Lyapunov sublevel set over a finite operating horizon, the expected state norm decays exponentially up to three residual floors: a process-noise floor, an MPPI approximation floor, and a confidence floor from the per-step sampling failure probability. The sufficient sample threshold is explicit and computable from the DARE solution, LQR stability margin, MPPI sampling parameters, temperature, and planning horizon. In the joint limit of infinite samples and vanishing temperature bias, the result recovers the stochastic LQR stability bound.

</details>


### [158] [Sequential Pricing with Deadlines and Correlated Buyers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04027)
*Shruti Penumatsa,Rajan Udwani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了截止日期约束下的序列定价问题，提出首个面向多种场景（确定性/随机截止日期、独立/相关估值）的收入最大化近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管截止日期约束下的序列定价具有重要实践意义，但现有算法研究非常有限，仅覆盖了特殊情形。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究采用序列定价模型：卖家依次向买家提供"要么接受要么放弃"的价格，当有买家接受或截止时间到达时停止。考虑了确定性截止日期和随机截止日期两种情形，并允许买家估值相互独立或任意相关。

Result: 针对上述多种截止日期约束下的序列定价模型，首次提出了收入最大化的近似算法。

Conclusion: 本文为截止日期约束下的序列定价问题提供了首个通用的近似算法框架，填补了该领域在算法研究上的空白。

Abstract: We study sequential posted pricing for selling a single item under an exogenous deadline: a seller makes take-it-or-leave-it price offers to buyers one at a time, stopping when some buyer accepts or the selling opportunity expires. We consider both deterministic deadlines and random deadlines drawn from a known distribution, and allow buyer valuations to be either independent or arbitrarily correlated. Despite its practical relevance, this collection of deadline-constrained sequential pricing models has received limited algorithmic study beyond special cases. We develop the first approximation algorithms for revenue maximization across these settings.

</details>


### [159] [Fast, Parallel, Query-Efficient Binary Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04062)
*Ishani Karmarkar,Liam O'Carroll,Aaron Sidford*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对二分类问题中的最大间隔分离超平面计算，提出了两种随机化算法，分别实现了$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2(ω+1)/3})$的串行时间与$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$的并行深度，以及$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2})$的串行时间与$\tilde{O}(γ^{-4/3})$的并行深度，两者均匹配近最优的确定性矩阵-向量查询复杂度，但以随机性换取更优的运行时与并行深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决二分类中计算最大间隔分离超平面的基础问题，旨在降低串行运行时间和并行深度，同时保持接近最优的矩阵-向量查询复杂度。

Method: 提出两种随机化算法：第一种在$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2(ω+1)/3})$串行时间和$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$并行深度内求解，仅需$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$次矩阵-向量查询；第二种以$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2})$串行时间和$\tilde{O}(γ^{-4/3})$并行深度运行，同样使用$\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$次矩阵-向量查询。

Result: 两种算法均匹配了Kornowski和Shamir [2025]、Karmarkar等人 [2026]最近建立的近最优确定性matvec复杂度，并在串行运行时间和并行深度上取得改进。

Conclusion: 通过引入随机性，可以在不牺牲矩阵-向量查询复杂度的前提下，显著提升串行运行时间和并行深度，证明了计算效率与查询复杂度之间的权衡可以通过随机化实现优化。

Abstract: We study the fundamental classification problem of computing a separating hyperplane for a binary-labeled dataset of size $n$ with normalized $d$-dimensional features. Letting $Φ\in \mathbb{R}^{n \times d}$ denote the feature matrix and $γ$ the margin of the maximum-margin separating hyperplane, we present a randomized algorithm that solves this problem in $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2(ω+1)/3})$-sequential running time (work), $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-parallel (computational) depth, and accesses $Φ$ only through $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-matrix-vector queries (matvecs). We also present a second, faster randomized algorithm with a $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3}\, \operatorname{nnz}(Φ) + γ^{-2})$-sequential running time that uses $\tilde{O}(γ^{-2/3})$-matvecs to $Φ$, but achieves only $\tilde{O}(γ^{-4/3})$-parallel depth. Both algorithms match the near-optimal deterministic matvec complexity recently established by Kornowski and Shamir [2025], Karmarkar et al. [2026] and achieve improved sequential runtime and parallel depth, albeit at the expense of using randomness.

</details>


### [160] [Robust Receding Horizon Games with Additive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04213)
*Dinesh Patra,Tanish Jain,Ashish R. Hota*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于管状约束收紧的鲁棒博弈控制框架，结合有限时域广义纳什均衡问题与DARE终端代价，保证了递归可行性和名义状态渐近收敛到稳态变分广义纳什均衡，实际状态收敛到其邻域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式/多智能体控制方法在处理存在有界扰动、私有约束和共享耦合约束的线性系统时，缺乏同时保证鲁棒递归可行性和纳什均衡收敛的理论框架。

Method: 1) 采用管状模型预测控制（tube-based MPC）思想进行约束收紧；2) 将问题建模为有限时域广义纳什均衡问题（GNEP）；3) 基于离散代数Riccati方程（DARE）设计终端代价函数；4) 利用解耦的正向不变终端集；5) 利用跟踪代价诱导的势博弈结构进行收敛性分析。

Result: 1) 对任意有界扰动实现递归可行性；2) 名义状态渐近收敛到稳态vGNE；3) 实际状态收敛到由最小鲁棒正向不变集决定的vGNE邻域。

Conclusion: 该框架为存在有界扰动的多智能体线性系统提供了一种兼具鲁棒递归可行性和纳什均衡收敛保证的博弈控制方法。

Abstract: We study a receding horizon game in which multiple agents drive linear systems subject to additive disturbances, private state and input constraints, and shared coupling constraints. We propose a robust game-theoretic control framework that combines tube-based constraint tightening with a finite-horizon generalized Nash equilibrium problem (GNEP), equipped with a discrete algebraic Riccati equation (DARE)-based terminal cost and a decoupled positively invariant terminal set. The framework guarantees recursive feasibility for every bounded disturbance realization. Exploiting the potential-game structure induced by tracking costs, we further establish asymptotic convergence of each agent's nominal state to a steady-state variational generalized Nash equilibrium (vGNE), and show that each agent's actual state converges to a neighborhood of the vGNE determined by the minimal robust positively invariant set.

</details>


### [161] [Anytime Plug-and-Play Control with Contract-Based Distributed MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04215)
*Sabrina Bodmer,Danilo Saccani,Melanie N. Zeilinger,Andrea Carron*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局部通信的分布式多智能体控制算法，支持智能体随时加入或离开网络，无需集中协调，通过距离邻居定义和预测轨迹合同约束保障避碰，在高速动态多车场景中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人移动应用中通信拓扑随时间动态变化，智能体可能随时加入或退出网络，现有方法难以处理这种非先验可约束的动态变化，亟需一种无需集中协调的分布式控制方案。

Method: 基于局部通信的分布式控制算法：①采用距离邻居定义实现高效扩展；②利用预测轨迹导出合同约束，保障碰撞避免和约束满足；③支持智能体任意时刻加入/离开，无需全局重规划。

Result: 在自动驾驶多智能体场景中，通过仿真和真实实验验证了该方法在高速、动态、对向行驶环境下的有效碰撞避免能力。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式合同约束方法能够有效应对通信拓扑的动态变化，在无集中协调条件下实现可扩展的多智能体安全控制。

Abstract: A central challenge in many mobile multi-robot applications is that communication topologies are inherently time-varying. Agents may enter or exit the network and such changes cannot generally be restricted a priori. This work introduces a distributed multi-agent control algorithm based on local communication that supports anytime agent joining and leaving the communication network without centralized coordination. The method scales efficiently with the number of agents by relying on a distance-based neighbor definition and on contracts derived from predicted trajectories. The resulting contract constraints guarantee collision avoidance and constraint satisfaction. We validate the proposed method in an autonomous multi-agent driving scenario, demonstrating effective collision avoidance in high-speed, dynamic environments with agents moving in opposite directions, in both simulated and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [162] [Unified convergence analysis for gradient descent optimization methods in the training of deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04233)
*Shokhrukh Ibragimov,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为具有解析激活函数（如softplus和GeLU）的深度神经网络训练中一大类基于梯度的优化方法（包括GD、动量、NAG、RMSprop、Adam、Adamax、Nadam、Nadamax、Adan、AdaBelief、AMSGrad、Yogi等）提供了统一的收敛性分析框架，利用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz不等式证明了这些方法收敛到临界点，其中Adam情形的分析本身也是新贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中，针对各类自适应/加速梯度优化方法（如Adam等）在深度神经网络训练中的收敛性分析缺乏统一的理论框架，且即使在Adam这一特例下也缺少相关收敛性结果。

Method: 利用Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (KL) 不等式理论，对具有解析激活函数（softplus、GeLU）的DNN训练中一大类基于梯度的优化方法建立统一的收敛性分析框架。

Result: 成功为GD、动量、NAG、RMSprop、Adam、Adamax、Nadam、Nadamax、Adan、AdaBelief、AMSGrad、Yogi等12种优化方法提供了统一的收敛性证明，证明了这些方法在训练中均收敛到临界点。

Conclusion: 本文建立了深度神经网络训练中多种梯度优化方法的统一收敛性理论，即使在Adam这一特例下也属于首次贡献，填补了相关理论空白。

Abstract: Gradient based optimization methods are nowadays the methods of choice for training deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. In practically relevant DNN training problems, one does usually not apply the standard gradient descent (GD) optimization method but instead one employs suitable sophisticated GD optimization methods, which incorporate adaptivity and/or acceleration techniques, such as the famous Adam optimizer. It is a key contribution of this work to provide a general unified convergence analysis for GD optimization methods in the training of DNNs with analytic activations such as the softplus and the popular Gaussian error linear unit (GeLU) activation. Our general unified convergence result applies to a large class of gradient based optimization methods such as the standard GD, the momentum, the Nesterov accelerated gradient (NAG), the RMSprop, the Adam, the Adamax, the Nadam, the Nadamax, the Adan, the AdaBelief, the AMSGrad, and the Yogi optimizers. Our analysis employs the theory of Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (KL) inequalities to establish convergence to critical points in the training of DNNs. To the best of our knowledge, the generality of our convergence analysis is also just in the special situation of the Adam optimizer a new contribution to the literature on the analysis of AI optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [163] [Markov Information Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04308)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究承诺能力的设计者如何为战略性交互、有远见的代理人设计信息结构，其中代理人行为驱动一个持久、受控的马尔可夫状态。引入马尔可夫贝叶斯相关均衡（Markov BCE），通过动态服从条件刻画最优信息设计，并证明该问题可递归求解，在LQG情形下简化为代数Riccati方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当存在持久性状态转移且代理人具有前瞻性时，静态信息设计方法不再适用。本文旨在将信息设计理论扩展到受控马尔可夫动态环境，为设计者如何最优地影响具有战略交互和远见能力的代理人的信息环境提供理论基础。

Method: 引入Markov BCE概念，推广Bergemann和Morris(2016)的BCE至受控马尔可夫环境；提出动态服从条件（含延续价值项）；建立以代理人承诺延续效用为状态变量的递归求解框架；采用集值逆向归纳算法求解；对LQG情形推导协方差条件及代数Riccati方程；分析代理人学习转移动态时的信息租金。

Result: Markov BCE的最优值介于无披露和第一最优值之间；LQG情形下服从条件转化为修正交互矩阵的协方差条件，稳态解满足代数Riccati方程；代理人拥有比设计者更精确的动态模型时获得非负信息租金，在持续激励下累积租金呈对数增长。

Conclusion: 本文为动态环境下的信息设计提供了统一的框架和可操作的方法，通过两个实例（拥堵和资源协调）及数值研究验证了理论的有效性。

Abstract: We study information design when a designer with commitment shapes the information of strategically interacting, far-sighted agents whose actions drive a persistent, controlled Markov state. We introduce the Markov Bayes correlated equilibrium (Markov BCE), the controlled-Markov generalisation of the BCE of Bergemann and Morris (2016), characterised by a dynamic obedience condition that adds a continuation-value term to the static one and reduces to it when actions cannot move the state. Recommending actions is without loss; the designer's problem is recursive in the agents' promised continuation utilities and is solved by a set-valued backward-induction algorithm whose optimum exists and lies between the no-disclosure and first-best values. For linear-quadratic-Gaussian payoffs the obedience condition becomes a covariance condition with a modified interaction matrix, and the stationary case reduces to an algebraic Riccati equation. When agents instead learn the transition, we identify the rent an agent earns from a model of the dynamics sharper than the designer anticipates: it is non-negative, zero at the known-dynamics benchmark, and deterred only by building slack into obedience. Under persistent excitation the cumulative rent grows logarithmically as heterogeneous agents' estimates converge. Two worked examples, in congestion and resource coordination, together with a numerical study illustrate the theory.

</details>


### [164] [Exploiting Variable Implications in Presolve for Mixed Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04313)
*Wei-Kun Chen,Chang-Long Li,Zhao-Wei Wang,Yu-Hong Dai,Zi-Shuo Li,Meng Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种利用变量蕴含关系（VIs）的MIP预求解新技术：VI聚合（将多个VIs聚合成单一不等式）和VI感知线性约束传播（在标准LCP基础上结合VIs推导更紧的变量界），实验表明在HiGHS求解器上实现了求解时间减少4%、节点数减少6%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIP预求解旨在消除冗余信息、加强模型并提取有用结构信息（如变量蕴含关系VIs），但现有方法对VIs的利用不充分，缺乏系统性地利用VIs来推导约简的技术。

Method: 1) VI聚合：利用形成团（clique）的二进制变量集合与某变量之间的蕴含关系，将多个VIs聚合成一个不等式，减少约束数量并收紧LP松弛。2) VI感知线性约束传播（VI-aware LCP）：在标准LCP基础上引入与待收紧变量相关的VIs，推导更多约简并得到更紧的变量界，且保持线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在MIPLIB 2017基准测试集上，两种技术集成到HiGHS后，求解时间平均减少4%，搜索节点数平均减少6%。

Conclusion: 提出的VI聚合和VI感知LCP能有效提升MIP求解器性能，且VI感知LCP在推导变量界方面优于现有前沿方法。

Abstract: Presolve for mixed integer programming (MIP) problems aims to eliminate redundant information, strengthen the formulation, and extract useful structural information for the subsequent branch-and-cut process. An important type of such structural information is the variable implications (VIs), which describe how a bound on a variable depends on a bound of a binary variable. In this paper, we develop two new presolve techniques that exploit VIs to derive reductions for MIP problems. The first technique, called VI aggregation, aggregates multiple VIs into a single inequality by using implications between a variable and a set of binary variables that form a clique. This aggregation can reduce the number of constraints and tighten the linear programming relaxation. The second technique, called VI-aware linear constraint propagation (LCP), builds on the standard LCP but incorporates VIs associated with the variable being tightened to derive more reductions and can derive tighter variable bounds. We show that although VI information is additionally considered, the tightest lower or upper bound of a variable can still be derived in linear time. Moreover, compared with a state-of-the-art approach in the literature, the proposed VI-aware LCP can derive tighter variable bounds. Computational results on MIPLIB 2017 benchmark instances demonstrate the effectiveness of VI aggregation and VI-aware LCP in improving the performance of the open-source MIP solver HiGHS. In particular, using the two proposed presolve techniques, a reduction of 4% in solving time and 6% in node number on HiGHS can be achieved.

</details>


### [165] [An open, reproducible branch-and-cut for the capacitated profitable tour problem: a component study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04497)
*Simon Spoorendonk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于全开源MIP栈（HiGHS）的可复现分支切割算法，用于求解带容量约束的盈利巡游问题（CPTP）及其开放s-t路径变体。通过组件消融实验发现，容量类割平面贡献了几乎全部收益，而梳状割、舍入广义大星形割、降成本定界和基于界的传播未带来可测量改进，最短路径不兼容割（SPI）在所有实例中均未发现违反不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题类的现有分支切割算法依赖于商业求解器（如CPLEX），导致结果难以复现和扩展。作者旨在提供一个完全开放、可复现的基线求解器，以便后续研究可以直接使用和对比。

Method: 在HiGHS开源MIP栈上重新实现了Jepsen et al. (2014)的公式和割族，并增加了基于界的预处理、域传播和降成本变量固定。通过组件消融实验分解各模块贡献，并以动态规划/标记算法为基准进行对比。

Result: 容量类割平面将求解实例数从52提升至64（共76个），搜索树规模缩小10倍以上；梳状割、舍入广义大星形割、降成本固定和基于界传播无额外收益；SPI割在所有实例中均未发现违反不等式。

Conclusion: 容量类割平面是CPTP分支切割算法的核心有效组件，其他几类经典割平面在该问题上的实际贡献有限。开源基线求解器为后续研究提供了可复现的起点。

Abstract: We present an open, reproducible branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm for the capacitated profitable tour problem (CPTP) and its open s-t path variant, the capacity-constrained elementary shortest-path problem. The solver re-implements the formulation and cut families of Jepsen et al. (2014) on a fully open mixed-integer programming stack (HiGHS; Huangfu and Hall, 2018), and adds bound-based preprocessing, domain propagation, and reduced-cost variable fixing. We claim no new method; the contribution is twofold. First, an open, reproducible artifact: to our knowledge the first branch-and-cut for this problem class on a fully open stack, with the formulation, every separator, and all benchmark scripts released, so the results below can be rerun and the solver reused and extended as a baseline. Second, a component study on this common modern stack, benchmarked against a dynamic-programming/labelling reference, that decomposes which components pay off and where the running time goes. We find that the capacity-class cuts account for essentially the entire benefit (adding them to a connectivity-only baseline lifts the number of instances solved from 52 to 64 of 76 and shrinks the search tree more than tenfold), while comb and rounded generalized-large-multistar cuts, reduced-cost fixing, and bound-based propagation add nothing measurable. We also report a negative result: the shortest-path-incompatibility (SPI) cut, a variant of the node-precedence inequalities of García (2009), finds no violated inequality on any instance. The solver and all experiments are released as open, reproducible software (Spoorendonk, 2026).

</details>


### [166] [Cooperation in Conic Programming with Applications to Control, Production, and Portfolio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04499)
*Miguel Martínez-Antón,Justo Puerto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了合作锥博弈（cooperative conic games）这一新框架，将基于优化的合作博弈统一在锥优化问题下，利用锥对偶理论导出核心非空的条件与分配方法，并通过多智能体最优控制、非线性生产博弈、投资组合选择及组合优化博弈等应用展示其广泛适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的合作博弈（如线性生产博弈）缺乏统一数学框架，且核心非空性的验证通常依赖组合枚举，计算代价高。本文旨在利用锥优化的结构优势，统一刻画此类博弈并提供可计算的核心分配条件。

Method: 定义合作锥博弈的特征函数为锥优化问题的最优值；利用锥对偶理论推导核心非空的一般性条件，并证明最优对偶解可直接构造核心分配；通过多个代表性应用（多智能体最优控制、Cobb-Douglas生产、Markowitz/CVaR/熵风险投资组合、组合优化博弈）验证框架的表达能力。

Result: 建立了核心非空性的充要条件（基于锥对偶间隙为零及强对偶成立）；核心分配可通过求解对偶问题直接计算，无需组合验证；统一了线性锥、二阶锥、半定锥等多种锥类下的合作博弈模型。

Conclusion: 合作锥博弈框架将数学规划与合作博弈论深度结合，为多智能体合作决策提供了普适且可计算的工具，未来可进一步推广至更一般的凸优化博弈及非凸情形。

Abstract: We introduce the class of cooperative conic games, a new family of transferable utility games whose characteristic function can be computed by solving a conic optimization problem. This framework unifies a broad range of optimization-based cooperative games within a common mathematical formulation, encompassing linear, second-order cone, semidefinite, and other convex nonlinear optimization problems admitting conic representations. Exploiting the structural properties of conic programs and conic duality theory, we derive general conditions ensuring the existence of core payoff allocations (or cost shares) and show how such allocations can be computed directly from optimal dual solutions. In particular, the proposed methodology replaces combinatorial verification procedures for core nonemptiness with tractable optimization-based criteria. The expressive power of the framework is illustrated through several representative applications. We show that it models cooperation in multi-agent optimal control, generalizes linear production games to nonlinear production settings such as Cobb--Douglas models, introduces cooperative formulations for portfolio selection under Markowitz, conditional value-at-risk, and entropic risk measures, and unifies a broad class of combinatorial optimization games through exact conic reformulations. These examples involve the most relevant cones in modern convex optimization, highlighting both the versatility of the proposed framework and its potential to strengthen the interaction between mathematical programming and cooperative game theory.

</details>


### [167] [Constrained Flow Matching via Lagrangian Dual Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04513)
*Vince Kurtz,Alexander Davydov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出拉格朗日对偶流（Lagrangian Dual Flows），通过在去噪过程中引入对偶协态变量，实现对复杂非线性约束的满足，无需昂贵的优化子问题或投影步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配模型在机器人、规划和物理等领域的应用需要在推理时满足复杂的非线性约束，而现有方法（如图像修复的线性约束方法、投影或优化替代方案）要么不足以处理非线性约束，要么计算成本过高。

Method: 基于拉格朗日对偶动力学，在生成样本的同时流式更新一个对偶协态变量，将约束满足问题转化为对偶变量的动态调整过程，避免了去噪过程中需要求解优化子问题、伪逆或投影步骤。

Result: 提出的约束生成算法简单、有效，能够在无需昂贵计算的前提下保证非线性约束的满足。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了流匹配与数值优化中原始-对偶方法之间的新理论联系，为复杂约束下的生成建模提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Flow matching is a powerful tool for generative modeling, but emerging applications in robotics, planning, and physics require inference-time constraints on generated outputs. Such constraints are often complex and highly nonlinear. As a result, methods designed for linear constraints like image inpainting are rarely sufficient, and projection or optimization-based alternatives can be prohibitively expensive. In this paper, we introduce Lagrangian Dual Flows, a new family of constrained generation techniques based on Lagrangian dual dynamics. By simply flowing a dual co-state alongside generated samples, we can guarantee nonlinear constraint satisfaction without expensive optimization subproblems, pseudoinverses, or projection steps during the denoising process. The resulting constrained generation algorithms are simple, effective, and open new theoretical connections between flow matching and primal-dual methods in numerical optimization.

</details>


### [168] [Rockafellian relaxation and minimum-norm slack for the Walrasian equilibrium problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04717)
*Julio Deride*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Rockafellian松弛方法来处理可能不存在均衡的交换经济中的Walras均衡问题。通过引入非负变量v来松弛市场出清条件并惩罚其范数，使得松弛问题始终适定。随着惩罚增大，残差收敛到超额需求可行范围内最小范数的向量v*∞，衡量了到最近均衡存在经济的距离。一个压力测试的Shapley-Shubik示例恢复了分析不可行性下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Walras均衡问题在真实经济中可能不存在均衡解，需要一种能够处理这种不可行性的松弛方法，并量化经济与均衡之间的距离。

Method: 采用Rockafellian松弛方法，用非负变量v松弛市场出清条件，并对v的范数施加惩罚项，使得松弛后的优化问题始终适定。

Result: 当惩罚参数增大时，残差收敛到超额需求可行域内最小范数向量v*∞，该向量衡量了原经济与最近均衡存在经济之间的距离。

Conclusion: 该方法为不存在均衡的经济提供了有意义的近似解和距离度量，并通过Shapley-Shubik示例验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We propose a Rockafellian relaxation of the Walrasian equilibrium problem for an exchange economy that may not admit one. Market clearing is slackened by a non-negative variable $v$ whose norm is penalized; the relaxation is well posed throughout. As the penalty grows, the residual converges to a vector $v^*_\infty$ of minimum norm in the feasible range of excess demand, measuring the distance to the nearest equilibrium-admitting economy. A stressed Shapley--Shubik example recovers the analytical infeasibility floor to machine

</details>


### [169] [Risk Sensitive Filtering for Singular Systems subject to Round-Robin Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04734)
*Ashna Goel,Shovan Bhaumik,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对轮询协议（round-robin protocol）通信约束下的离散时间线性随机奇异系统，提出了一种风险敏感（risk sensitive）卡尔曼滤波框架。通过Weierstrass标准型变换将奇异系统转化为等效增广状态空间模型，得到轮询协议诱导的周期系统（RRIPS）；基于贝叶斯公式和指数二次代价函数最小化推导了递归风险敏感卡尔曼滤波器（RSKF），并引入在线自适应风险参数调整机制增强鲁棒性，同时建立了滤波器稳定性的充分条件。实验表明该方法相比标准KF具有更好的估计性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奇异系统滤波方法未考虑网络通信约束（如轮询协议导致的部分测量传输）和模型不确定性，且风险敏感卡尔曼滤波尚未应用于奇异系统。本文旨在填补这一空白，同时解决风险参数的手动调参困难问题。

Method: 1) 使用Weierstrass标准型将奇异系统解耦为慢子系统和快子系统，获得等效增广状态空间模型；2) 依据轮询协议的周期性测量模式，将系统建模为轮询协议诱导的周期系统（RRIPS）；3) 采用贝叶斯公式和指数二次代价函数最小化推导递归滤波方程；4) 引入自适应风险敏感机制，利用预测协方差信息在线调整风险参数，确保正定性；5) 基于周期系统的能观性和能控性概念建立稳定性充分条件。

Result: 提出的自适应RSKF在轮询协议通信约束下能有效估计奇异系统状态，相比标准KF具有更高的估计精度和更强的鲁棒性；自适应风险参数调整策略保证了预测协方差的正定性和滤波稳定性；当风险参数为零时退化为标准KF，当奇异矩阵化为单位阵时退化为标准RSKF。

Conclusion: 本文成功将风险敏感滤波思想推广至轮询协议通信约束下的奇异系统，提出的自适应RSKF框架兼顾了估计性能、鲁棒性和稳定性，为网络化奇异系统的状态估计提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Abstract: This paper develops a risk sensitive (RS) Kalman filtering framework for discrete-time linear stochastic singular systems operating under communication constraints imposed by a round-robin protocol. Due to limited network bandwidth, only a subset of the available measurements can be transmitted at each sampling instant, resulting in a periodically varying measurement structure. By employing the Weierstrass canonical form (WCF), the singular system is transformed into an equivalent augmented state space model, yielding a round-robin induced periodic system (RRIPS). A recursive risk sensitive Kalman filter (RSKF) is then developed for the RRIPS through a Bayesian formulation and the minimization of an exponential quadratic cost function, from which the recursive filtering equations are obtained for the original singular system. To enhance robustness against modeling uncertainties and disturbances, an adaptive RS mechanism is introduced in which the risk parameter is adjusted online according to the available covariance information. This adaptive strategy guarantees the positive definiteness of the predicted covariance matrix while adjusting the degree of risk sensitivity to the prevailing estimation uncertainty. Furthermore, sufficient conditions ensuring the filter stability are established using the observability and controllability concepts of periodic systems. The proposed framework reduces to the standard KF for singular systems when the RS parameter vanishes and recovers the standard RSKF when the singular matrix reduces to the identity matrix. Finally, numerical results are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and improved estimation performance of the proposed approach in comparison with the standard KF.

</details>


### [170] [Mixed precision Newton's method for optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04828)
*Nicolas Brisebarre,Giuseppe Carrino,Theo Mary,Elisa Riccietti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对牛顿法及其近似变体（拟牛顿法、不精确牛顿法）在混合精度浮点算术下的误差和收敛性进行了理论分析，并提出了混合精度算法，通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶优化方法（如牛顿法）收敛快、精度高，但计算成本高。混合精度算术通过为不同操作分配不同浮点精度来提升效率，但缺乏系统的理论指导，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对牛顿法进行误差分析，考虑近似误差和舍入误差等多重不精确来源；建立混合精度下序列的收敛性分析，给出收敛速率和可达精度界限；基于理论框架设计混合精度算法，并通过数值实验验证。

Result: 建立了涵盖拟牛顿法和不精确牛顿法的收敛性理论框架，给出了混合精度下牛顿法及其变体的收敛速率和精度界限，数值实验验证了理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 混合精度算术能有效提升牛顿法及其变体的计算效率，所提出的理论框架为设计实用混合精度二阶优化算法提供了理论基础和指导。

Abstract: Second-order optimization methods, such as Newton's algorithm, achieve fast local convergence and high accuracy, but their practical use is often limited by high computational costs. To mitigate this issue, variants such as inexact and quasi-Newton methods are widely used. A complementary and promising approach to improve the efficiency of the method is to employ mixed precision arithmetic, using different floating-point precisions for different operations, based on their impact on the convergence and accuracy of the method. In this work, we perform an error analysis of Newton's method accounting for different sources of inexactness, including approximations and rounding errors. We present a convergence analysis for the generated sequence, establishing bounds on the convergence rate and attainable accuracy. This theoretical framework covers quasi-Newton and inexact Newton methods, and is leveraged to propose mixed precision algorithms. We present a wide set of numerical experiments to illustrate our theoretical results and the behavior of Newton's method and its approximate variants in mixed precision floating-point arithmetic.

</details>


### [171] [Exploring polynomial models in the Search Step of Direct Multisearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04902)
*Ana Luísa Custódio,Marta Pozzi,Everton José da Silva*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究Direct Multisearch (DMS)多目标无导数优化算法中不同的模型最小化策略对性能的影响，提出了包括基于Improved Front Steepest Descent算法在内的替代策略，并进行了数值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DMS算法的搜索步骤中，二次多项式模型的构建已有研究，但如何联合最小化这些模型（通常采用min-max标量化）未得到充分关注，因此需要探索不同模型最小化策略对DMS性能的影响。

Method: 本文提出了替代的模型最小化策略，包括基于Improved Front Steepest Descent算法的方法，并在DMS的搜索步骤中对这些策略进行数值评估。

Result: 通过数值实验评估了不同模型最小化策略对DMS算法性能的影响。

Conclusion: 不同模型最小化策略会显著影响DMS算法的数值效率，改进的策略具有潜力提升算法性能。

Abstract: Direct Multisearch (DMS) is a class of direct-search algorithms designed for multiobjective derivative-free optimization. Its framework consists of an optional search step and a poll step, the latter ensuring the corresponding theoretical convergence properties. Recently, a search strategy based on the minimization of quadratic polynomial models, constructed from previously evaluated points, was proposed to improve the numerical efficiency of the method. While the construction of these surrogate models has been investigated, considerably less attention has been devoted to how they should be jointly minimized, with min-max scalarization typically being adopted.
  This work investigates how different model-minimization strategies influence the performance of DMS. To this end, alternative strategies for exploring the quadratic polynomial models within the search step of DMS are proposed and numerically assessed, including one based on the recently proposed Improved Front Steepest Descent algorithm.

</details>


### [172] [Multi Impulse Low Earth Orbit Maneuver Synthesis Through Thrust Measure Primer Vector Conditions and Piecewise Multiple Shooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05057)
*Pedro Kuntz Puglia,Willer Gomes dos Santos,Emilien Flayac*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文应用原始向量理论（primer vector theory）对近地轨道（LEO）中的最优脉冲机动进行求解，并适配了该环境中保守（J2）与非保守（J2+大气阻力）摄动模型，通过脉冲多次打靶优化方案验证了原始向量在降低机动成本方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中缺乏将原始向量理论应用于近地轨道（LEO）环境并适配各类摄动模型的研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先回顾最优控制与轨道机动理论，基于测度扩展推力控制并推广至保守与非保守摄动模型；提出笛卡尔坐标系下的脉冲多次打靶优化方案，采用分段方法求解；在开普勒模型已知解的基础上，引入J2模型（保守摄动）和J2+Drag模型（非保守摄动）进行对比验证。

Result: 在J2和J2+Drag摄动模型下，提出并验证了多种有效的原始向量计算方法，通过相互对比确认了一致性；证明原始向量在降低轨道机动成本方面是有用的工具。

Conclusion: 原始向量理论可成功推广至受摄动的LEO环境，为优化脉冲机动提供了有效手段。

Abstract: This work sets out to apply primer vector theory to optimal impulse maneuvers in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) and adapt it to the types of perturbations encountered in this environment, which is not readily available in the literature. A review of the theory of optimal control and orbital maneuvering is made and in particular, primer vector theory is laid out in detail based on the extension of the thrust control to a measure, including its generalization to conservative and non-conservative perturbation models. An impulsive multiple shooting optimization scheme in Cartesian coordinates is presented, through a piecewise approach to the problem. Then, some maneuver scenarios with known solutions under the Keplerian model are solved under perturbed orbital dynamics with the help of the primer vector, and the resulting trajectories are compared. The perturbation models include a J2 model, representing the class of conservative perturbations by modeling Earth's nonspherical gravity field, and a J2+Drag model, representing the class of non-conservative perturbations by including the effects of atmospheric drag in LEO\@. For each model, some valid methods of primer vector calculation have been tried and validated between each other, and the primer vector is proven to be a useful tool in reducing the cost of orbital maneuvers.

</details>


### [173] [An augmented Lagrangian method with exact multipliers for non-separable composite $\ell_0$-$\ell_2$ regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05073)
*Huan Ren,Guiyun Xiao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对非可分复合$\ell_0$-$\ell_2$正则化模型，提出了两种基于精确乘子的增广拉格朗日算法，分别适用于满行秩和一般矩阵情形，所有子问题通过闭式解全局优化，并在理论上证明了线性收敛性与KKT点收敛性，实验验证了算法在效率和精度上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非可分复合$\ell_0$-$\ell_2$正则化模型的不精确增广拉格朗日方法存在计算复杂度高和收敛不稳定的问题，亟需开发高效且收敛性有保障的求解算法。

Method: 提出两种基于精确乘子的增广拉格朗日算法：针对满行秩变换矩阵情形设计一种算法，针对一般矩阵情形设计另一种算法；所有子问题均通过闭式解实现全局优化。

Result: 在合成数据、趋势滤波和图像平滑任务上，所提方法相比现有方法在效率和精度上均表现更优。

Conclusion: 所提算法在满行秩情形下具有线性收敛性，在一般情形下所有聚点均为原问题的KKT点，理论分析与实验验证一致。

Abstract: This paper studies a non-separable composite $\ell_0$-$\ell_2$ regularization model that simultaneously enforces sparsity and smoothness for inverse problems. The $\ell_0$ norm induces inherent nonconvexity and nonsmoothness, while linear transformations further introduce nonseparability, making the problem computationally challenging to solve. The existing inexact augmented Lagrangian method suffers from high computational complexity and unstable convergence. To overcome these difficulties, we develop two novel augmented Lagrangian algorithms with exact multipliers, designed respectively for the full row-rank case and the general matrix case, where all subproblems are globally optimized via closed-form solutions. Furthermore, we prove linear convergence of the proposed method when the transformation matrix is full row rank. In the general setting, all accumulation points of the generated sequence are KKT points for the original problem. Numerical experiments on synthetic data, trend filtering, and image smoothing demonstrate the superior efficiency and accuracy of the proposed methods over the existing method, confirming our theoretical analysis.

</details>


### [174] [Adaptive direct search algorithms with relaxable and quantifiable constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05183)
*Charles Audet,Théo Denorme,Youssef Diouane,Sébastien Le Digabel,Christophe Tribes*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出ADS-PB，一种扩展的自适应直接搜索框架，用于求解约束黑箱优化问题。该框架不依赖网格结构或目标函数的充分下降条件，采用渐进式障碍机制处理可量化的可放松约束，同时利用约束和目标函数值。在温和假设下进行了收敛性分析，并通过分析型和仿真型约束测试问题集验证了性能，与包括MADS框架内PB方法在内的最先进黑箱优化求解器进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ADS框架仅处理不可放松约束（极端障碍方法），缺乏对可量化和可放松约束的有效处理机制，限制了其在更广泛约束黑箱优化问题中的应用。

Method: 在ADS框架基础上引入Progressive Barrier (PB)机制，同时利用约束违反度和目标函数值来量化迭代点的质量，无需网格结构或目标函数充分下降条件。

Result: 在分析型和仿真型约束测试问题上验证了性能，与MADS-PB等最先进求解器进行了比较。

Conclusion: ADS-PB成功扩展了ADS框架处理可放松约束的能力，在温和假设下具有收敛性保证，且在实际测试中表现具有竞争力。

Abstract: This work introduces ADS-PB, an extension of the Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) framework for solving constrained blackbox optimization problems. With ADS, iterates progress without relying on mesh structures or sufficient decrease conditions on the objective function value. Unlike the extreme barrier approach used in ADS, where only unrelaxable constraints are considered, the proposed method also handles quantifiable and relaxable constraints using a Progressive Barrier (PB) mechanism that exploits both constraint and objective function values. A convergence analysis of the proposed framework under mild assumptions is presented. The performance of the proposed method is assessed using sets of analytical and simulation-based constrained test problems and is compared with state-of-the-art blackbox optimization solvers, including the PB approach within the Mesh Adaptive Direct Search (MADS) framework.

</details>


### [175] [An Analytical Newsvendor Framework for Risk-Averse Energy Storage Capacity Reservation under Non-Normal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05372)
*Nuerxiati Abudurexiti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对储能运营商在不确定市场机会下的容量预留决策问题，提出了一个三目标存储-报童模型框架，同时最大化期望利润、最小化CVaR尾部损失和最小化最大遗憾值，并推导了关键分位数解和有限场景凸标量化方法。实验表明，正态分布假设会导致过度预留容量，而CVaR和最小最大遗憾策略虽牺牲部分期望利润，但能显著降低尾部风险和遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 储能运营商在日前市场等场景中需要在不确定机会实现前预留可用容量，而价格尖峰、可再生能源预测误差、调用通知和不平衡惩罚等因素导致决策相关的机会分布呈现非对称和重尾特征，而非高斯分布。现有方法多假设正态分布，无法有效处理这种分布特性，因此需要更鲁棒的决策框架。

Method: 提出了三目标存储-报童模型框架：(1) 最大化期望利润；(2) 最小化CVaR尾部损失；(3) 最小化候选非正态分布集上的最大遗憾值。推导了关键分位数形式的期望利润解，以及有限场景下的凸标量化方法，可同时求解三个目标。

Result: 在均值方差匹配的对数NMVM实验中，基于正态分布假设的策略会过度预留容量最多达1.337单位。在对数正态策略比较中，CVaR策略和最小最大遗憾策略虽然降低了期望利润，但有效减少了尾部损失和候选集上的遗憾值。

Conclusion: 储能容量预留决策中应考虑分布的非对称和重尾特性，单纯依赖正态分布假设会导致次优决策；三目标框架提供了在期望利润、尾部风险和分布鲁棒性之间的系统化权衡方法。

Abstract: Energy storage operators often reserve usable capacity before uncertain market opportunities are realized, for example when a day-ahead operator commits capacity for an evening peak-spread or ancillary-service window. Price spikes, renewable forecast errors, activation calls and imbalance penalties can make the decision-relevant opportunity distribution asymmetric and heavy-tailed rather than Gaussian. This paper develops a tri-objective storage-newsvendor framework for this capacity-reservation decision. The model jointly maximizes expected profit, minimizes CVaR tail loss and minimizes maximum regret over candidate non-normal distributions. We derive a critical-fractile expected-profit solution and a finite-scenario convex scalarization that solves the three objectives together. In stylized log-NMVM experiments with matched mean and variance, a moment-matched normal policy over-reserves capacity by up to 1.337 units. In a lognormal policy comparison, the CVaR and maximum-regret policies reduce tail loss and candidate-set regret at the cost of lower expected profit.

</details>
